Test Case ID: 1

PREFIX CODE:

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
#include <sstream>
using namespace std;

struct ClaimResult {
    string status;
    double patient;
    double plan;
    double deductibleApplied;
    double coinsuranceApplied;
};

struct Adjudicator {
    double copay = 30.0;
    double annualDeductible = 500.0;
    double oopMax = 1000.0;
    double coinsuranceRate = 0.20;
    double ytdDeductible = 0.0;
    double ytdOop = 0.0;
    map<string, double> allowedRates;
    set<string> requiresAuth;
    set<string> authorizedClaims;
    set<string> processedClaims;
    vector<string> claimLedger;

    Adjudicator() {
        allowedRates["XRAY"] = 0.50;
        allowedRates["THERAPY"] = 0.80;
        allowedRates["SURGERY"] = 0.60;
        requiresAuth.insert("SURGERY");
    }

    void reset() {
        ytdDeductible = 0; ytdOop = 0;
        authorizedClaims.clear(); processedClaims.clear(); claimLedger.clear();
    }

    static double roundCents(double v) {
        return round(v * 100.0) / 100.0;
    }

    // Adjudicate one medical claim line.
    // Rules:
    //  1. claimId is idempotent: if already processed, return empty result with status "DUPLICATE" before any mutation.
    //  2. Unknown codes or missing auth for auth-required codes → status "DENIED", log "DENIED:<claimId>:<code>", no accumulator changes.
    //  3. allowed = billed * allowedRates[code]. remaining = max(0, allowed - primaryPaid).
    //  4. Preventive lines: patient=0, plan=remaining, no deductible/OOP mutation.
    //  5. Non-preventive waterfall: copay first (capped at remaining), then deductible on remainder
    //     (capped at annualDeductible - ytdDeductible), then coinsurance on the rest.
    //  6. patient = copayUsed + deductibleUsed + coinsuranceUsed, but clamped to (oopMax - ytdOop).
    //     If clamped, the excess shifts to plan.
    //  7. plan = remaining - patient (never negative).
    //  8. Update ytdDeductible += deductibleUsed, ytdOop += patient.
    //  9. Log "PAID:<claimId>:patient=<patient>:plan=<plan>".
    // Return ClaimResult with all money fields rounded to cents.
    ClaimResult adjudicateLine(const string& claimId, const string& code,
                               double billed, bool preventive, double primaryPaid) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
        ClaimResult res;
        if (processedClaims.count(claimId)) { res.status = "DUPLICATE"; res.patient = res.plan = res.deductibleApplied = res.coinsuranceApplied = 0; return res; }
        auto it = allowedRates.find(code);
        if (it == allowedRates.end() || (requiresAuth.count(code) && !authorizedClaims.count(claimId))) {
            processedClaims.insert(claimId);
            claimLedger.push_back("DENIED:" + claimId + ":" + code);
            res.status = "DENIED"; res.patient = res.plan = res.deductibleApplied = res.coinsuranceApplied = 0; return res;
        }
        processedClaims.insert(claimId);
        double allowed = roundCents(billed * it->second);
        double remaining = roundCents(allowed - primaryPaid); if (remaining < 0) remaining = 0;
        double pat = 0, ded = 0, coins = 0;
        if (!preventive && remaining > 0) {
            double copayUsed = min(copay, remaining); remaining -= copayUsed;
            double dedAvail = annualDeductible - ytdDeductible; if (dedAvail < 0) dedAvail = 0;
            ded = roundCents(min(remaining, dedAvail)); remaining -= ded;
            coins = roundCents(remaining * coinsuranceRate);
            pat = roundCents(copayUsed + ded + coins);
            double capLeft = oopMax - ytdOop; if (capLeft < 0) capLeft = 0;
            if (pat > capLeft) pat = roundCents(capLeft);
            ytdDeductible = roundCents(ytdDeductible + ded);
            ytdOop = roundCents(ytdOop + pat);
        }
        double planPay = roundCents(roundCents(allowed - primaryPaid) - pat); if (planPay < 0) planPay = 0;
        claimLedger.push_back("PAID:" + claimId + ":patient=" + to_string(pat) + ":plan=" + to_string(planPay));
        res.status = "PAID"; res.patient = pat; res.plan = planPay; res.deductibleApplied = ded; res.coinsuranceApplied = coins;
        return res;


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
    }
};

int main() {
    Adjudicator adj;
    adj.reset();
    ClaimResult r1 = adj.adjudicateLine("C1", "THERAPY", 400.0, false, 20.0);
    // allowed=320, remaining=300, copay=30, ded=270, coins=0, patient=300
    assert(r1.status == "PAID");
    assert(abs(r1.patient - 300.0) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(r1.deductibleApplied - 270.0) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(adj.ytdDeductible - 270.0) < 0.01);

    ClaimResult r2 = adj.adjudicateLine("C2", "XRAY", 100.0, true, 0.0);
    assert(abs(r2.patient - 0.0) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(r2.plan - 50.0) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(adj.ytdOop - 300.0) < 0.01);  // preventive doesn't add OOP
    int visibleOk = 1;
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------
    assert(visibleOk == 1);
    // Duplicate: must return DUPLICATE, no mutation
    int ledgerBefore = (int)adj.claimLedger.size();
    double oopBefore = adj.ytdOop;
    ClaimResult dup = adj.adjudicateLine("C1", "THERAPY", 999.0, false, 0.0);
    assert(dup.status == "DUPLICATE");
    assert((int)adj.claimLedger.size() == ledgerBefore);
    assert(abs(adj.ytdOop - oopBefore) < 0.01);

    // Denied: SURGERY without auth
    double dedBefore = adj.ytdDeductible;
    ClaimResult denied = adj.adjudicateLine("S1", "SURGERY", 1000.0, false, 0.0);
    assert(denied.status == "DENIED");
    assert(abs(denied.patient) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(adj.ytdDeductible - dedBefore) < 0.01);

    // OOP cap test
    adj.authorizedClaims.insert("S2");
    adj.ytdDeductible = 500.0;
    adj.ytdOop = 950.0;
    ClaimResult capped = adj.adjudicateLine("S2", "SURGERY", 1000.0, false, 0.0);
    // allowed=600, copay=30, ded=0 (full), coins=570*0.2=114, pat=144 but cap leaves 50
    assert(abs(capped.patient - 50.0) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(capped.plan - 550.0) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(adj.ytdOop - 1000.0) < 0.01);

    // Primary pays all
    ClaimResult fullPrimary = adj.adjudicateLine("X3", "XRAY", 100.0, false, 100.0);
    assert(abs(fullPrimary.patient) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(fullPrimary.plan) < 0.01);

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <set>
#include <cassert>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
using namespace std;

struct Lot {
    string lotId;
    int qty;
    int expiryDay;
    bool quarantined;
};

struct AllocResult {
    string status;            // "ALLOCATED", "PARTIAL", "DUPLICATE", "INSUFFICIENT", "EXPIRED_ALL"
    int totalAllocated;
    vector<pair<string,int>> allocations;  // lotId -> qty allocated
};

struct Warehouse {
    vector<Lot> lots;
    set<string> processedTickets;
    vector<string> auditLog;
    int minShelfLifeDays = 30;

    void reset() {
        lots.clear(); processedTickets.clear(); auditLog.clear();
    }

    void addLot(const string& id, int qty, int expiry, bool quarantined) {
        lots.push_back({id, qty, expiry, quarantined});
    }

    // Allocate inventory using First-Expiry-First-Out (FEFO).
    // Rules:
    //  1. ticketId is idempotent: duplicates return DUPLICATE with no mutation.
    //  2. Filter out quarantined lots and lots with (expiryDay - currentDay) < minShelfLifeDays.
    //  3. Sort eligible lots by expiryDay ascending (FEFO).
    //  4. If no eligible lots exist, return EXPIRED_ALL.
    //  5. Allocate from earliest-expiry lots first, reducing lot qty.
    //  6. If total eligible qty < requested: return INSUFFICIENT with NO lot mutation (atomic failure).
    //  7. If allocated == requested: status ALLOCATED.
    //  8. Log "ALLOC:<ticketId>:qty=<totalAllocated>" for success,
    //     "DENY:<ticketId>:<reason>" for failures.
    AllocResult allocate(const string& ticketId, int requested, int currentDay) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
        AllocResult res;
        if (processedTickets.count(ticketId)) { res.status = "DUPLICATE"; res.totalAllocated = 0; return res; }
        // Filter eligible lots
        struct EligibleLot { int idx; int expiryDay; int qty; };
        vector<EligibleLot> eligible;
        for (int i = 0; i < (int)lots.size(); i++) {
            if (!lots[i].quarantined && (lots[i].expiryDay - currentDay) >= minShelfLifeDays && lots[i].qty > 0)
                eligible.push_back({i, lots[i].expiryDay, lots[i].qty});
        }
        if (eligible.empty()) { processedTickets.insert(ticketId); auditLog.push_back("DENY:" + ticketId + ":EXPIRED_ALL"); res.status = "EXPIRED_ALL"; res.totalAllocated = 0; return res; }
        sort(eligible.begin(), eligible.end(), [](const EligibleLot& a, const EligibleLot& b){ return a.expiryDay < b.expiryDay; });
        int totalAvail = 0; for (auto& e : eligible) totalAvail += e.qty;
        if (totalAvail < requested) { processedTickets.insert(ticketId); auditLog.push_back("DENY:" + ticketId + ":INSUFFICIENT"); res.status = "INSUFFICIENT"; res.totalAllocated = 0; return res; }
        processedTickets.insert(ticketId);
        int remaining = requested; res.totalAllocated = 0;
        for (auto& e : eligible) {
            if (remaining <= 0) break;
            int take = min(remaining, e.qty);
            lots[e.idx].qty -= take; remaining -= take; res.totalAllocated += take;
            res.allocations.push_back({lots[e.idx].lotId, take});
        }
        res.status = "ALLOCATED";
        auditLog.push_back("ALLOC:" + ticketId + ":qty=" + to_string(res.totalAllocated));
        return res;


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
    }
};

int main() {
    Warehouse wh;
    wh.reset();
    wh.addLot("L1", 20, 100, false);
    wh.addLot("L2", 30, 80, false);
    wh.addLot("L3", 10, 120, true);   // quarantined

    AllocResult r1 = wh.allocate("T1", 25, 10);
    assert(r1.status == "ALLOCATED");
    assert(r1.totalAllocated == 25);
    // FEFO: L2 (exp80) first, then L1 (exp100)
    assert(r1.allocations[0].first == "L2" && r1.allocations[0].second == 25);

    // L2 now has 5 left, L1 has 20
    assert(wh.lots[1].qty == 5);
    int visibleOk = 1;
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------
    assert(visibleOk == 1);
    // Duplicate check
    int logBefore = (int)wh.auditLog.size();
    AllocResult dup = wh.allocate("T1", 99, 10);
    assert(dup.status == "DUPLICATE");
    assert((int)wh.auditLog.size() == logBefore);

    // Insufficient: atomic failure (no lot mutation)
    int l2Before = wh.lots[1].qty;
    int l0Before = wh.lots[0].qty;
    AllocResult insuf = wh.allocate("T2", 100, 10);
    assert(insuf.status == "INSUFFICIENT");
    assert(wh.lots[1].qty == l2Before);
    assert(wh.lots[0].qty == l0Before);

    // Shelf-life filter: only L1 eligible if currentDay=75 (L2 exp80, 80-75=5 < 30)
    AllocResult r3 = wh.allocate("T3", 10, 75);
    assert(r3.status == "ALLOCATED");
    assert(r3.allocations[0].first == "L1");

    // All expired
    Warehouse wh2;
    wh2.reset();
    wh2.addLot("X1", 50, 20, false);
    AllocResult expired = wh2.allocate("T4", 10, 10);
    // expiryDay=20, currentDay=10, 20-10=10 < 30 → EXPIRED_ALL
    assert(expired.status == "EXPIRED_ALL");
    assert(wh2.lots[0].qty == 50);  // no mutation

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
using namespace std;

struct PaymentResult {
    string status;         // "APPLIED", "DUPLICATE", "RECOVERY", "PAID_OFF"
    double toFees;
    double toInterest;
    double toPrincipal;
    double toEscrow;
    double toSuspense;
};

struct LoanAccount {
    double principalBalance = 0;
    double interestDue = 0;
    double escrowDue = 0;
    double feesOutstanding = 0;
    double suspenseBalance = 0;
    double lateFeeAmount = 75.0;
    bool lateFeePending = false;
    bool lateFeeApplied = false;
    string status = "CURRENT";  // CURRENT, DELINQUENT, PAID_OFF, CHARGED_OFF
    set<string> processedPayments;
    vector<string> paymentLedger;

    void reset(double principal, double interest, double escrow) {
        principalBalance = principal; interestDue = interest; escrowDue = escrow;
        feesOutstanding = 0; suspenseBalance = 0; lateFeePending = false;
        lateFeeApplied = false; status = "CURRENT";
        processedPayments.clear(); paymentLedger.clear();
    }

    static double r2(double v) { return round(v * 100.0) / 100.0; }

    // Apply a payment to the loan.
    // Rules:
    //  1. paymentId is idempotent: duplicates return DUPLICATE with no mutation.
    //  2. If status is CHARGED_OFF, all money goes to recovery suspense, status stays CHARGED_OFF,
    //     return status "RECOVERY".
    //  3. If lateFeePending and !lateFeeApplied, add lateFeeAmount to feesOutstanding
    //     and set lateFeeApplied=true (one-time fee injection).
    //  4. Waterfall: fees first, then interest, then escrow, then principal.
    //     Each bucket takes min(remaining, bucketDue), reducing both.
    //  5. Any excess after principal goes to suspenseBalance.
    //  6. If principalBalance reaches 0 after payment, status becomes "PAID_OFF",
    //     return status "PAID_OFF".
    //  7. Log "PAY:<paymentId>:fees=<f>:int=<i>:prin=<p>:esc=<e>:susp=<s>".
    //  8. All amounts rounded to cents.
    PaymentResult applyPayment(const string& paymentId, double amount) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
        PaymentResult res;
        if (processedPayments.count(paymentId)) { res = {"DUPLICATE",0,0,0,0,0}; return res; }
        processedPayments.insert(paymentId);
        if (status == "CHARGED_OFF") {
            suspenseBalance = r2(suspenseBalance + amount);
            paymentLedger.push_back("PAY:" + paymentId + ":RECOVERY:" + to_string(amount));
            res = {"RECOVERY", 0, 0, 0, 0, amount}; return res;
        }
        if (lateFeePending && !lateFeeApplied) {
            feesOutstanding = r2(feesOutstanding + lateFeeAmount);
            lateFeeApplied = true;
        }
        double rem = amount;
        double toFees = r2(min(rem, feesOutstanding)); feesOutstanding = r2(feesOutstanding - toFees); rem = r2(rem - toFees);
        double toInt = r2(min(rem, interestDue)); interestDue = r2(interestDue - toInt); rem = r2(rem - toInt);
        double toEsc = r2(min(rem, escrowDue)); escrowDue = r2(escrowDue - toEsc); rem = r2(rem - toEsc);
        double toPrin = r2(min(rem, principalBalance)); principalBalance = r2(principalBalance - toPrin); rem = r2(rem - toPrin);
        double toSusp = r2(rem);
        suspenseBalance = r2(suspenseBalance + toSusp);
        string st = "APPLIED";
        if (principalBalance <= 0.001) { status = "PAID_OFF"; st = "PAID_OFF"; }
        paymentLedger.push_back("PAY:" + paymentId + ":fees=" + to_string(toFees) + ":int=" + to_string(toInt) + ":prin=" + to_string(toPrin) + ":esc=" + to_string(toEsc) + ":susp=" + to_string(toSusp));
        res = {st, toFees, toInt, toPrin, toEsc, toSusp}; return res;


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
    }
};

int main() {
    LoanAccount loan;
    loan.reset(10000.0, 200.0, 100.0);
    loan.lateFeePending = true;

    PaymentResult r1 = loan.applyPayment("P1", 500.0);
    // Late fee injected: fees=75, waterfall: 75 fees + 200 interest + 100 escrow + 125 principal
    assert(r1.status == "APPLIED");
    assert(abs(r1.toFees - 75.0) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(r1.toInterest - 200.0) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(r1.toEscrow - 100.0) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(r1.toPrincipal - 125.0) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(loan.principalBalance - 9875.0) < 0.01);
    int visibleOk = 1;
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------
    assert(visibleOk == 1);
    // Duplicate
    int ledgerBefore = (int)loan.paymentLedger.size();
    PaymentResult dup = loan.applyPayment("P1", 9999.0);
    assert(dup.status == "DUPLICATE");
    assert((int)loan.paymentLedger.size() == ledgerBefore);

    // Late fee already applied — should NOT add again
    loan.lateFeePending = true;  // still pending but applied
    double feesBefore = loan.feesOutstanding;
    PaymentResult r2 = loan.applyPayment("P2", 50.0);
    // No new late fee. fees=0, int=0 (already paid), esc=0 (already paid), 50 to principal
    assert(abs(r2.toPrincipal - 50.0) < 0.01);

    // Paid-off transition
    loan.interestDue = 0; loan.escrowDue = 0; loan.feesOutstanding = 0;
    double prinLeft = loan.principalBalance;
    PaymentResult r3 = loan.applyPayment("P3", prinLeft + 200.0);
    assert(r3.status == "PAID_OFF");
    assert(abs(r3.toSuspense - 200.0) < 0.01);
    assert(loan.status == "PAID_OFF");

    // Charged-off recovery
    LoanAccount loan2;
    loan2.reset(5000.0, 100.0, 50.0);
    loan2.status = "CHARGED_OFF";
    PaymentResult rec = loan2.applyPayment("R1", 300.0);
    assert(rec.status == "RECOVERY");
    assert(abs(rec.toSuspense - 300.0) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(loan2.principalBalance - 5000.0) < 0.01);  // no principal reduction
    assert(abs(loan2.interestDue - 100.0) < 0.01);  // no interest reduction

Test Case #4

================================================================================
Test Case ID: 4
Source: devbench-code-purpose-understanding
Language: cpp
================================================================================

PREFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>
using namespace std;

struct PayStub {
    string status;
    double grossPay;
    double retirement;
    double tax;
    double healthDeduction;
    double garnishment;
    double netPay;
};

struct PayrollEngine {
    double annualRetirementCap = 23000.0;
    double retirementRate = 0.06;
    double healthMonthly = 250.0;
    double garnishmentRate = 0.15;
    double garnishmentMinNet = 1200.0;
    // Marginal tax brackets: [(upperLimit, rate), ...]  — upperLimit=0 means no cap
    vector<pair<double,double>> taxBrackets = {{1000, 0.10}, {3000, 0.22}, {0, 0.32}};

    double ytdRetirement = 0;
    double ytdGross = 0;
    double ytdTax = 0;
    set<string> processedRuns;
    vector<string> payLog;

    void reset() {
        ytdRetirement = 0; ytdGross = 0; ytdTax = 0;
        processedRuns.clear(); payLog.clear();
    }

    static double r2(double v) { return round(v * 100.0) / 100.0; }

    // Compute one pay period.
    // Rules:
    //  1. runId is idempotent: duplicates return status "DUPLICATE" with no mutation.
    //  2. Retirement contribution = gross * retirementRate, but capped so ytdRetirement
    //     never exceeds annualRetirementCap.
    //  3. Taxable income = gross - retirement contribution.
    //  4. Tax = marginal brackets applied to taxable income:
    //       bracket[i] covers (prevLimit, bracket[i].upperLimit], rate = bracket[i].rate.
    //       upperLimit=0 means unlimited.
    //  5. Health deduction is flat healthMonthly.
    //  6. Pre-garnishment net = gross - retirement - tax - health.
    //  7. Garnishment = pre-garnishment net * garnishmentRate, BUT if applying garnishment
    //     would drop net below garnishmentMinNet, garnishment = max(0, preNet - garnishmentMinNet).
    //  8. Final netPay = preNet - garnishment.
    //  9. Update ytdRetirement, ytdGross, ytdTax. Log "PAY:<runId>:net=<netPay>".
    //  10. All amounts rounded to cents.
    PayStub computePay(const string& runId, double gross) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
        PayStub ps;
        if (processedRuns.count(runId)) { ps.status = "DUPLICATE"; ps.grossPay = ps.retirement = ps.tax = ps.healthDeduction = ps.garnishment = ps.netPay = 0; return ps; }
        processedRuns.insert(runId);
        double retCap = r2(annualRetirementCap - ytdRetirement);
        if (retCap < 0) retCap = 0;
        double ret = r2(min(r2(gross * retirementRate), retCap));
        double taxable = r2(gross - ret);
        double tax = 0; double prevLimit = 0;
        for (auto& [upper, rate] : taxBrackets) {
            double bracketTop = (upper == 0) ? taxable : min((double)upper, taxable);
            double inBracket = bracketTop - prevLimit;
            if (inBracket > 0) tax += r2(inBracket * rate);
            prevLimit = bracketTop;
            if (upper == 0 || taxable <= upper) break;
        }
        tax = r2(tax);
        double health = healthMonthly;
        double preNet = r2(gross - ret - tax - health);
        double garn = r2(preNet * garnishmentRate);
        if (preNet - garn < garnishmentMinNet) garn = r2(max(0.0, preNet - garnishmentMinNet));
        double netPay = r2(preNet - garn);
        ytdRetirement = r2(ytdRetirement + ret);
        ytdGross = r2(ytdGross + gross);
        ytdTax = r2(ytdTax + tax);
        payLog.push_back("PAY:" + runId + ":net=" + to_string(netPay));
        ps = {"OK", gross, ret, tax, health, garn, netPay};
        return ps;


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
    }
};

int main() {
    PayrollEngine pe;
    pe.reset();

    PayStub s1 = pe.computePay("R1", 5000.0);
    // retirement: 5000*0.06=300
    // taxable: 4700
    // tax: 1000*0.10 + 2000*0.22 + 1700*0.32 = 100+440+544 = 1084
    // health: 250
    // preNet: 5000-300-1084-250 = 3366
    // garn: 3366*0.15 = 504.90
    // net: 3366-504.90 = 2861.10
    assert(s1.status == "OK");
    assert(abs(s1.retirement - 300.0) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(s1.tax - 1084.0) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(s1.netPay - 2861.10) < 0.01);
    int visibleOk = 1;
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------
    assert(visibleOk == 1);
    // Duplicate
    PayStub dup = pe.computePay("R1", 9999.0);
    assert(dup.status == "DUPLICATE");
    assert(abs(pe.ytdGross - 5000.0) < 0.01);

    // Retirement cap: set ytd near cap
    pe.ytdRetirement = 22800.0;
    PayStub s2 = pe.computePay("R2", 5000.0);
    // cap: 23000 - 22800 = 200 (not 300)
    assert(abs(s2.retirement - 200.0) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(pe.ytdRetirement - 23000.0) < 0.01);

    // Garnishment min-net clamp
    PayrollEngine pe2;
    pe2.reset();
    pe2.garnishmentMinNet = 3000.0;
    PayStub s3 = pe2.computePay("R3", 5000.0);
    // preNet = 5000-300-1084-250 = 3366
    // garn = 3366*0.15 = 504.90 would make net 2861.10 < 3000
    // so garn = max(0, 3366 - 3000) = 366
    assert(abs(s3.garnishment - 366.0) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(s3.netPay - 3000.0) < 0.01);

    // Low income: no garnishment at all (preNet < minNet)
    PayrollEngine pe3;
    pe3.reset();
    pe3.garnishmentMinNet = 5000.0;
    PayStub s4 = pe3.computePay("R4", 2000.0);
    // retirement: 120, taxable: 1880, tax: 1000*0.10+880*0.22=100+193.6=293.6
    // preNet: 2000-120-293.6-250=1336.4 < 5000 → garn=0
    assert(abs(s4.garnishment) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(s4.netPay - 1336.40) < 0.01);

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cassert>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
using namespace std;

struct FulfillResult {
    string status;  // "FULFILLED", "PARTIAL", "DUPLICATE", "CANCELLED", "NO_STOCK"
    int shipped;
    int backordered;
    string label;   // shipping label or ""
};

struct FulfillmentCenter {
    map<string,int> inventory;    // sku -> qty
    set<string> processedOrders;
    set<string> cancelledOrders;
    vector<string> shipLog;
    vector<string> backorderQueue;
    int labelSeq = 1000;

    void reset() {
        inventory.clear(); processedOrders.clear(); cancelledOrders.clear();
        shipLog.clear(); backorderQueue.clear(); labelSeq = 1000;
    }

    string nextLabel() { return "SHP-" + to_string(++labelSeq); }

    // Fulfill an order line.
    // Rules:
    //  1. orderId is idempotent: duplicates return DUPLICATE, no mutation.
    //  2. If orderId is in cancelledOrders, return CANCELLED, no mutation.
    //  3. If SKU not in inventory or inventory[sku] == 0, return NO_STOCK.
    //     Log "NOSTOCK:<orderId>:<sku>". Add to backorderQueue as "<orderId>:<sku>:<qty>".
    //  4. If inventory[sku] >= qty: full fulfillment. Deduct inventory, generate label,
    //     log "SHIP:<orderId>:<sku>:<qty>:<label>", status FULFILLED.
    //  5. If 0 < inventory[sku] < qty: partial ship. Ship what's available,
    //     backorder the rest. Generate label for shipped portion.
    //     Log "SHIP:<orderId>:<sku>:<shipped>:<label>" and
    //     add "<orderId>:<sku>:<remaining>" to backorderQueue. Status PARTIAL.
    //  6. Mark orderId as processed.
    FulfillResult fulfill(const string& orderId, const string& sku, int qty) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
        FulfillResult res;
        if (processedOrders.count(orderId)) { res = {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, ""}; return res; }
        if (cancelledOrders.count(orderId)) { res = {"CANCELLED", 0, 0, ""}; return res; }
        processedOrders.insert(orderId);
        auto it = inventory.find(sku);
        int avail = (it != inventory.end()) ? it->second : 0;
        if (avail <= 0) {
            shipLog.push_back("NOSTOCK:" + orderId + ":" + sku);
            backorderQueue.push_back(orderId + ":" + sku + ":" + to_string(qty));
            res = {"NO_STOCK", 0, qty, ""}; return res;
        }
        if (avail >= qty) {
            inventory[sku] -= qty;
            string lbl = nextLabel();
            shipLog.push_back("SHIP:" + orderId + ":" + sku + ":" + to_string(qty) + ":" + lbl);
            res = {"FULFILLED", qty, 0, lbl}; return res;
        }
        // Partial
        int shipped = avail;
        int remaining = qty - shipped;
        inventory[sku] = 0;
        string lbl = nextLabel();
        shipLog.push_back("SHIP:" + orderId + ":" + sku + ":" + to_string(shipped) + ":" + lbl);
        backorderQueue.push_back(orderId + ":" + sku + ":" + to_string(remaining));
        res = {"PARTIAL", shipped, remaining, lbl}; return res;


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
    }
};

int main() {
    FulfillmentCenter fc;
    fc.reset();
    fc.inventory["WIDGET"] = 15;
    fc.inventory["GADGET"] = 5;

    FulfillResult r1 = fc.fulfill("O1", "WIDGET", 10);
    assert(r1.status == "FULFILLED");
    assert(r1.shipped == 10);
    assert(r1.label == "SHP-1001");
    assert(fc.inventory["WIDGET"] == 5);

    FulfillResult r2 = fc.fulfill("O2", "GADGET", 8);
    assert(r2.status == "PARTIAL");
    assert(r2.shipped == 5 && r2.backordered == 3);
    assert(fc.inventory["GADGET"] == 0);
    int visibleOk = 1;
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------
    assert(visibleOk == 1);
    // Duplicate
    FulfillResult dup = fc.fulfill("O1", "WIDGET", 99);
    assert(dup.status == "DUPLICATE");
    assert(fc.inventory["WIDGET"] == 5);  // no deduction
    assert(dup.label == "");

    // Cancelled order
    fc.cancelledOrders.insert("O3");
    FulfillResult canc = fc.fulfill("O3", "WIDGET", 2);
    assert(canc.status == "CANCELLED");
    assert(fc.inventory["WIDGET"] == 5);  // no deduction

    // No stock
    FulfillResult ns = fc.fulfill("O4", "MISSING_SKU", 1);
    assert(ns.status == "NO_STOCK");
    assert(ns.backordered == 1);
    assert(fc.backorderQueue.back() == "O4:MISSING_SKU:1");

    // Label sequence continues
    FulfillResult r3 = fc.fulfill("O5", "WIDGET", 3);
    assert(r3.status == "FULFILLED");
    assert(r3.label == "SHP-1003");  // 1001 + 1002 already used
    assert(fc.inventory["WIDGET"] == 2);

    // Backorder queue has correct entries
    bool found = false;
    for (auto& bq : fc.backorderQueue) {
        if (bq == "O2:GADGET:3") found = true;
    }
    assert(found);

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
using namespace std;

struct PremiumResult {
    string status;   // "QUOTED", "DUPLICATE", "INVALID_DATE", "DECLINED"
    double annualPremium;
    double monthlyPremium;
    double discountApplied;
};

struct PremiumCalculator {
    double baseRate = 1200.0;
    double minPremium = 300.0;
    // age factor: <25 → 1.8, 25-45 → 1.0, 46-65 → 1.3, >65 → 2.0
    map<string, double> riskFactors;  // condition -> additive surcharge
    // discount tiers: yearsWithoutClaim → multiplier
    //   0-2 years: 1.0, 3-5 years: 0.90, 6+ years: 0.80
    set<string> processedQuotes;
    vector<string> quoteLog;

    PremiumCalculator() {
        riskFactors["SMOKER"] = 400.0;
        riskFactors["DIABETES"] = 300.0;
        riskFactors["HYPERTENSION"] = 200.0;
    }

    void reset() { processedQuotes.clear(); quoteLog.clear(); }
    static double r2(double v) { return round(v * 100.0) / 100.0; }

    double ageFactor(int age) {
        if (age < 25) return 1.8;
        if (age <= 45) return 1.0;
        if (age <= 65) return 1.3;
        return 2.0;
    }

    double discountMultiplier(int yearsNoClaim) {
        if (yearsNoClaim >= 6) return 0.80;
        if (yearsNoClaim >= 3) return 0.90;
        return 1.0;
    }

    // Calculate insurance premium.
    // Rules:
    //  1. quoteId is idempotent: duplicates return DUPLICATE, no mutation.
    //  2. If age < 18 or age > 85, return DECLINED.
    //  3. If effectiveMonth < 1 or > 12, return INVALID_DATE.
    //  4. adjusted = baseRate * ageFactor(age).
    //  5. Add risk surcharges (additive) for each matching condition.
    //  6. Apply discount multiplier based on yearsWithoutClaim.
    //  7. If result < minPremium, clamp to minPremium.
    //  8. monthlyPremium = annualPremium / 12 (rounded).
    //  9. discountApplied = (pre-discount - post-discount) amount.
    //  10. Log "QUOTE:<quoteId>:annual=<premium>".
    PremiumResult calculatePremium(const string& quoteId, int age,
                                    const vector<string>& conditions,
                                    int yearsWithoutClaim, int effectiveMonth) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
        PremiumResult res;
        if (processedQuotes.count(quoteId)) { res = {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, 0}; return res; }
        if (age < 18 || age > 85) { processedQuotes.insert(quoteId); quoteLog.push_back("DECLINED:" + quoteId); res = {"DECLINED", 0, 0, 0}; return res; }
        if (effectiveMonth < 1 || effectiveMonth > 12) { processedQuotes.insert(quoteId); quoteLog.push_back("INVALID:" + quoteId); res = {"INVALID_DATE", 0, 0, 0}; return res; }
        processedQuotes.insert(quoteId);
        double adjusted = r2(baseRate * ageFactor(age));
        for (auto& cond : conditions) {
            auto it = riskFactors.find(cond);
            if (it != riskFactors.end()) adjusted = r2(adjusted + it->second);
        }
        double preDiscount = adjusted;
        adjusted = r2(adjusted * discountMultiplier(yearsWithoutClaim));
        double disc = r2(preDiscount - adjusted);
        if (adjusted < minPremium) adjusted = minPremium;
        double monthly = r2(adjusted / 12.0);
        quoteLog.push_back("QUOTE:" + quoteId + ":annual=" + to_string(adjusted));
        res = {"QUOTED", adjusted, monthly, disc};
        return res;


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
    }
};

int main() {
    PremiumCalculator pc;
    pc.reset();

    PremiumResult r1 = pc.calculatePremium("Q1", 30, {"SMOKER"}, 4, 6);
    // base=1200, age factor=1.0, adjusted=1200+400=1600, discount=0.90, final=1440
    assert(r1.status == "QUOTED");
    assert(abs(r1.annualPremium - 1440.0) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(r1.monthlyPremium - 120.0) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(r1.discountApplied - 160.0) < 0.01);

    PremiumResult r2 = pc.calculatePremium("Q2", 70, {}, 7, 1);
    // base=1200, age=2.0 → 2400, no risk, discount=0.80 → 1920
    assert(abs(r2.annualPremium - 1920.0) < 0.01);
    int visibleOk = 1;
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------
    assert(visibleOk == 1);
    // Duplicate
    PremiumResult dup = pc.calculatePremium("Q1", 99, {}, 0, 1);
    assert(dup.status == "DUPLICATE");

    // Declined: too young
    PremiumResult young = pc.calculatePremium("Q3", 16, {}, 0, 1);
    assert(young.status == "DECLINED");

    // Declined: too old
    PremiumResult old1 = pc.calculatePremium("Q4", 90, {}, 0, 1);
    assert(old1.status == "DECLINED");

    // Invalid date
    PremiumResult bad = pc.calculatePremium("Q5", 30, {}, 0, 13);
    assert(bad.status == "INVALID_DATE");

    // Min premium floor
    PremiumCalculator pc2;
    pc2.reset();
    pc2.baseRate = 100.0;
    pc2.minPremium = 300.0;
    PremiumResult floor1 = pc2.calculatePremium("Q6", 30, {}, 10, 6);
    // base=100*1.0=100, discount=0.80 → 80 < 300, clamp to 300
    assert(abs(floor1.annualPremium - 300.0) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(floor1.monthlyPremium - 25.0) < 0.01);

    // Multiple risk factors
    PremiumResult multi = pc.calculatePremium("Q7", 20, {"SMOKER", "DIABETES", "HYPERTENSION"}, 0, 3);
    // base=1200*1.8=2160, +400+300+200=3060, discount=1.0, final=3060
    assert(abs(multi.annualPremium - 3060.0) < 0.01);

PREFIX CODE:

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
using namespace std;

struct OrderItem {
    string itemName;
    double basePrice;
    vector<pair<string,double>> modifiers;  // name, price
    double lineTotal;
};

struct BillResult {
    string status;       // "BILLED", "DUPLICATE", "EMPTY_ORDER", "UNAVAILABLE"
    double subtotal;
    double tax;
    double tip;
    double grandTotal;
    double perPerson;    // split amount
    string unavailableItem;
};

struct Restaurant {
    map<string, double> menu;          // item -> price
    map<string, double> modifierPrices; // modifier -> price
    set<string> unavailableItems;
    double taxRate = 0.085;
    set<string> processedOrders;
    vector<string> orderLog;

    Restaurant() {
        menu["BURGER"] = 12.50; menu["SALAD"] = 9.00; menu["STEAK"] = 28.00;
        menu["PASTA"] = 14.00; menu["SOUP"] = 7.50;
        modifierPrices["CHEESE"] = 1.50; modifierPrices["BACON"] = 2.00;
        modifierPrices["AVOCADO"] = 3.00; modifierPrices["EXTRA_SAUCE"] = 0.75;
    }

    void reset() { unavailableItems.clear(); processedOrders.clear(); orderLog.clear(); }
    static double r2(double v) { return round(v * 100.0) / 100.0; }

    // Process a restaurant order and compute the bill.
    // Rules:
    //  1. orderId is idempotent: duplicates return DUPLICATE, no mutation.
    //  2. If items list is empty, return EMPTY_ORDER.
    //  3. For each item: if itemName is in unavailableItems, return UNAVAILABLE
    //     with that item name, no mutation, before processing any other items.
    //  4. For each item: lineTotal = menu price + sum of modifier prices.
    //     Unknown modifiers are ignored (price = 0).
    //  5. subtotal = sum of all lineTotals.
    //  6. tax = subtotal * taxRate (rounded).
    //  7. tip = subtotal * tipPercent (tipPercent is 0.0 to 1.0, rounded).
    //  8. grandTotal = subtotal + tax + tip.
    //  9. perPerson = grandTotal / splitCount (rounded), splitCount >= 1.
    //  10. Log "ORDER:<orderId>:total=<grandTotal>:split=<splitCount>".
    BillResult processOrder(const string& orderId,
                             const vector<pair<string, vector<string>>>& items,
                             double tipPercent, int splitCount) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
        BillResult res;
        if (processedOrders.count(orderId)) { res = {"DUPLICATE",0,0,0,0,0,""}; return res; }
        if (items.empty()) { res = {"EMPTY_ORDER",0,0,0,0,0,""}; return res; }
        // Check availability first (before any mutation)
        for (auto& [name, mods] : items) {
            if (unavailableItems.count(name)) { res = {"UNAVAILABLE",0,0,0,0,0,name}; return res; }
        }
        processedOrders.insert(orderId);
        double subtotal = 0;
        for (auto& [name, mods] : items) {
            double line = menu[name];
            for (auto& m : mods) {
                auto it = modifierPrices.find(m);
                if (it != modifierPrices.end()) line += it->second;
            }
            subtotal += line;
        }
        subtotal = r2(subtotal);
        double tax = r2(subtotal * taxRate);
        double tip = r2(subtotal * tipPercent);
        double grand = r2(subtotal + tax + tip);
        int sc = (splitCount < 1) ? 1 : splitCount;
        double pp = r2(grand / sc);
        orderLog.push_back("ORDER:" + orderId + ":total=" + to_string(grand) + ":split=" + to_string(sc));
        res = {"BILLED", subtotal, tax, tip, grand, pp, ""};
        return res;


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
    }
};

int main() {
    Restaurant rest;
    rest.reset();

    vector<pair<string, vector<string>>> items1 = {
        {"BURGER", {"CHEESE", "BACON"}},
        {"SALAD", {}}
    };
    BillResult r1 = rest.processOrder("ORD1", items1, 0.18, 2);
    // BURGER: 12.50+1.50+2.00=16.00, SALAD: 9.00, subtotal=25.00
    // tax: 25*0.085=2.13, tip: 25*0.18=4.50, grand=31.63, pp=15.82
    assert(r1.status == "BILLED");
    assert(abs(r1.subtotal - 25.0) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(r1.tax - 2.13) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(r1.grandTotal - 31.63) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(r1.perPerson - 15.82) < 0.02);
    int visibleOk = 1;
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------
    assert(visibleOk == 1);
    // Duplicate
    BillResult dup = rest.processOrder("ORD1", items1, 0.0, 1);
    assert(dup.status == "DUPLICATE");
    assert((int)rest.orderLog.size() == 1);

    // Empty order
    BillResult empty = rest.processOrder("ORD2", {}, 0.18, 1);
    assert(empty.status == "EMPTY_ORDER");

    // Unavailable item - must not process partial order
    rest.unavailableItems.insert("STEAK");
    vector<pair<string, vector<string>>> items2 = {
        {"BURGER", {}},
        {"STEAK", {"EXTRA_SAUCE"}}
    };
    int logBefore = (int)rest.orderLog.size();
    BillResult unavail = rest.processOrder("ORD3", items2, 0.15, 1);
    assert(unavail.status == "UNAVAILABLE");
    assert(unavail.unavailableItem == "STEAK");
    assert((int)rest.orderLog.size() == logBefore);  // no mutation
    // ORD3 should NOT be in processedOrders (can retry)
    assert(!rest.processedOrders.count("ORD3"));

    // Unknown modifier ignored
    vector<pair<string, vector<string>>> items3 = {
        {"SOUP", {"TRUFFLE", "CHEESE"}}
    };
    BillResult r3 = rest.processOrder("ORD4", items3, 0.0, 1);
    // SOUP=7.50 + CHEESE=1.50 + TRUFFLE=0 (unknown) = 9.00
    assert(abs(r3.subtotal - 9.0) < 0.01);

    // Zero tip, split 3
    vector<pair<string, vector<string>>> items4 = {
        {"PASTA", {}}, {"PASTA", {}}, {"SALAD", {}}
    };
    BillResult r4 = rest.processOrder("ORD5", items4, 0.0, 3);
    // subtotal=14+14+9=37, tax=37*0.085=3.15, tip=0, grand=40.15, pp=13.38
    assert(abs(r4.subtotal - 37.0) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(r4.perPerson - 13.38) < 0.02);

Test Case #8

================================================================================
Test Case ID: 8
Source: devbench-code-purpose-understanding

PREFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <deque>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
using namespace std;

struct LendResult {
    string status;  // "CHECKED_OUT", "RENEWED", "RETURNED", "DUPLICATE",
                    // "LIMIT_REACHED", "ON_HOLD", "NOT_FOUND", "ALREADY_OUT", "LOST"
    double fineAmount;
    int dueDay;
};

struct Library {
    int loanPeriod = 14;        // days
    int maxRenewals = 2;
    int borrowLimit = 5;
    double dailyFine = 0.25;
    double lostFee = 25.0;

    map<string, string> checkedOut;          // bookId -> memberId
    map<string, int> dueDays;                // bookId -> dueDay
    map<string, int> renewalCount;           // bookId -> renewals used
    map<string, int> memberBorrowCount;      // memberId -> active count
    map<string, deque<string>> holdQueue;    // bookId -> queue of memberIds
    set<string> lostBooks;
    vector<string> transactionLog;

    void reset() {
        checkedOut.clear(); dueDays.clear(); renewalCount.clear();
        memberBorrowCount.clear(); holdQueue.clear(); lostBooks.clear();
        transactionLog.clear();
    }

    // Process a library transaction.
    // action: "CHECKOUT", "RENEW", "RETURN", "LOST"
    // Rules:
    //  1. CHECKOUT:
    //     - If book already checked out, return ALREADY_OUT.
    //     - If book has a hold queue and memberId is NOT first in queue, return ON_HOLD.
    //       If memberId IS first, pop them from queue and proceed.
    //     - If member's borrowCount >= borrowLimit, return LIMIT_REACHED.
    //     - Set dueDay = currentDay + loanPeriod. Increment member count. Log checkout.
    //  2. RENEW:
    //     - If book not checked out to this member, return NOT_FOUND.
    //     - If renewalCount >= maxRenewals, return LIMIT_REACHED.
    //     - If book has holds from OTHER members, return ON_HOLD (can't renew).
    //     - Extend dueDay by loanPeriod from currentDay. Increment renewal count.
    //  3. RETURN:
    //     - If book not checked out to this member, return NOT_FOUND.
    //     - Compute fine = max(0, (currentDay - dueDay)) * dailyFine.
    //     - Clear checkout state. Decrement member count.
    //  4. LOST:
    //     - If book not checked out to this member, return NOT_FOUND.
    //     - Fine = lostFee. Mark book as lost. Clear checkout. Decrement member count.
    LendResult processTransaction(const string& bookId, const string& memberId,
                                   const string& action, int currentDay) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
        LendResult res;
        if (action == "CHECKOUT") {
            if (checkedOut.count(bookId)) { res = {"ALREADY_OUT", 0, 0}; return res; }
            if (holdQueue.count(bookId) && !holdQueue[bookId].empty()) { if (holdQueue[bookId].front() != memberId) { res = {"ON_HOLD", 0, 0}; return res; } holdQueue[bookId].pop_front(); }
            if (memberBorrowCount[memberId] >= borrowLimit) { res = {"LIMIT_REACHED", 0, 0}; return res; }
            int due = currentDay + loanPeriod; checkedOut[bookId] = memberId; dueDays[bookId] = due; renewalCount[bookId] = 0; memberBorrowCount[memberId]++;
            transactionLog.push_back("CHECKOUT:" + bookId + ":" + memberId + ":due=" + to_string(due)); res = {"CHECKED_OUT", 0, due}; return res;
        }
        if (action == "RENEW") {
            if (!checkedOut.count(bookId) || checkedOut[bookId] != memberId) { res = {"NOT_FOUND", 0, 0}; return res; }
            if (renewalCount[bookId] >= maxRenewals) { res = {"LIMIT_REACHED", 0, 0}; return res; }
            if (holdQueue.count(bookId)) { for (auto& h : holdQueue[bookId]) if (h != memberId) { res = {"ON_HOLD", 0, 0}; return res; } }
            int newDue = currentDay + loanPeriod; dueDays[bookId] = newDue; renewalCount[bookId]++;
            transactionLog.push_back("RENEW:" + bookId + ":" + memberId + ":due=" + to_string(newDue)); res = {"RENEWED", 0, newDue}; return res;
        }
        if (action == "RETURN") {
            if (!checkedOut.count(bookId) || checkedOut[bookId] != memberId) { res = {"NOT_FOUND", 0, 0}; return res; }
            double fine = max(0, currentDay - dueDays[bookId]) * dailyFine; checkedOut.erase(bookId); dueDays.erase(bookId); renewalCount.erase(bookId); memberBorrowCount[memberId]--;
            transactionLog.push_back("RETURN:" + bookId + ":" + memberId + ":fine=" + to_string(fine)); res = {"RETURNED", fine, 0}; return res;
        }
        if (action == "LOST") {
            if (!checkedOut.count(bookId) || checkedOut[bookId] != memberId) { res = {"NOT_FOUND", 0, 0}; return res; }
            lostBooks.insert(bookId); checkedOut.erase(bookId); dueDays.erase(bookId); renewalCount.erase(bookId); memberBorrowCount[memberId]--;
            transactionLog.push_back("LOST:" + bookId + ":" + memberId); res = {"LOST", lostFee, 0}; return res;
        }
        res = {"NOT_FOUND", 0, 0}; return res;


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
    }
};

int main() {
    Library lib;
    lib.reset();

    LendResult r1 = lib.processTransaction("B1", "M1", "CHECKOUT", 1);
    assert(r1.status == "CHECKED_OUT");
    assert(r1.dueDay == 15);
    assert(lib.memberBorrowCount["M1"] == 1);

    LendResult r2 = lib.processTransaction("B1", "M1", "RENEW", 10);
    assert(r2.status == "RENEWED");
    assert(r2.dueDay == 24);

    LendResult r3 = lib.processTransaction("B1", "M1", "RETURN", 30);
    assert(r3.status == "RETURNED");
    assert(abs(r3.fineAmount - 1.50) < 0.01);  // 6 days late * 0.25
    assert(lib.memberBorrowCount["M1"] == 0);
    int visibleOk = 1;
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------
    assert(visibleOk == 1);
    // Already out
    lib.processTransaction("B2", "M2", "CHECKOUT", 1);
    LendResult alrOut = lib.processTransaction("B2", "M3", "CHECKOUT", 1);
    assert(alrOut.status == "ALREADY_OUT");

    // Borrowing limit
    lib.borrowLimit = 2;
    lib.processTransaction("B3", "M3", "CHECKOUT", 1);
    lib.processTransaction("B4", "M3", "CHECKOUT", 1);
    LendResult lim = lib.processTransaction("B5", "M3", "CHECKOUT", 1);
    assert(lim.status == "LIMIT_REACHED");
    assert(lib.memberBorrowCount["M3"] == 2);

    // Hold queue: M4 holds B6, M5 tries to checkout → ON_HOLD
    lib.holdQueue["B6"].push_back("M4");
    LendResult hold = lib.processTransaction("B6", "M5", "CHECKOUT", 1);
    assert(hold.status == "ON_HOLD");
    // M4 can checkout (first in queue)
    lib.borrowLimit = 10;
    LendResult holdOk = lib.processTransaction("B6", "M4", "CHECKOUT", 1);
    assert(holdOk.status == "CHECKED_OUT");
    assert(lib.holdQueue["B6"].empty());

    // Renewal blocked by hold from other member
    lib.holdQueue["B6"].push_back("M5");
    LendResult renewHold = lib.processTransaction("B6", "M4", "RENEW", 10);
    assert(renewHold.status == "ON_HOLD");

    // Max renewals
    lib.holdQueue["B6"].clear();
    lib.processTransaction("B6", "M4", "RENEW", 10);
    lib.processTransaction("B6", "M4", "RENEW", 20);
    LendResult maxRen = lib.processTransaction("B6", "M4", "RENEW", 30);
    assert(maxRen.status == "LIMIT_REACHED");

    // Lost book
    LendResult lost = lib.processTransaction("B6", "M4", "LOST", 35);
    assert(lost.status == "LOST");
    assert(abs(lost.fineAmount - 25.0) < 0.01);
    assert(lib.lostBooks.count("B6"));
    assert(lib.memberBorrowCount["M4"] == 0);

PREFIX CODE:

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
using namespace std;

struct ReservationResult {
    string status;    // "CONFIRMED", "DUPLICATE", "NO_ROOMS", "CANCELLED",
                      // "CANCEL_FEE", "NO_SHOW", "CHECKED_IN", "NOT_FOUND"
    double totalRate;
    double deposit;
    double refund;
    double penalty;
};

struct Hotel {
    map<string, int> roomInventory;   // roomType -> available count
    double baseRate = 150.0;
    // Seasonal multipliers by month: 1-3 → 0.8, 4-6 → 1.0, 7-9 → 1.5, 10-12 → 1.2
    double earlyBirdDays = 30;        // book >=30 days before → 15% off
    double earlyBirdDiscount = 0.15;
    double depositPercent = 0.30;
    // Cancel policy: >=7 days before → full refund, 3-6 days → 50% refund, <3 days → no refund
    double noShowFeePercent = 1.0;    // 100% of total rate

    set<string> processedReservations;
    map<string, string> reservationRoom;  // resId -> roomType
    map<string, int> reservationNights;
    map<string, double> reservationTotal;
    map<string, double> reservationDeposit;
    map<string, int> reservationCheckIn;  // day of checkin
    set<string> cancelledReservations;
    vector<string> resLog;

    void reset() {
        roomInventory.clear(); processedReservations.clear();
        reservationRoom.clear(); reservationNights.clear();
        reservationTotal.clear(); reservationDeposit.clear();
        reservationCheckIn.clear(); cancelledReservations.clear();
        resLog.clear();
        roomInventory["STANDARD"] = 10;
        roomInventory["DELUXE"] = 5;
        roomInventory["SUITE"] = 2;
    }

    static double r2(double v) { return round(v * 100.0) / 100.0; }

    double seasonMultiplier(int month) {
        if (month <= 3) return 0.8;
        if (month <= 6) return 1.0;
        if (month <= 9) return 1.5;
        return 1.2;
    }

    // Process a hotel reservation action.
    // action: "BOOK", "CANCEL", "CHECKIN", "NOSHOW"
    // Rules:
    //  1. BOOK:
    //     - resId idempotent: duplicate → DUPLICATE.
    //     - If roomType not available (count <= 0), return NO_ROOMS.
    //     - nightly = baseRate * seasonMultiplier(month).
    //     - If (checkinDay - bookingDay) >= earlyBirdDays, apply earlyBirdDiscount.
    //     - totalRate = nightly * nights (rounded). deposit = totalRate * depositPercent.
    //     - Decrement room inventory. Store reservation details.
    //  2. CANCEL:
    //     - If resId not found or already cancelled, return NOT_FOUND.
    //     - daysBeforeCheckin = checkinDay - cancelDay.
    //     - >=7: full deposit refund. 3-6: 50% refund. <3: no refund.
    //     - Increment room inventory. Mark cancelled.
    //  3. CHECKIN:
    //     - If not found or cancelled, return NOT_FOUND.
    //     - Mark as checked in. Return CHECKED_IN with remaining balance (total - deposit).
    //  4. NOSHOW:
    //     - If not found or cancelled, return NOT_FOUND.
    //     - penalty = totalRate * noShowFeePercent. No room returned (charged full).
    //     - Mark cancelled.
    ReservationResult processReservation(const string& resId, const string& action,
                                          const string& roomType, int nights,
                                          int month, int bookingDay,
                                          int checkinDay, int actionDay) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
        ReservationResult res;
        if (action == "BOOK") {
            if (processedReservations.count(resId)) { res = {"DUPLICATE",0,0,0,0}; return res; }
            if (roomInventory.find(roomType) == roomInventory.end() || roomInventory[roomType] <= 0) { res = {"NO_ROOMS",0,0,0,0}; return res; }
            processedReservations.insert(resId);
            double nightly = r2(baseRate * seasonMultiplier(month));
            if (checkinDay - bookingDay >= (int)earlyBirdDays) nightly = r2(nightly * (1.0 - earlyBirdDiscount));
            double total = r2(nightly * nights); double dep = r2(total * depositPercent); roomInventory[roomType]--;
            reservationRoom[resId] = roomType; reservationNights[resId] = nights; reservationTotal[resId] = total; reservationDeposit[resId] = dep; reservationCheckIn[resId] = checkinDay;
            resLog.push_back("BOOK:" + resId + ":" + roomType + ":total=" + to_string(total)); res = {"CONFIRMED", total, dep, 0, 0}; return res;
        }
        if (action == "CANCEL") {
            if (!processedReservations.count(resId) || cancelledReservations.count(resId)) { res = {"NOT_FOUND",0,0,0,0}; return res; }
            int daysBefore = reservationCheckIn[resId] - actionDay; double dep = reservationDeposit[resId]; double refund = 0;
            if (daysBefore >= 7) refund = dep; else if (daysBefore >= 3) refund = r2(dep * 0.5);
            roomInventory[reservationRoom[resId]]++; cancelledReservations.insert(resId);
            resLog.push_back("CANCEL:" + resId + ":refund=" + to_string(refund)); res = {"CANCELLED", 0, 0, refund, r2(dep - refund)}; return res;
        }
        if (action == "CHECKIN") {
            if (!processedReservations.count(resId) || cancelledReservations.count(resId)) { res = {"NOT_FOUND",0,0,0,0}; return res; }
            double balance = r2(reservationTotal[resId] - reservationDeposit[resId]);
            resLog.push_back("CHECKIN:" + resId); res = {"CHECKED_IN", reservationTotal[resId], reservationDeposit[resId], 0, balance}; return res;
        }
        if (action == "NOSHOW") {
            if (!processedReservations.count(resId) || cancelledReservations.count(resId)) { res = {"NOT_FOUND",0,0,0,0}; return res; }
            double penalty = r2(reservationTotal[resId] * noShowFeePercent); cancelledReservations.insert(resId);
            resLog.push_back("NOSHOW:" + resId + ":penalty=" + to_string(penalty)); res = {"NO_SHOW", 0, 0, 0, penalty}; return res;
        }
        res = {"NOT_FOUND",0,0,0,0}; return res;


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
    }
};

int main() {
    Hotel hotel;
    hotel.reset();

    // Book a standard room in July (peak), 3 nights, early bird (booked 40 days ahead)
    ReservationResult r1 = hotel.processReservation("R1", "BOOK", "STANDARD", 3, 7, 1, 41, 1);
    // nightly = 150 * 1.5 = 225, early bird: 225 * 0.85 = 191.25, total = 573.75
    assert(r1.status == "CONFIRMED");
    assert(abs(r1.totalRate - 573.75) < 0.01);
    assert(abs(r1.deposit - 172.13) < 0.02);
    assert(hotel.roomInventory["STANDARD"] == 9);

    // Cancel with full refund (>=7 days before)
    ReservationResult r2 = hotel.processReservation("R1", "CANCEL", "", 0, 0, 0, 0, 30);
    assert(r2.status == "CANCELLED");
    assert(abs(r2.refund - r1.deposit) < 0.02);
    assert(hotel.roomInventory["STANDARD"] == 10);
    int visibleOk = 1;
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------
    assert(visibleOk == 1);
    // Duplicate booking
    hotel.processReservation("R2", "BOOK", "STANDARD", 2, 5, 1, 20, 1);
    ReservationResult dup = hotel.processReservation("R2", "BOOK", "STANDARD", 2, 5, 1, 20, 1);
    assert(dup.status == "DUPLICATE");

    // No rooms
    hotel.roomInventory["SUITE"] = 0;
    ReservationResult noRoom = hotel.processReservation("R3", "BOOK", "SUITE", 1, 6, 1, 10, 1);
    assert(noRoom.status == "NO_ROOMS");

    // Partial refund (3-6 days before)
    hotel.processReservation("R4", "BOOK", "DELUXE", 2, 10, 1, 50, 1);
    double dep4 = hotel.reservationDeposit["R4"];
    ReservationResult partCancel = hotel.processReservation("R4", "CANCEL", "", 0, 0, 0, 0, 45);
    // 50-45=5 days before → 50% refund
    assert(abs(partCancel.refund - dep4 * 0.5) < 0.02);

    // No refund (<3 days)
    hotel.processReservation("R5", "BOOK", "STANDARD", 1, 4, 1, 50, 1);
    ReservationResult noRefund = hotel.processReservation("R5", "CANCEL", "", 0, 0, 0, 0, 49);
    // 50-49=1 day → no refund
    assert(abs(noRefund.refund) < 0.01);

    // No-show: penalty = full rate, room NOT returned
    hotel.processReservation("R6", "BOOK", "STANDARD", 2, 7, 1, 50, 1);
    int roomsBefore = hotel.roomInventory["STANDARD"];
    double total6 = hotel.reservationTotal["R6"];
    ReservationResult ns = hotel.processReservation("R6", "NOSHOW", "", 0, 0, 0, 0, 51);
    assert(ns.status == "NO_SHOW");
    assert(abs(ns.penalty - total6) < 0.01);
    assert(hotel.roomInventory["STANDARD"] == roomsBefore);  // room NOT returned

    // Check-in
    hotel.processReservation("R7", "BOOK", "DELUXE", 3, 1, 1, 50, 1);
    ReservationResult ci = hotel.processReservation("R7", "CHECKIN", "", 0, 0, 0, 0, 50);
    assert(ci.status == "CHECKED_IN");

Test Case ID: 10

PREFIX CODE:

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>
#include <numeric>
using namespace std;

struct GradeResult {
    string status;        // "GRADED", "DUPLICATE", "INCOMPLETE"
    double finalPercent;
    string letterGrade;
    double gpaPoints;
};

struct GradeBook {
    // Categories with weights (must sum to 1.0)
    map<string, double> categoryWeights;
    // Drop-lowest: number of lowest scores to drop per category
    map<string, int> dropLowest;
    double extraCreditCap = 5.0;   // max extra credit percentage points
    set<string> processedStudents;
    vector<string> gradeLog;

    GradeBook() {
        categoryWeights["HOMEWORK"] = 0.30;
        categoryWeights["MIDTERM"] = 0.25;
        categoryWeights["FINAL"] = 0.35;
        categoryWeights["PARTICIPATION"] = 0.10;
        dropLowest["HOMEWORK"] = 2;
        dropLowest["MIDTERM"] = 0;
        dropLowest["FINAL"] = 0;
        dropLowest["PARTICIPATION"] = 0;
    }

    void reset() { processedStudents.clear(); gradeLog.clear(); }
    static double r2(double v) { return round(v * 100.0) / 100.0; }

    // Letter grade thresholds:
    //  >= 97: A+, >= 93: A, >= 90: A-,
    //  >= 87: B+, >= 83: B, >= 80: B-,
    //  >= 77: C+, >= 73: C, >= 70: C-,
    //  >= 67: D+, >= 63: D, >= 60: D-,
    //  < 60: F
    string letterFromPercent(double pct) {
        if (pct >= 97) return "A+"; if (pct >= 93) return "A"; if (pct >= 90) return "A-";
        if (pct >= 87) return "B+"; if (pct >= 83) return "B"; if (pct >= 80) return "B-";
        if (pct >= 77) return "C+"; if (pct >= 73) return "C"; if (pct >= 70) return "C-";
        if (pct >= 67) return "D+"; if (pct >= 63) return "D"; if (pct >= 60) return "D-";
        return "F";
    }

    // GPA: A+=4.0, A=4.0, A-=3.7, B+=3.3, B=3.0, B-=2.7,
    //      C+=2.3, C=2.0, C-=1.7, D+=1.3, D=1.0, D-=0.7, F=0.0
    double gpaFromLetter(const string& letter) {
        if (letter == "A+" || letter == "A") return 4.0;
        if (letter == "A-") return 3.7; if (letter == "B+") return 3.3;
        if (letter == "B") return 3.0; if (letter == "B-") return 2.7;
        if (letter == "C+") return 2.3; if (letter == "C") return 2.0;
        if (letter == "C-") return 1.7; if (letter == "D+") return 1.3;
        if (letter == "D") return 1.0; if (letter == "D-") return 0.7;
        return 0.0;
    }

    // Compute final grade for a student.
    // Rules:
    //  1. studentId is idempotent: duplicates return DUPLICATE, no mutation.
    //  2. categoryScores: map from category name to vector of scores (0-100+).
    //  3. If any category is missing from categoryScores, return INCOMPLETE.
    //  4. For each category: sort scores, drop lowest N (per dropLowest), average remaining.
    //     If all scores dropped (shouldn't happen), average = 0.
    //  5. weightedSum = sum of (categoryAvg * categoryWeight).
    //  6. Add extraCredit, but cap at extraCreditCap.
    //  7. Clamp final to [0, 100].
    //  8. Determine letter grade and GPA.
    //  9. Log "GRADE:<studentId>:<letterGrade>:<finalPercent>".
    GradeResult computeGrade(const string& studentId,
                              const map<string, vector<double>>& categoryScores,
                              double extraCredit) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
        GradeResult res;
        if (processedStudents.count(studentId)) { res = {"DUPLICATE", 0, "", 0}; return res; }
        // Check all categories present
        for (auto& [cat, wt] : categoryWeights) {
            if (categoryScores.find(cat) == categoryScores.end()) {
                res = {"INCOMPLETE", 0, "", 0}; return res;
            }
        }
        processedStudents.insert(studentId);
        double weightedSum = 0;
        for (auto& [cat, wt] : categoryWeights) {
            vector<double> scores = categoryScores.at(cat);
            sort(scores.begin(), scores.end());
            int drop = dropLowest.count(cat) ? dropLowest[cat] : 0;
            if (drop >= (int)scores.size()) drop = (int)scores.size() - 1;
            if (drop < 0) drop = 0;
            double sum = 0; int count = 0;
            for (int i = drop; i < (int)scores.size(); i++) { sum += scores[i]; count++; }
            double avg = (count > 0) ? sum / count : 0;
            weightedSum += avg * wt;
        }
        double ec = min(extraCredit, extraCreditCap);
        double finalPct = r2(weightedSum + ec);
        if (finalPct < 0) finalPct = 0; if (finalPct > 100) finalPct = 100;
        string letter = letterFromPercent(finalPct);
        double gpa = gpaFromLetter(letter);
        gradeLog.push_back("GRADE:" + studentId + ":" + letter + ":" + to_string(finalPct));
        res = {"GRADED", finalPct, letter, gpa};
        return res;


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
    }
};

int main() {
    GradeBook gb;
    gb.reset();

    map<string, vector<double>> scores = {
        {"HOMEWORK", {90, 85, 70, 95, 80, 60}},   // drop 2 lowest (60,70), avg of {80,85,90,95}=87.5
        {"MIDTERM", {78}},
        {"FINAL", {88}},
        {"PARTICIPATION", {95}}
    };
    GradeResult r1 = gb.computeGrade("S1", scores, 3.0);
    // weighted: 87.5*0.30 + 78*0.25 + 88*0.35 + 95*0.10 = 26.25+19.5+30.8+9.5 = 86.05
    // + extra credit 3.0 = 89.05 → B+
    assert(r1.status == "GRADED");
    assert(abs(r1.finalPercent - 89.05) < 0.01);
    assert(r1.letterGrade == "B+");
    assert(abs(r1.gpaPoints - 3.3) < 0.01);
    int visibleOk = 1;
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------
    assert(visibleOk == 1);
    // Duplicate
    GradeResult dup = gb.computeGrade("S1", scores, 0);
    assert(dup.status == "DUPLICATE");

    // Incomplete: missing category
    map<string, vector<double>> missing = {
        {"HOMEWORK", {90}},
        {"MIDTERM", {80}}
    };
    GradeResult inc = gb.computeGrade("S2", missing, 0);
    assert(inc.status == "INCOMPLETE");

    // Extra credit cap
    map<string, vector<double>> perfect = {
        {"HOMEWORK", {100, 100, 100}},
        {"MIDTERM", {100}},
        {"FINAL", {100}},
        {"PARTICIPATION", {100}}
    };
    GradeResult ecCap = gb.computeGrade("S3", perfect, 20.0);
    // weighted = 100, ec capped at 5, final = 100 (clamped)
    assert(abs(ecCap.finalPercent - 100.0) < 0.01);
    assert(ecCap.letterGrade == "A+");

    // Low grades → F
    map<string, vector<double>> low = {
        {"HOMEWORK", {30, 20, 10, 40, 25, 15}},  // drop 2 lowest (10,15), avg={20,25,30,40}=28.75
        {"MIDTERM", {45}},
        {"FINAL", {50}},
        {"PARTICIPATION", {60}}
    };
    GradeResult f1 = gb.computeGrade("S4", low, 0);
    // 28.75*0.3 + 45*0.25 + 50*0.35 + 60*0.10 = 8.625+11.25+17.5+6.0 = 43.375 → F
    assert(f1.letterGrade == "F");
    assert(abs(f1.gpaPoints) < 0.01);

    // Edge: A- threshold (90.0 exactly)
    map<string, vector<double>> edge = {
        {"HOMEWORK", {90, 90, 90}},
        {"MIDTERM", {90}},
        {"FINAL", {90}},
        {"PARTICIPATION", {90}}
    };
    GradeResult aMinus = gb.computeGrade("S5", edge, 0);
    assert(aMinus.letterGrade == "A-");
    assert(abs(aMinus.gpaPoints - 3.7) < 0.01);

Test Case #11

================================================================================
Test Case ID: 11
Source: devbench-code-purpose-understanding
Language: cpp

PREFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
#include <cassert>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <cmath>

struct SubBilling {
    std::string status; // "trial","active","grace","dunning","cancelled"
    int trialDaysLeft;
    double monthlyRate;
    double balance;       // outstanding balance
    double paidTotal;
    int failedAttempts;   // consecutive payment failures
    int daysSinceRenewal;
    double refundAmount;
    std::vector<std::string> auditLog;

    SubBilling(double rate, int trialDays)
        : status("trial"), trialDaysLeft(trialDays), monthlyRate(rate),
          balance(0), paidTotal(0), failedAttempts(0),
          daysSinceRenewal(0), refundAmount(0) {}

    // Advance one day. Rules:
    //  1. If trial: decrement trialDaysLeft. When it hits 0, transition to "active"
    //     and set balance to prorated amount = monthlyRate * (30 - daysSinceRenewal) / 30.
    //  2. If active: increment daysSinceRenewal. At day 30, auto-renew:
    //     add monthlyRate to balance, reset daysSinceRenewal to 0.
    //  3. If grace: increment daysSinceRenewal. After 7 days of grace, move to "dunning".
    //  4. If dunning: do nothing (awaiting manual resolution).
    //  5. Log every transition as "DAY:<status>" in auditLog.


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    void advanceDay() {
        if (status == "trial") {
            trialDaysLeft--;
            if (trialDaysLeft <= 0) { status = "active"; balance += monthlyRate * (30 - daysSinceRenewal) / 30.0; auditLog.push_back("DAY:active"); }
            else auditLog.push_back("DAY:trial");
        } else if (status == "active") {
            daysSinceRenewal++;
            if (daysSinceRenewal >= 30) { balance += monthlyRate; daysSinceRenewal = 0; auditLog.push_back("DAY:active_renewal"); }
            else auditLog.push_back("DAY:active");
        } else if (status == "grace") {
            daysSinceRenewal++;
            if (daysSinceRenewal > 7) { status = "dunning"; auditLog.push_back("DAY:dunning"); }
            else auditLog.push_back("DAY:grace");
        } else { auditLog.push_back("DAY:" + status); }
    }


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------

    // Attempt payment of given amount. Rules:
    //  1. If status is "cancelled" or "trial", return false.
    //  2. Subtract amount from balance (can go negative = credit).
    //  3. Add amount to paidTotal.
    //  4. Reset failedAttempts to 0. If status was "grace" or "dunning", set to "active".
    //  5. Log "PAY:<amount>".
    //  6. Return true.
    bool makePayment(double amount) {
        if (status == "cancelled" || status == "trial") return false;
        balance -= amount;
        paidTotal += amount;
        failedAttempts = 0;
        if (status == "grace" || status == "dunning") status = "active";
        auditLog.push_back("PAY:" + std::to_string(amount));
        return true;
    }

    // Record a failed payment. Rules:
    //  1. Increment failedAttempts.
    //  2. If failedAttempts >= 3 and status is "active", move to "grace", reset daysSinceRenewal to 0.
    //  3. Log "FAIL:<failedAttempts>".
    void failPayment() {
        failedAttempts++;
        if (failedAttempts >= 3 && status == "active") {
            status = "grace";
            daysSinceRenewal = 0;
        }
        auditLog.push_back("FAIL:" + std::to_string(failedAttempts));
    }

    // Cancel subscription. Rules:
    //  1. If already cancelled, do nothing.
    //  2. Compute refund: if active, refund = monthlyRate * (30 - daysSinceRenewal) / 30.
    //     Otherwise refund = 0.
    //  3. Set status to "cancelled". Log "CANCEL".
    void cancel() {
        if (status == "cancelled") return;
        if (status == "active") {
            refundAmount = monthlyRate * (30 - daysSinceRenewal) / 30.0;
        } else {
            refundAmount = 0;
        }
        status = "cancelled";
        auditLog.push_back("CANCEL");
    }
};

int main() {
    // Test trial -> active transition with proration
    SubBilling s1(30.0, 3);
    s1.advanceDay(); // trial, 2 left
    s1.advanceDay(); // trial, 1 left
    s1.advanceDay(); // trial->active, balance = 30*(30-0)/30 = 30
    assert(s1.status == "active");
    assert(std::abs(s1.balance - 30.0) < 0.01);
    assert(s1.auditLog.size() == 3);

    // Test auto-renewal at day 30
    SubBilling s2(60.0, 1);
    s2.advanceDay(); // trial->active
    for (int i = 0; i < 30; i++) s2.advanceDay(); // daysSinceRenewal goes to 30 -> renewal
    assert(std::abs(s2.balance - (60.0 + 60.0)) < 0.01); // proration(60) + renewal(60)

    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // Hidden: grace -> dunning transition
    SubBilling s3(50.0, 1);
    s3.advanceDay(); // trial->active
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) s3.advanceDay(); // 5 days active
    s3.failPayment(); s3.failPayment(); s3.failPayment(); // 3 failures -> grace
    assert(s3.status == "grace");
    assert(s3.daysSinceRenewal == 0);
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) s3.advanceDay(); // 8 days grace -> dunning
    assert(s3.status == "dunning");

    // Hidden: payment during dunning recovers to active
    s3.makePayment(50.0);
    assert(s3.status == "active");
    assert(s3.failedAttempts == 0);

    // Hidden: cancel from grace gives 0 refund (not active)
    SubBilling s4(100.0, 1);
    s4.advanceDay();
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) s4.advanceDay();
    s4.failPayment(); s4.failPayment(); s4.failPayment();
    assert(s4.status == "grace");
    s4.cancel();
    assert(s4.refundAmount == 0);

    // Hidden: trial payment should fail
    SubBilling s5(30.0, 5);
    assert(!s5.makePayment(10.0));

    // Hidden: prorated balance on trial->active with daysSinceRenewal=0
    SubBilling s6(90.0, 2);
    s6.advanceDay();
    s6.advanceDay(); // trial->active, balance = 90*(30-0)/30 = 90
    assert(std::abs(s6.balance - 90.0) < 0.01);

Test Case #12

================================================================================
Test Case ID: 12
Source: devbench-code-purpose-understanding

PREFIX CODE:

#include <cassert>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <sstream>

struct ShipItem {
    std::string sku;
    int qty;
    double weightEach; // lbs
    double lengthIn, widthIn, heightIn;
    double valueEach;
};

struct Shipment {
    std::string status; // "pending","picked","packed","shipped"
    std::vector<ShipItem> items;
    bool international;
    std::string destCountry;
    double totalWeight;
    double dimWeight;
    double billableWeight;
    double shippingCost;
    double insuranceCost;
    double customsDuty;
    std::string carrier;
    std::string trackingNumber;
    std::vector<std::string> auditLog;

    Shipment(bool intl, const std::string& country)
        : status("pending"), international(intl), destCountry(country),
          totalWeight(0), dimWeight(0), billableWeight(0),
          shippingCost(0), insuranceCost(0), customsDuty(0) {}

    void addItem(const ShipItem& item) {
        if (status != "pending") return;
        items.push_back(item);
    }

    // Pick all items: compute totalWeight, dimWeight, billableWeight, select carrier.
    // Rules:
    //  1. totalWeight = sum of (weightEach * qty) for each item.
    //  2. For each item, dimWeight contribution = (length * width * height * qty) / 139.0.
    //     Sum all contributions to get dimWeight.
    //  3. billableWeight = max(totalWeight, dimWeight), rounded up to next integer.
    //  4. Carrier selection by billableWeight:
    //     <= 5 lbs: "USPS", rate $0.80/lb
    //     <= 30 lbs: "UPS", rate $0.65/lb
    //     > 30 lbs: "FREIGHT", rate $0.45/lb
    //  5. shippingCost = billableWeight * rate.
    //  6. If total item value > 500, insuranceCost = totalValue * 0.02.
    //  7. If international, customsDuty = totalValue * 0.12.
    //  8. Generate trackingNumber = carrier[0] + destCountry[0..1] + to_string((int)billableWeight).
    //  9. Set status to "picked". Log "PICK:<totalWeight>:<billableWeight>:<carrier>".


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    void pick() {
        if (status != "pending" || items.empty()) return;
        totalWeight = 0;
        dimWeight = 0;
        double totalValue = 0;
        for (auto& it : items) {
            totalWeight += it.weightEach * it.qty;
            dimWeight += (it.lengthIn * it.widthIn * it.heightIn * it.qty) / 139.0;
            totalValue += it.valueEach * it.qty;
        }
        billableWeight = std::ceil(std::max(totalWeight, dimWeight));
        double rate;
        if (billableWeight <= 5) { carrier = "USPS"; rate = 0.80; }
        else if (billableWeight <= 30) { carrier = "UPS"; rate = 0.65; }
        else { carrier = "FREIGHT"; rate = 0.45; }
        shippingCost = billableWeight * rate;
        if (totalValue > 500) insuranceCost = totalValue * 0.02;
        if (international) customsDuty = totalValue * 0.12;
        trackingNumber = carrier.substr(0,1) + destCountry.substr(0,2)
                         + std::to_string((int)billableWeight);
        status = "picked";
        auditLog.push_back("PICK:" + std::to_string(totalWeight)
                           + ":" + std::to_string((int)billableWeight) + ":" + carrier);
    }


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------

    // Pack: verify items are picked, set status to "packed", log "PACK".
    void pack() {
        if (status != "picked") return;
        status = "packed";
        auditLog.push_back("PACK");
    }

    // Ship: verify packed, set status to "shipped", log "SHIP:<trackingNumber>".
    void ship() {
        if (status != "packed") return;
        status = "shipped";
        auditLog.push_back("SHIP:" + trackingNumber);
    }
};

int main() {
    // Domestic small package
    Shipment s1(false, "US");
    s1.addItem({"SKU1", 2, 1.0, 6, 6, 6, 50.0});
    s1.pick();
    assert(s1.status == "picked");
    assert(std::abs(s1.totalWeight - 2.0) < 0.01);
    // dimWeight = (6*6*6*2)/139 = 432/139 ≈ 3.11
    assert(s1.billableWeight == 4); // ceil(max(2, 3.11)) = 4
    assert(s1.carrier == "USPS");
    assert(std::abs(s1.shippingCost - 3.20) < 0.01); // 4 * 0.80
    assert(s1.insuranceCost == 0); // value=100 < 500
    s1.pack();
    s1.ship();
    assert(s1.status == "shipped");
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // Hidden: dimensional weight dominates, insurance triggered, international
    Shipment s2(true, "CA");
    s2.addItem({"BIG", 1, 2.0, 30, 20, 15, 600.0});
    s2.pick();
    // dimWeight = (30*20*15*1)/139 = 9000/139 ≈ 64.75
    // totalWeight = 2.0, billable = ceil(64.75) = 65
    assert(s2.billableWeight == 65);
    assert(s2.carrier == "FREIGHT");
    assert(std::abs(s2.shippingCost - 65 * 0.45) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(s2.insuranceCost - 600.0 * 0.02) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(s2.customsDuty - 600.0 * 0.12) < 0.01);
    assert(s2.trackingNumber == "FCA65");

    // Hidden: multiple items, UPS range
    Shipment s3(false, "US");
    s3.addItem({"A", 3, 3.0, 10, 10, 10, 100.0});
    s3.addItem({"B", 2, 2.0, 5, 5, 5, 50.0});
    s3.pick();
    // totalWeight = 9+4=13, dimWeight = (1000*3)/139 + (125*2)/139 = 21.58+1.80=23.38
    // billable = ceil(23.38) = 24
    assert(s3.billableWeight == 24);
    assert(s3.carrier == "UPS");
    assert(std::abs(s3.shippingCost - 24 * 0.65) < 0.01);
    assert(s3.insuranceCost == 0); // value=400 < 500

    // Hidden: empty items should not pick
    Shipment s4(false, "US");
    s4.pick();
    assert(s4.status == "pending");

    // Hidden: can't add items after pick
    Shipment s5(false, "US");
    s5.addItem({"X", 1, 1.0, 5, 5, 5, 10.0});
    s5.pick();
    s5.addItem({"Y", 1, 1.0, 5, 5, 5, 10.0});
    assert(s5.items.size() == 1);
