#include <cassert>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <set>
#include <cmath>

struct LeaveManager {
    double vacationBalance;    // in days (supports half-days as 0.5)
    double sickBalance;
    double carryoverLimit;     // max days carried over from previous year
    int unpaidDaysTaken;
    std::set<int> blackoutDays; // day numbers that cannot have vacation
    bool managerApproved;       // must be set before vacation can be taken
    std::vector<std::string> leaveLog;

    LeaveManager(double vacInit, double sickInit, double carryLimit)
        : vacationBalance(vacInit), sickBalance(sickInit),
          carryoverLimit(carryLimit), unpaidDaysTaken(0),
          managerApproved(false) {}

    void addBlackout(int day) { blackoutDays.insert(day); }
    void approveVacation() { managerApproved = true; }
    void revokeApproval() { managerApproved = false; }

    // Request leave for a given day. Rules:
    //  type: "vacation", "sick", "unpaid"
    //  halfDay: if true, deducts 0.5 instead of 1.0
    //
    //  1. If type=="vacation":
    //     a. If day is in blackoutDays, return "BLACKOUT".
    //     b. If !managerApproved, return "NO_APPROVAL".
    //     c. Compute deduction = halfDay ? 0.5 : 1.0.
    //     d. If vacationBalance < deduction, return "INSUFFICIENT".
    //     e. Deduct from vacationBalance, log "VAC:<day>:<deduction>", return "OK".
    //     f. After a vacation is taken, revoke approval (must re-approve for next).
    //  2. If type=="sick":
    //     a. Compute deduction = halfDay ? 0.5 : 1.0.
    //     b. If sickBalance >= deduction, deduct from sickBalance, log "SICK:<day>:<deduction>", return "OK".
    //     c. Else: the remainder (deduction - sickBalance) converts to unpaid.
    //        Set sickBalance to 0, increment unpaidDaysTaken by 1, log "SICK_TO_UNPAID:<day>", return "PARTIAL".
    //  3. If type=="unpaid":
    //     a. Increment unpaidDaysTaken by 1 (always full day regardless of halfDay).
    //     b. Log "UNPAID:<day>", return "OK".
    //  4. Otherwise return "INVALID".


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    std::string requestLeave(const std::string& type, int day, bool halfDay) {
        double deduction = halfDay ? 0.5 : 1.0;
        if (type == "vacation") {
            if (blackoutDays.count(day)) return "BLACKOUT";
            if (!managerApproved) return "NO_APPROVAL";
            if (vacationBalance < deduction) return "INSUFFICIENT";
            vacationBalance -= deduction;
            leaveLog.push_back("VAC:" + std::to_string(day) + ":" + std::to_string(deduction));
            managerApproved = false; return "OK";
        } else if (type == "sick") {
            if (sickBalance >= deduction) { sickBalance -= deduction; leaveLog.push_back("SICK:" + std::to_string(day) + ":" + std::to_string(deduction)); return "OK"; }
            sickBalance = 0; unpaidDaysTaken++; leaveLog.push_back("SICK_TO_UNPAID:" + std::to_string(day)); return "PARTIAL";
        } else if (type == "unpaid") {
            unpaidDaysTaken++; leaveLog.push_back("UNPAID:" + std::to_string(day)); return "OK";
        }
        return "INVALID";
    }


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------

    // Year-end carryover: cap vacationBalance at carryoverLimit, reset sick to given amount.
    void yearEndReset(double newSickDays) {
        if (vacationBalance > carryoverLimit)
            vacationBalance = carryoverLimit;
        sickBalance = newSickDays;
        unpaidDaysTaken = 0;
        managerApproved = false;
        leaveLog.push_back("YEAR_END");
    }
};

int main() {
    LeaveManager lm(10.0, 5.0, 5.0);
    lm.addBlackout(25);
    lm.addBlackout(26);

    // Vacation requires approval
    assert(lm.requestLeave("vacation", 10, false) == "NO_APPROVAL");
    lm.approveVacation();
    assert(lm.requestLeave("vacation", 10, false) == "OK");
    assert(std::abs(lm.vacationBalance - 9.0) < 0.01);
    // Approval is revoked after use
    assert(lm.requestLeave("vacation", 11, false) == "NO_APPROVAL");

    // Blackout check
    lm.approveVacation();
    assert(lm.requestLeave("vacation", 25, false) == "BLACKOUT");
    // Approval NOT consumed on blackout
    assert(lm.requestLeave("vacation", 12, false) == "OK");

    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // Hidden: half-day vacation
    LeaveManager lm2(2.0, 1.0, 1.0);
    lm2.approveVacation();
    assert(lm2.requestLeave("vacation", 1, true) == "OK");
    assert(std::abs(lm2.vacationBalance - 1.5) < 0.01);
    // Approval revoked even for half-day
    assert(lm2.requestLeave("vacation", 2, true) == "NO_APPROVAL");

    // Hidden: sick to unpaid conversion
    LeaveManager lm3(5.0, 0.3, 5.0);
    assert(lm3.requestLeave("sick", 1, false) == "PARTIAL");
    assert(lm3.sickBalance == 0);
    assert(lm3.unpaidDaysTaken == 1);

    // Hidden: half-day sick when balance is 0.5
    LeaveManager lm4(5.0, 0.5, 5.0);
    assert(lm4.requestLeave("sick", 1, true) == "OK");
    assert(std::abs(lm4.sickBalance) < 0.01);

    // Hidden: unpaid always full day even if halfDay=true
    LeaveManager lm5(5.0, 5.0, 5.0);
    assert(lm5.requestLeave("unpaid", 1, true) == "OK");
    assert(lm5.unpaidDaysTaken == 1); // not 0.5

    // Hidden: year-end carryover caps vacation
    LeaveManager lm6(8.0, 2.0, 5.0);
    lm6.yearEndReset(10.0);
    assert(std::abs(lm6.vacationBalance - 5.0) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(lm6.sickBalance - 10.0) < 0.01);
    assert(lm6.unpaidDaysTaken == 0);

    // Hidden: invalid type
    LeaveManager lm7(5.0, 5.0, 5.0);
    assert(lm7.requestLeave("personal", 1, false) == "INVALID");

    // Hidden: insufficient vacation
    LeaveManager lm8(0.3, 5.0, 5.0);
    lm8.approveVacation();
    assert(lm8.requestLeave("vacation", 1, false) == "INSUFFICIENT");

Test Case ID: 14

PREFIX CODE:

#include <cassert>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <map>
#include <set>

struct OrderResult {
    std::string status; // "FILLED","PARTIAL","REJECTED","MARGIN_FAIL"
    int filledQty;
    double avgPrice;
    double commission;
    int settlementDay;
};

struct TradingEngine {
    // Order book: price -> quantity available at that price (sorted ascending for asks)
    std::map<double, int> askBook;
    // Portfolio
    std::map<std::string, int> positions;
    double cashBalance;
    double marginUsed;
    double marginLimit;
    int currentDay;
    std::set<std::string> processedOrders;
    std::vector<std::string> tradeLog;

    TradingEngine(double cash, double mLimit)
        : cashBalance(cash), marginUsed(0), marginLimit(mLimit), currentDay(1) {}

    void addAsk(double price, int qty) { askBook[price] += qty; }

    // Execute a buy order. Rules:
    //  1. If orderId already in processedOrders, return {"DUPLICATE",0,0,0,0}.
    //  2. orderType: "MARKET" or "LIMIT".
    //     For LIMIT: only match asks with price <= limitPrice.
    //     For MARKET: match all asks in ascending price order.
    //  3. Match asks from lowest to highest price:
    //     - Take min(ask qty, remaining order qty).
    //     - Accumulate cost = sum(price * qty_filled_at_that_price).
    //     - Remove or reduce ask entries.
    //  4. After matching:
    //     - filledQty = total matched, avgPrice = totalCost / filledQty (0 if none).
    //     - commission = 0.001 * totalCost (0.1%), minimum $1.00 if filledQty > 0.
    //     - totalDebit = totalCost + commission.
    //     - If totalDebit > cashBalance + (marginLimit - marginUsed), UNDO all fills
    //       (restore askBook), return {"MARGIN_FAIL",0,0,0,0}.
    //     - Otherwise: deduct totalDebit from cashBalance (can go negative to use margin),
    //       if cashBalance < 0, marginUsed += (-cashBalance), cashBalance = 0.
    //     - Update positions[symbol] += filledQty.
    //     - settlementDay = currentDay + 2 (T+2).
    //  5. If filledQty == requestedQty: status="FILLED". If filledQty > 0: "PARTIAL". Else "REJECTED".
    //  6. Add orderId to processedOrders.
    //  7. Log "TRADE:<orderId>:<status>:<filledQty>@<avgPrice>".


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    OrderResult executeBuy(const std::string& orderId, const std::string& symbol, const std::string& orderType, int requestedQty, double limitPrice) {
        if (processedOrders.count(orderId)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, 0, 0};
        processedOrders.insert(orderId);
        auto savedBook = askBook; double totalCost = 0; int filledQty = 0;
        auto it = askBook.begin();
        while (it != askBook.end() && filledQty < requestedQty) {
            if (orderType == "LIMIT" && it->first > limitPrice) break;
            int take = std::min(it->second, requestedQty - filledQty);
            totalCost += it->first * take; filledQty += take;
            it->second -= take; if (it->second == 0) it = askBook.erase(it); else ++it;
        }
        double avgPrice = filledQty > 0 ? totalCost / filledQty : 0;
        double commission = filledQty > 0 ? std::max(1.0, totalCost * 0.001) : 0;
        double totalDebit = totalCost + commission;
        if (filledQty > 0 && totalDebit > cashBalance + (marginLimit - marginUsed)) { askBook = savedBook; return {"MARGIN_FAIL", 0, 0, 0, 0}; }
        cashBalance -= totalDebit;
        if (cashBalance < 0) { marginUsed += (-cashBalance); cashBalance = 0; }
        positions[symbol] += filledQty;
        std::string status = (filledQty == requestedQty) ? "FILLED" : (filledQty > 0) ? "PARTIAL" : "REJECTED";
        tradeLog.push_back("TRADE:" + orderId + ":" + status + ":" + std::to_string(filledQty) + "@" + std::to_string(avgPrice));
        return {status, filledQty, avgPrice, commission, currentDay + 2};
    }


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------

};

int main() {
    TradingEngine eng(10000.0, 5000.0);
    eng.addAsk(100.0, 10);
    eng.addAsk(101.0, 20);
    eng.addAsk(105.0, 5);

    // Market buy 15 shares: fills 10@100 + 5@101 = 1505, commission max(1, 1.505)=1.505
    auto r1 = eng.executeBuy("O1", "AAPL", "MARKET", 15, 0);
    assert(r1.status == "FILLED");
    assert(r1.filledQty == 15);
    assert(std::abs(r1.avgPrice - 1505.0/15) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r1.commission - 1.505) < 0.01);
    assert(r1.settlementDay == 3);
    assert(eng.positions["AAPL"] == 15);

    // Limit buy 20@101: only fills remaining 15@101, partial
    auto r2 = eng.executeBuy("O2", "AAPL", "LIMIT", 20, 101.0);
    assert(r2.status == "PARTIAL");
    assert(r2.filledQty == 15);
    assert(eng.positions["AAPL"] == 30);

    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // Hidden: duplicate order
    TradingEngine eng2(10000.0, 5000.0);
    eng2.addAsk(50.0, 10);
    auto rd1 = eng2.executeBuy("X1", "GOOG", "MARKET", 5, 0);
    assert(rd1.status == "FILLED");
    auto rd2 = eng2.executeBuy("X1", "GOOG", "MARKET", 5, 0);
    assert(rd2.status == "DUPLICATE");
    assert(eng2.positions["GOOG"] == 5);

    // Hidden: margin failure with rollback
    TradingEngine eng3(100.0, 50.0);
    eng3.addAsk(200.0, 5);
    auto rm = eng3.executeBuy("M1", "TSLA", "MARKET", 5, 0);
    // totalCost=1000, commission=1, totalDebit=1001 > 100+50=150 -> MARGIN_FAIL
    assert(rm.status == "MARGIN_FAIL");
    assert(rm.filledQty == 0);
    // askBook must be restored
    assert(eng3.askBook[200.0] == 5);
    assert(eng3.cashBalance == 100.0);

    // Hidden: margin usage (partial margin)
    TradingEngine eng4(80.0, 100.0);
    eng4.addAsk(50.0, 2);
    auto rmu = eng4.executeBuy("MU1", "IBM", "MARKET", 2, 0);
    // cost=100, commission=1, debit=101. Cash goes to 80-101=-21, margin used=21, cash=0
    assert(rmu.status == "FILLED");
    assert(eng4.cashBalance == 0);
    assert(std::abs(eng4.marginUsed - 21.0) < 0.01);

    // Hidden: rejected (no asks matching limit)
    TradingEngine eng5(10000.0, 5000.0);
    eng5.addAsk(200.0, 10);
    auto rr = eng5.executeBuy("R1", "MSFT", "LIMIT", 5, 100.0);
    assert(rr.status == "REJECTED");
    assert(rr.filledQty == 0);
    assert(rr.commission == 0);

    // Hidden: commission minimum $1
    TradingEngine eng6(10000.0, 5000.0);
    eng6.addAsk(5.0, 1);
    auto rc = eng6.executeBuy("C1", "PENNY", "MARKET", 1, 0);
    // cost=5, commission = max(1, 0.005) = 1.0
    assert(std::abs(rc.commission - 1.0) < 0.01);

Test Case #15

================================================================================
Test Case ID: 15
Source: devbench-code-purpose-understanding

PREFIX CODE:

#include <cassert>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>

struct Milestone {
    std::string id;
    std::string name;
    int durationDays;
    double budget;
    double spent;
    int progress; // 0-100
    std::vector<std::string> dependencies; // milestone IDs that must complete first
    bool completed;
};

struct Project {
    std::map<std::string, Milestone> milestones;
    std::vector<std::string> statusLog;
    double totalBudget;
    double totalSpent;

    Project() : totalBudget(0), totalSpent(0) {}

    void addMilestone(const Milestone& m) {
        milestones[m.id] = m;
        totalBudget += m.budget;
    }

    // Update progress on a milestone. Rules:
    //  1. If milestone doesn't exist, return "NOT_FOUND".
    //  2. If any dependency is not completed, return "DEPS_BLOCKED".
    //  3. Set progress to newProgress (clamped to 0-100).
    //  4. Add spending: additionalSpend is added to milestone.spent and totalSpent.
    //  5. If progress reaches 100, set completed=true.
    //  6. Compute riskFlags:
    //     a. "OVER_BUDGET" if milestone.spent > milestone.budget.
    //     b. "SLOW" if progress < 50 and milestone.spent > milestone.budget * 0.5.
    //  7. Log "UPDATE:<id>:<progress>%:<risk_flags_comma_separated>" (empty string if no flags).
    //  8. Return "OK".


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    std::string updateProgress(const std::string& msId, int newProgress, double additionalSpend) {
        auto it = milestones.find(msId);
        if (it == milestones.end()) return "NOT_FOUND";
        Milestone& ms = it->second;
        for (auto& dep : ms.dependencies) {
            if (!milestones[dep].completed) return "DEPS_BLOCKED";
        }
        ms.progress = std::max(0, std::min(100, newProgress));
        ms.spent += additionalSpend;
        totalSpent += additionalSpend;
        if (ms.progress >= 100) ms.completed = true;
        std::string flags;
        if (ms.spent > ms.budget) {
            flags += "OVER_BUDGET";
        }
        if (ms.progress < 50 && ms.spent > ms.budget * 0.5) {
            if (!flags.empty()) flags += ",";
            flags += "SLOW";
        }
        statusLog.push_back("UPDATE:" + msId + ":" + std::to_string(ms.progress) + "%:" + flags);
        return "OK";
    }


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------

    // Compute overall project progress: weighted average by budget.
    // progress = sum(milestone.progress * milestone.budget) / totalBudget
    double overallProgress() const {
        if (totalBudget == 0) return 0;
        double sum = 0;
        for (auto& [id, ms] : milestones) {
            sum += ms.progress * ms.budget;
        }
        return sum / totalBudget;
    }
};

int main() {
    Project p;
    p.addMilestone({"M1", "Design", 10, 1000, 0, 0, {}, false});
    p.addMilestone({"M2", "Build", 20, 3000, 0, 0, {"M1"}, false});
    p.addMilestone({"M3", "Test", 10, 1000, 0, 0, {"M2"}, false});

    // M2 blocked by M1
    assert(p.updateProgress("M2", 50, 100) == "DEPS_BLOCKED");

    // Complete M1
    assert(p.updateProgress("M1", 100, 800) == "OK");
    assert(p.milestones["M1"].completed);

    // Now M2 can proceed
    assert(p.updateProgress("M2", 30, 2000) == "OK");
    // M2: spent=2000 > budget*0.5=1500, progress=30 < 50 -> SLOW flag
    assert(p.statusLog.back().find("SLOW") != std::string::npos);

    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // Hidden: OVER_BUDGET flag
    Project p2;
    p2.addMilestone({"A", "TaskA", 5, 500, 0, 0, {}, false});
    p2.updateProgress("A", 80, 600);
    assert(p2.statusLog.back().find("OVER_BUDGET") != std::string::npos);

    // Hidden: both flags at once
    Project p3;
    p3.addMilestone({"B", "TaskB", 5, 100, 0, 0, {}, false});
    p3.updateProgress("B", 30, 120); // progress<50, spent(120)>budget(100), spent>budget*0.5
    assert(p3.statusLog.back().find("OVER_BUDGET") != std::string::npos);
    assert(p3.statusLog.back().find("SLOW") != std::string::npos);

    // Hidden: NOT_FOUND
    Project p4;
    assert(p4.updateProgress("NONE", 50, 100) == "NOT_FOUND");

    // Hidden: clamping progress
    Project p5;
    p5.addMilestone({"C", "TaskC", 5, 1000, 0, 0, {}, false});
    p5.updateProgress("C", 150, 0);
    assert(p5.milestones["C"].progress == 100);
    assert(p5.milestones["C"].completed);

    // Hidden: overall progress weighted
    Project p6;
    p6.addMilestone({"D1", "X", 5, 1000, 0, 0, {}, false});
    p6.addMilestone({"D2", "Y", 5, 3000, 0, 0, {}, false});
    p6.updateProgress("D1", 100, 0);
    p6.updateProgress("D2", 50, 0);
    // overall = (100*1000 + 50*3000) / 4000 = 250000/4000 = 62.5
    assert(std::abs(p6.overallProgress() - 62.5) < 0.01);

    // Hidden: negative clamping
    Project p7;
    p7.addMilestone({"E", "Z", 5, 1000, 0, 0, {}, false});
    p7.updateProgress("E", -10, 0);
    assert(p7.milestones["E"].progress == 0);
    assert(!p7.milestones["E"].completed);

================================================================================
Test Case ID: 16
Source: devbench-code-purpose-understanding

PREFIX CODE:

#include <cassert>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>

struct Appointment {
    std::string apptId;
    std::string patientId;
    std::string providerId;
    int slot; // time slot number
    double copay;
    std::string status; // "CONFIRMED","CANCELLED","WAITLISTED"
};

struct ClinicScheduler {
    // provider -> set of booked slots
    std::map<std::string, std::set<int>> providerSchedule;
    // patient -> set of booked slots
    std::map<std::string, std::set<int>> patientSchedule;
    // provider -> set of unavailable slots (blocked)
    std::map<std::string, std::set<int>> providerBlocked;
    // insurance -> copay rate (fraction of $100 base)
    std::map<std::string, double> insuranceRates;
    // waitlist: slot -> list of {apptId, patientId, providerId}
    std::map<int, std::vector<Appointment>> waitlist;
    // all appointments
    std::map<std::string, Appointment> appointments;
    std::vector<std::string> auditLog;
    int nextApptId;

    ClinicScheduler() : nextApptId(1) {}

    void blockProvider(const std::string& prov, int slot) {
        providerBlocked[prov].insert(slot);
    }
    void setInsuranceRate(const std::string& ins, double rate) {
        insuranceRates[ins] = rate;
    }

    // Book an appointment. Rules:
    //  1. If provider slot is in providerBlocked, return "PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE".
    //  2. If provider slot already booked (providerSchedule), add to waitlist, return "WAITLISTED".
    //  3. If patient already has an appointment at this slot, return "PATIENT_CONFLICT".
    //  4. Compute copay: base $100 * insuranceRates[insurance]. If insurance not found, copay = $100.
    //  5. Create appointment, add to providerSchedule and patientSchedule.
    //  6. Log "BOOK:<apptId>:<providerId>:<slot>:<copay>".
    //  7. Return "CONFIRMED".


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    std::string bookAppointment(const std::string& patientId, const std::string& providerId, int slot, const std::string& insurance) {
        if (providerBlocked[providerId].count(slot)) return "PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE";
        std::string apptId = "A" + std::to_string(nextApptId++);
        double rate = insuranceRates.count(insurance) ? insuranceRates[insurance] : 1.0;
        double copay = 100.0 * rate;
        if (providerSchedule[providerId].count(slot)) {
            Appointment wl{apptId, patientId, providerId, slot, copay, "WAITLISTED"};
            waitlist[slot].push_back(wl); appointments[apptId] = wl;
            auditLog.push_back("WAITLIST:" + apptId + ":" + providerId + ":" + std::to_string(slot));
            return "WAITLISTED";
        }
        if (patientSchedule[patientId].count(slot)) return "PATIENT_CONFLICT";
        Appointment appt{apptId, patientId, providerId, slot, copay, "CONFIRMED"};
        providerSchedule[providerId].insert(slot); patientSchedule[patientId].insert(slot);
        appointments[apptId] = appt;
        auditLog.push_back("BOOK:" + apptId + ":" + providerId + ":" + std::to_string(slot) + ":" + std::to_string(copay));
        return "CONFIRMED";
    }


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------

    // Cancel an appointment. Rules:
    //  1. Find appointment by apptId. If not found or already cancelled, return "NOT_FOUND".
    //  2. Set status to "CANCELLED".
    //  3. Remove from providerSchedule and patientSchedule.
    //  4. If there's anyone on waitlist for that provider+slot, promote first entry:
    //     set their status to "CONFIRMED", add to schedules.
    //  5. Log "CANCEL:<apptId>". If promoted, also log "PROMOTE:<promotedApptId>".
    //  6. Return "CANCELLED".
    std::string cancelAppointment(const std::string& apptId) {
        auto it = appointments.find(apptId);
        if (it == appointments.end() || it->second.status == "CANCELLED")
            return "NOT_FOUND";
        Appointment& appt = it->second;
        appt.status = "CANCELLED";
        providerSchedule[appt.providerId].erase(appt.slot);
        patientSchedule[appt.patientId].erase(appt.slot);
        auditLog.push_back("CANCEL:" + apptId);

        // Promote from waitlist
        int s = appt.slot;
        if (waitlist.count(s) && !waitlist[s].empty()) {
            // Find first waitlisted entry for same provider
            for (auto wit = waitlist[s].begin(); wit != waitlist[s].end(); ++wit) {
                if (wit->providerId == appt.providerId && wit->status == "WAITLISTED") {
                    if (!patientSchedule[wit->patientId].count(s)) {
                        wit->status = "CONFIRMED";
                        appointments[wit->apptId].status = "CONFIRMED";
                        providerSchedule[appt.providerId].insert(s);
                        patientSchedule[wit->patientId].insert(s);
                        auditLog.push_back("PROMOTE:" + wit->apptId);
                        waitlist[s].erase(wit);
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return "CANCELLED";
    }
};

int main() {
    ClinicScheduler cs;
    cs.setInsuranceRate("BlueCross", 0.20);
    cs.setInsuranceRate("Aetna", 0.30);
    cs.blockProvider("DrSmith", 5);

    // Blocked slot
    assert(cs.bookAppointment("P1", "DrSmith", 5, "BlueCross") == "PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE");

    // Normal booking (blocked call didn't consume an ID, so this is A1)
    assert(cs.bookAppointment("P1", "DrSmith", 10, "BlueCross") == "CONFIRMED");
    assert(cs.appointments["A1"].copay == 20.0);

    // Same slot, same provider -> waitlist
    assert(cs.bookAppointment("P2", "DrSmith", 10, "Aetna") == "WAITLISTED");

    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // Hidden: patient conflict
    ClinicScheduler cs2;
    cs2.bookAppointment("PA", "Dr1", 1, "none");
    assert(cs2.bookAppointment("PA", "Dr2", 1, "none") == "PATIENT_CONFLICT");

    // Hidden: unknown insurance defaults to $100
    ClinicScheduler cs3;
    cs3.bookAppointment("PB", "Dr1", 2, "Unknown");
    assert(cs3.appointments["A1"].copay == 100.0);

    // Hidden: cancel and promote from waitlist
    ClinicScheduler cs4;
    cs4.setInsuranceRate("Ins", 0.25);
    cs4.bookAppointment("P1", "DrA", 3, "Ins"); // A1 confirmed
    cs4.bookAppointment("P2", "DrA", 3, "Ins"); // A2 waitlisted
    assert(cs4.appointments["A2"].status == "WAITLISTED");
    cs4.cancelAppointment("A1");
    assert(cs4.appointments["A2"].status == "CONFIRMED");
    assert(cs4.providerSchedule["DrA"].count(3));
    assert(cs4.patientSchedule["P2"].count(3));

    // Hidden: blocked call does NOT consume an apptId
    ClinicScheduler cs5;
    cs5.blockProvider("Dr1", 1);
    assert(cs5.bookAppointment("P1", "Dr1", 1, "none") == "PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE");
    assert(cs5.bookAppointment("P1", "Dr1", 2, "none") == "CONFIRMED");
    // Since blocked didn't consume ID, this booking is A1
    assert(cs5.appointments.count("A1"));
    assert(cs5.appointments["A1"].slot == 2);

    // Hidden: cancel already-cancelled
    ClinicScheduler cs6;
    cs6.bookAppointment("P1", "Dr1", 1, "none");
    cs6.cancelAppointment("A1");
    assert(cs6.cancelAppointment("A1") == "NOT_FOUND");

Test Case ID: 17

PREFIX CODE:

#include <cassert>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>

struct CartItem {
    std::string sku;
    std::string category; // "electronics","clothing","food","luxury"
    double price;
    int qty;
};

struct Coupon {
    std::string code;
    double discountPct; // 0-1
    double minPurchase;
    bool singleUse;
    std::set<std::string> excludedCategories;
};

struct CheckoutResult {
    std::string status; // "SUCCESS","INSUFFICIENT_INVENTORY","COUPON_INVALID","EXPIRED","AUTH_FAIL"
    double subtotal;
    double discount;
    double tax;
    double shipping;
    double total;
};

struct CartProcessor {
    std::vector<CartItem> items;
    std::map<std::string, int> inventory; // sku -> qty available
    std::map<std::string, Coupon> coupons;
    std::set<std::string> usedCoupons;
    // Tax rates by category
    std::map<std::string, double> taxRates;
    int cartCreatedTime;
    int cartExpiryMinutes;
    std::vector<std::string> auditLog;

    CartProcessor(int createdTime, int expiryMin)
        : cartCreatedTime(createdTime), cartExpiryMinutes(expiryMin) {
        taxRates["electronics"] = 0.08;
        taxRates["clothing"] = 0.04;
        taxRates["food"] = 0.0;
        taxRates["luxury"] = 0.12;
    }

    void addItem(const CartItem& item) { items.push_back(item); }
    void setInventory(const std::string& sku, int qty) { inventory[sku] = qty; }
    void addCoupon(const Coupon& c) { coupons[c.code] = c; }

    // Checkout the cart. Rules (must be checked in this order):
    //  1. Check cart expiry: if currentTime - cartCreatedTime > cartExpiryMinutes, return EXPIRED.
    //  2. Check inventory for all items: if any item's qty > inventory[sku], return INSUFFICIENT_INVENTORY.
    //     Do NOT deduct inventory yet.
    //  3. Compute subtotal = sum(price * qty) for all items.
    //  4. Apply coupon if provided (couponCode != ""):
    //     a. If coupon not found or (singleUse and already in usedCoupons): return COUPON_INVALID.
    //     b. If subtotal < coupon.minPurchase: return COUPON_INVALID.
    //     c. Compute eligible subtotal: sum of (price*qty) for items NOT in excludedCategories.
    //     d. discount = eligibleSubtotal * coupon.discountPct.
    //     e. If singleUse, add to usedCoupons.
    //  5. Compute tax: for each item, tax += price * qty * taxRates[category] (0 if unknown category).
    //     Tax is computed on ORIGINAL prices (before discount).
    //  6. Compute shipping based on (subtotal - discount):
    //     >= 100: free, >= 50: $5.99, >= 25: $8.99, else $12.99.
    //  7. total = subtotal - discount + tax + shipping.
    //  8. If paymentAuthorized is false, return AUTH_FAIL. (Do NOT deduct inventory.)
    //  9. Deduct inventory for all items.
    //  10. Log "CHECKOUT:<total>:<status>".
    //  11. Return SUCCESS with all computed values.


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    CheckoutResult checkout(int currentTime, const std::string& couponCode, bool paymentAuthorized) {
        if (currentTime - cartCreatedTime > cartExpiryMinutes) return {"EXPIRED", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
        for (auto& item : items) if (item.qty > inventory[item.sku]) return {"INSUFFICIENT_INVENTORY", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
        double subtotal = 0; for (auto& item : items) subtotal += item.price * item.qty;
        double discount = 0;
        if (!couponCode.empty()) {
            auto cit = coupons.find(couponCode);
            if (cit == coupons.end() || (cit->second.singleUse && usedCoupons.count(couponCode))) return {"COUPON_INVALID", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
            if (subtotal < cit->second.minPurchase) return {"COUPON_INVALID", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
            double eligible = 0;
            for (auto& item : items) if (!cit->second.excludedCategories.count(item.category)) eligible += item.price * item.qty;
            discount = eligible * cit->second.discountPct;
            if (cit->second.singleUse) usedCoupons.insert(couponCode);
        }
        double tax = 0;
        for (auto& item : items) { double r = taxRates.count(item.category) ? taxRates[item.category] : 0; tax += item.price * item.qty * r; }
        double ad = subtotal - discount;
        double shipping = ad >= 100 ? 0 : ad >= 50 ? 5.99 : ad >= 25 ? 8.99 : 12.99;
        double total = subtotal - discount + tax + shipping;
        if (!paymentAuthorized) return {"AUTH_FAIL", subtotal, discount, tax, shipping, total};
        for (auto& item : items) inventory[item.sku] -= item.qty;
        auditLog.push_back("CHECKOUT:" + std::to_string(total) + ":SUCCESS");
        return {"SUCCESS", subtotal, discount, tax, shipping, total};
    }


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------

};

int main() {
    CartProcessor cp(0, 30); // created at time 0, expires after 30 min
    cp.setInventory("PHONE", 10);
    cp.setInventory("SHIRT", 20);
    cp.addItem({"PHONE", "electronics", 500.0, 1});
    cp.addItem({"SHIRT", "clothing", 30.0, 2});
    cp.addCoupon({"SAVE10", 0.10, 50.0, true, {"luxury"}});

    auto r1 = cp.checkout(10, "SAVE10", true);
    assert(r1.status == "SUCCESS");
    // subtotal = 500 + 60 = 560
    assert(std::abs(r1.subtotal - 560.0) < 0.01);
    // discount: all items eligible (no luxury), discount = 560 * 0.10 = 56
    assert(std::abs(r1.discount - 56.0) < 0.01);
    // tax: 500*0.08 + 60*0.04 = 40 + 2.4 = 42.4
    assert(std::abs(r1.tax - 42.4) < 0.01);
    // shipping: 560-56=504 >= 100, free
    assert(std::abs(r1.shipping) < 0.01);
    // total = 560 - 56 + 42.4 + 0 = 546.4
    assert(std::abs(r1.total - 546.4) < 0.01);

    // Coupon is single-use, second use fails
    cp.addItem({"PHONE", "electronics", 500.0, 1});
    cp.setInventory("PHONE", 10);
    // Need new items since old ones were checked out
    CartProcessor cp2(0, 30);
    cp2.setInventory("PHONE", 5);
    cp2.addItem({"PHONE", "electronics", 100.0, 1});
    cp2.addCoupon({"SAVE10", 0.10, 50.0, true, {"luxury"}});
    cp2.usedCoupons.insert("SAVE10"); // already used
    auto r2 = cp2.checkout(5, "SAVE10", true);
    assert(r2.status == "COUPON_INVALID");

    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // Hidden: cart expiry
    CartProcessor cp3(0, 10);
    cp3.setInventory("X", 5);
    cp3.addItem({"X", "food", 20.0, 1});
    auto re = cp3.checkout(11, "", true);
    assert(re.status == "EXPIRED");

    // Hidden: insufficient inventory
    CartProcessor cp4(0, 60);
    cp4.setInventory("Y", 2);
    cp4.addItem({"Y", "clothing", 50.0, 5});
    auto ri = cp4.checkout(5, "", true);
    assert(ri.status == "INSUFFICIENT_INVENTORY");

    // Hidden: excluded category in coupon
    CartProcessor cp5(0, 60);
    cp5.setInventory("LUX", 3);
    cp5.addItem({"LUX", "luxury", 200.0, 2});
    cp5.addCoupon({"NOLUX", 0.20, 100.0, false, {"luxury"}});
    auto rl = cp5.checkout(5, "NOLUX", true);
    assert(rl.status == "SUCCESS");
    assert(std::abs(rl.discount) < 0.01); // all items excluded
    // tax: 400*0.12 = 48
    assert(std::abs(rl.tax - 48.0) < 0.01);
    // shipping: 400-0=400 >= 100, free
    assert(std::abs(rl.shipping) < 0.01);

    // Hidden: auth fail does NOT deduct inventory
    CartProcessor cp6(0, 60);
    cp6.setInventory("Z", 3);
    cp6.addItem({"Z", "electronics", 40.0, 2});
    auto ra = cp6.checkout(5, "", false);
    assert(ra.status == "AUTH_FAIL");
    assert(cp6.inventory["Z"] == 3); // not deducted

    // Hidden: shipping tiers
    CartProcessor cp7(0, 60);
    cp7.setInventory("A", 100);
    cp7.addItem({"A", "food", 10.0, 1}); // subtotal=10, no discount
    auto rs = cp7.checkout(1, "", true);
    assert(std::abs(rs.shipping - 12.99) < 0.01);

    CartProcessor cp8(0, 60);
    cp8.setInventory("B", 100);
    cp8.addItem({"B", "food", 30.0, 1});
    auto rs2 = cp8.checkout(1, "", true);
    assert(std::abs(rs2.shipping - 8.99) < 0.01);

    CartProcessor cp9(0, 60);
    cp9.setInventory("E", 5);
    cp9.addItem({"E", "electronics", 100.0, 1});
    cp9.addCoupon({"HALF", 0.50, 0.0, false, {}});
    auto rt = cp9.checkout(1, "HALF", true);
    // tax = 100*0.08 = 8 (on original, not on 50)
    assert(std::abs(rt.tax - 8.0) < 0.01);
    // discount = 100*0.50 = 50
    assert(std::abs(rt.discount - 50.0) < 0.01);

    // Hidden: coupon minPurchase check
    CartProcessor cp10(0, 60);
    cp10.setInventory("F", 5);
    cp10.addItem({"F", "clothing", 20.0, 1});
    cp10.addCoupon({"BIG", 0.10, 100.0, false, {}});
    auto rm = cp10.checkout(1, "BIG", true);
    assert(rm.status == "COUPON_INVALID"); // subtotal 20 < minPurchase 100

Test Case #18

================================================================================
Test Case ID: 18
Source: devbench-code-purpose-understanding
Language: cpp

PREFIX CODE:

#include <cassert>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <map>
#include <cmath>

struct ServiceRecord {
    std::string vehicleId;
    std::string serviceType;
    int mileage;
    double laborCost;
    double partsCost;
    bool warrantyApplied;
    bool rentalProvided;
};

struct FleetManager {
    struct Vehicle {
        int currentMileage;
        int lastServiceMileage;
        int warrantyMileageLimit;  // warranty valid up to this mileage
        std::vector<ServiceRecord> history;
    };

    std::map<std::string, Vehicle> vehicles;
    std::map<std::string, int> partInventory;  // part name -> qty
    int serviceInterval;       // miles between services
    double laborRatePerHour;
    std::vector<std::string> auditLog;

    FleetManager(int interval, double laborRate)
        : serviceInterval(interval), laborRatePerHour(laborRate) {}

    void addVehicle(const std::string& id, int mileage, int warrantyLimit) {
        vehicles[id] = {mileage, mileage, warrantyLimit, {}};
    }
    void addPart(const std::string& part, int qty) { partInventory[part] += qty; }

    // Perform service on a vehicle. Rules:
    //  1. If vehicle not found, return "NOT_FOUND".
    //  2. If (currentMileage - lastServiceMileage) < serviceInterval, return "NOT_DUE".
    //  3. Estimate laborHours based on serviceType:
    //     "oil_change": 1, "tire_rotation": 2, "brake_service": 4, "transmission": 8. Default: 2.
    //  4. Parts required (map serviceType to part name):
    //     "oil_change"->"oil_filter", "tire_rotation"->"none",
    //     "brake_service"->"brake_pads", "transmission"->"trans_fluid". Default: "none".
    //  5. If part != "none" and partInventory[part] < 1, return "NO_PARTS".
    //  6. laborCost = laborHours * laborRatePerHour.
    //  7. partsCost: "oil_filter"=$25, "brake_pads"=$80, "trans_fluid"=$120, else $0.
    //  8. warrantyApplied: if currentMileage <= warrantyMileageLimit, partsCost = 0, set warrantyApplied=true.
    //  9. If currentMileage > warrantyMileageLimit + 50000, provide rental: rentalProvided=true.
    //     (Old vehicle out of warranty by >50k miles needs longer service, gets rental.)
    //  10. If part != "none", decrement partInventory[part].
    //  11. Update lastServiceMileage = currentMileage.
    //  12. Record in history, log "SERVICE:<vehicleId>:<serviceType>:<totalCost>".
    //  13. Return "SERVICED".


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    std::string performService(const std::string& vehicleId, const std::string& serviceType) {
        auto it = vehicles.find(vehicleId); if (it == vehicles.end()) return "NOT_FOUND";
        Vehicle& v = it->second;
        if (v.currentMileage - v.lastServiceMileage < serviceInterval) return "NOT_DUE";
        int lh = serviceType == "oil_change" ? 1 : serviceType == "tire_rotation" ? 2 : serviceType == "brake_service" ? 4 : serviceType == "transmission" ? 8 : 2;
        std::string part = serviceType == "oil_change" ? "oil_filter" : serviceType == "brake_service" ? "brake_pads" : serviceType == "transmission" ? "trans_fluid" : "none";
        if (part != "none" && partInventory[part] < 1) return "NO_PARTS";
        double laborCost = lh * laborRatePerHour;
        double partsCost = part == "oil_filter" ? 25 : part == "brake_pads" ? 80 : part == "trans_fluid" ? 120 : 0;
        bool warrantyApplied = false;
        if (v.currentMileage <= v.warrantyMileageLimit) { partsCost = 0; warrantyApplied = true; }
        bool rentalProvided = (v.currentMileage > v.warrantyMileageLimit + 50000);
        if (part != "none") partInventory[part]--;
        v.lastServiceMileage = v.currentMileage;
        double totalCost = laborCost + partsCost;
        v.history.push_back({vehicleId, serviceType, v.currentMileage, laborCost, partsCost, warrantyApplied, rentalProvided});
        auditLog.push_back("SERVICE:" + vehicleId + ":" + serviceType + ":" + std::to_string(totalCost));
        return "SERVICED";
    }


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------

    void updateMileage(const std::string& id, int newMileage) {
        if (vehicles.count(id)) vehicles[id].currentMileage = newMileage;
    }
};

int main() {
    FleetManager fm(5000, 75.0);
    fm.addVehicle("V1", 10000, 36000);
    fm.addPart("oil_filter", 5);
    fm.addPart("brake_pads", 3);

    // Not due yet (0 miles since last service)
    assert(fm.performService("V1", "oil_change") == "NOT_DUE");

    fm.updateMileage("V1", 15000);
    assert(fm.performService("V1", "oil_change") == "SERVICED");
    // Under warranty: partsCost=0, labor=1*75=75
    assert(fm.vehicles["V1"].history.back().warrantyApplied);
    assert(fm.vehicles["V1"].history.back().partsCost == 0);
    assert(fm.vehicles["V1"].history.back().laborCost == 75.0);

    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // Hidden: out of warranty, parts cost applies
    FleetManager fm2(5000, 50.0);
    fm2.addVehicle("V2", 40000, 36000); // over warranty
    fm2.addPart("brake_pads", 2);
    fm2.updateMileage("V2", 45000);
    assert(fm2.performService("V2", "brake_service") == "SERVICED");
    assert(!fm2.vehicles["V2"].history.back().warrantyApplied);
    assert(fm2.vehicles["V2"].history.back().partsCost == 80.0);
    assert(fm2.vehicles["V2"].history.back().laborCost == 200.0); // 4*50

    // Hidden: rental provided when way out of warranty
    FleetManager fm3(5000, 50.0);
    fm3.addVehicle("V3", 90000, 36000); // 90000 > 36000+50000=86000
    fm3.addPart("oil_filter", 1);
    fm3.updateMileage("V3", 95000);
    assert(fm3.performService("V3", "oil_change") == "SERVICED");
    assert(fm3.vehicles["V3"].history.back().rentalProvided);

    // Hidden: no rental when within 50k of warranty end
    FleetManager fm4(5000, 50.0);
    fm4.addVehicle("V4", 60000, 36000); // 60000 < 36000+50000=86000
    fm4.addPart("oil_filter", 1);
    fm4.updateMileage("V4", 65000);
    assert(fm4.performService("V4", "oil_change") == "SERVICED");
    assert(!fm4.vehicles["V4"].history.back().rentalProvided);

    // Hidden: no parts available
    FleetManager fm5(5000, 50.0);
    fm5.addVehicle("V5", 10000, 50000);
    fm5.updateMileage("V5", 15000);
    assert(fm5.performService("V5", "transmission") == "NO_PARTS");

    // Hidden: tire rotation needs no parts
    FleetManager fm6(5000, 50.0);
    fm6.addVehicle("V6", 10000, 50000);
    fm6.updateMileage("V6", 15000);
    assert(fm6.performService("V6", "tire_rotation") == "SERVICED");
    assert(fm6.vehicles["V6"].history.back().partsCost == 0);
    assert(fm6.vehicles["V6"].history.back().laborCost == 100.0); // 2*50

    // Hidden: not found
    FleetManager fm7(5000, 50.0);
    assert(fm7.performService("NOPE", "oil_change") == "NOT_FOUND");

    // Hidden: part inventory decrements
    FleetManager fm8(5000, 50.0);
    fm8.addVehicle("V8", 10000, 50000);
    fm8.addPart("oil_filter", 1);
    fm8.updateMileage("V8", 15000);
    fm8.performService("V8", "oil_change");
    assert(fm8.partInventory["oil_filter"] == 0);
    fm8.updateMileage("V8", 20000);
    assert(fm8.performService("V8", "oil_change") == "NO_PARTS");

PREFIX CODE:

#include <cassert>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <cmath>

struct EscrowAccount {
    double salePrice;
    double earnestDeposit;
    double escrowBalance;   // funds held
    bool inspectionPassed;
    bool appraisalPassed;
    bool titleClear;
    double buyerClosingPct;  // fraction of closing costs buyer pays (rest is seller)
    double closingCostRate;  // as fraction of sale price
    double annualTax;
    double monthlyHoa;
    int closingDayOfYear;    // 1-365
    std::string status;      // "OPEN","CONTINGENCY_FAIL","CLOSED","REFUNDED"
    std::vector<std::string> ledger;

    EscrowAccount(double price, double earnest, double buyerPct, double closingRate,
                  double tax, double hoa, int closingDay)
        : salePrice(price), earnestDeposit(earnest), escrowBalance(earnest),
          inspectionPassed(false), appraisalPassed(false), titleClear(false),
          buyerClosingPct(buyerPct), closingCostRate(closingRate),
          annualTax(tax), monthlyHoa(hoa), closingDayOfYear(closingDay),
          status("OPEN") {}

    void passInspection() { inspectionPassed = true; ledger.push_back("INSPECTION:PASS"); }
    void failInspection() { inspectionPassed = false; ledger.push_back("INSPECTION:FAIL"); }
    void passAppraisal() { appraisalPassed = true; ledger.push_back("APPRAISAL:PASS"); }
    void failAppraisal() { appraisalPassed = false; ledger.push_back("APPRAISAL:FAIL"); }
    void clearTitle() { titleClear = true; ledger.push_back("TITLE:CLEAR"); }

    // Close the escrow. Rules (in order):
    //  1. If status != "OPEN", return "ALREADY_CLOSED".
    //  2. If !inspectionPassed, set status="CONTINGENCY_FAIL", refund earnest to buyer,
    //     set escrowBalance=0, log "REFUND:INSPECTION:<earnestDeposit>", return "CONTINGENCY_FAIL".
    //  3. If !appraisalPassed, set status="CONTINGENCY_FAIL", refund earnest minus $500 appraisal fee,
    //     set escrowBalance = 0, log "REFUND:APPRAISAL:<refundAmount>", return "CONTINGENCY_FAIL".
    //  4. If !titleClear, set status="CONTINGENCY_FAIL", refund full earnest,
    //     set escrowBalance=0, log "REFUND:TITLE:<earnestDeposit>", return "CONTINGENCY_FAIL".
    //  5. Compute prorated tax: annualTax * (closingDayOfYear / 365.0). Seller owes this to buyer.
    //  6. Compute prorated HOA: monthlyHoa * (closingDayOfYear % 30) / 30.0. Seller credit to buyer.
    //  7. Compute total closing costs: salePrice * closingCostRate.
    //     Buyer pays: closingCosts * buyerClosingPct.
    //     Seller pays: closingCosts * (1 - buyerClosingPct).
    //  8. Disbursement waterfall:
    //     a. buyerOwes = salePrice - earnestDeposit + buyerClosingCosts - proratedTax - proratedHoa.
    //     b. sellerReceives = salePrice - sellerClosingCosts - proratedTax - proratedHoa.
    //     c. escrowBalance = buyerOwes (buyer deposits remaining).
    //  9. Set status="CLOSED".
    //  10. Log "CLOSE:buyer_owes=<X>:seller_receives=<Y>".
    //  11. Return "CLOSED".


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    std::string closeEscrow() {
        if (status != "OPEN") return "ALREADY_CLOSED";
        if (!inspectionPassed) { status = "CONTINGENCY_FAIL"; escrowBalance = 0; ledger.push_back("REFUND:INSPECTION:" + std::to_string(earnestDeposit)); return "CONTINGENCY_FAIL"; }
        if (!appraisalPassed) { status = "CONTINGENCY_FAIL"; escrowBalance = 0; ledger.push_back("REFUND:APPRAISAL:" + std::to_string(earnestDeposit - 500.0)); return "CONTINGENCY_FAIL"; }
        if (!titleClear) { status = "CONTINGENCY_FAIL"; escrowBalance = 0; ledger.push_back("REFUND:TITLE:" + std::to_string(earnestDeposit)); return "CONTINGENCY_FAIL"; }
        double proratedTax = annualTax * (closingDayOfYear / 365.0);
        double proratedHoa = monthlyHoa * ((closingDayOfYear % 30) / 30.0);
        double totalClosing = salePrice * closingCostRate;
        double buyerClosing = totalClosing * buyerClosingPct;
        double sellerClosing = totalClosing * (1.0 - buyerClosingPct);
        double buyerOwes = salePrice - earnestDeposit + buyerClosing - proratedTax - proratedHoa;
        double sellerReceives = salePrice - sellerClosing - proratedTax - proratedHoa;
        escrowBalance = buyerOwes;
        status = "CLOSED";
        ledger.push_back("CLOSE:buyer_owes=" + std::to_string(buyerOwes) + ":seller_receives=" + std::to_string(sellerReceives));
        return "CLOSED";
    }


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------

};

int main() {
    // Standard closing: 300k house, 10k earnest, 60/40 split, 3% closing, tax 6000/yr, HOA 300/mo, day 180
    EscrowAccount e1(300000, 10000, 0.60, 0.03, 6000, 300, 180);
    e1.passInspection();
    e1.passAppraisal();
    e1.clearTitle();
    auto r1 = e1.closeEscrow();
    assert(r1 == "CLOSED");
    // proratedTax = 6000 * 180/365 ≈ 2958.90
    double pt1 = 6000.0 * (180.0 / 365.0);
    // proratedHoa = 300 * (180%30)/30 = 300 * 0/30 = 0
    double ph1 = 300.0 * ((180 % 30) / 30.0);
    // closingCosts = 300000*0.03 = 9000, buyer=5400, seller=3600
    double buyerOwes = 300000 - 10000 + 5400 - pt1 - ph1;
    double sellerReceives = 300000 - 3600 - pt1 - ph1;
    assert(std::abs(e1.escrowBalance - buyerOwes) < 1.0);

    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // Hidden: inspection failure -> full refund
    EscrowAccount e2(500000, 20000, 0.50, 0.02, 8000, 400, 100);
    // Don't pass inspection
    e2.passAppraisal();
    e2.clearTitle();
    auto r2 = e2.closeEscrow();
    assert(r2 == "CONTINGENCY_FAIL");
    assert(e2.escrowBalance == 0);
    // Ledger should have REFUND:INSPECTION
    bool foundInsp = false;
    for (auto& l : e2.ledger) if (l.find("REFUND:INSPECTION") != std::string::npos) foundInsp = true;
    assert(foundInsp);

    // Hidden: appraisal failure -> earnest minus $500
    EscrowAccount e3(400000, 15000, 0.50, 0.02, 8000, 400, 100);
    e3.passInspection();
    // Don't pass appraisal
    e3.clearTitle();
    auto r3 = e3.closeEscrow();
    assert(r3 == "CONTINGENCY_FAIL");
    // Refund = 15000 - 500 = 14500
    bool foundAppr = false;
    for (auto& l : e3.ledger) if (l.find("REFUND:APPRAISAL") != std::string::npos) foundAppr = true;
    assert(foundAppr);

    // Hidden: title unclear -> full refund even with everything else passing
    EscrowAccount e4(400000, 15000, 0.50, 0.02, 8000, 400, 100);
    e4.passInspection();
    e4.passAppraisal();
    // Don't clear title
    auto r4 = e4.closeEscrow();
    assert(r4 == "CONTINGENCY_FAIL");
    bool foundTitle = false;
    for (auto& l : e4.ledger) if (l.find("REFUND:TITLE") != std::string::npos) foundTitle = true;
    assert(foundTitle);

    // Hidden: already closed
    EscrowAccount e5(200000, 5000, 0.50, 0.02, 4000, 200, 90);
    e5.passInspection();
    e5.passAppraisal();
    e5.clearTitle();
    e5.closeEscrow();
    assert(e5.closeEscrow() == "ALREADY_CLOSED");

    // Hidden: prorated HOA with non-zero remainder
    EscrowAccount e6(200000, 5000, 0.50, 0.02, 3650, 300, 45);
    e6.passInspection();
    e6.passAppraisal();
    e6.clearTitle();
    e6.closeEscrow();
    // proratedTax = 3650 * 45/365 = 450
    // proratedHoa = 300 * (45%30)/30 = 300 * 15/30 = 150
    // closingCosts = 200000*0.02 = 4000, buyer=2000, seller=2000
    // buyerOwes = 200000 - 5000 + 2000 - 450 - 150 = 196400
    double expected6 = 200000 - 5000 + 2000 - 450 - 150;
    assert(std::abs(e6.escrowBalance - expected6) < 1.0);

#include <cassert>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>

struct EnergyBill {
    double kwhUsed;
    double kwhGenerated;    // solar/net metering
    double peakDemandKw;
    double powerFactor;     // 0-1
    bool isEstimated;
    double tieredCharge;
    double demandCharge;
    double pfPenalty;
    double netMeteringCredit;
    double minimumBill;
    double totalBill;
    std::string billType;   // "ACTUAL" or "ESTIMATED"
    std::vector<std::string> lineItems;
};

struct EnergyBiller {
    // Tiered rate structure (kWh thresholds and rates)
    // Tier 1: first 500 kWh at $0.08/kWh
    // Tier 2: next 500 kWh (501-1000) at $0.12/kWh
    // Tier 3: above 1000 kWh at $0.18/kWh
    double tier1Limit = 500;
    double tier1Rate = 0.08;
    double tier2Limit = 1000;
    double tier2Rate = 0.12;
    double tier3Rate = 0.18;

    // Time-of-use multiplier: peak hours add 50% surcharge
    double peakMultiplier = 1.5;

    // Demand charge: $5 per kW of peak demand
    double demandRate = 5.0;

    // Power factor penalty: if PF < 0.90, penalty = (0.90 - PF) * tieredCharge
    double pfThreshold = 0.90;

    // Net metering: excess generation credited at $0.05/kWh
    double netMeteringRate = 0.05;

    // Minimum bill
    double minBill = 15.0;

    // If estimated reading, apply 0.95 factor to usage (conservative estimate)
    double estimationFactor = 0.95;

    std::vector<std::string> billingLog;

    // Compute a monthly bill. Rules (in order):
    //  1. If isEstimated, adjust kwhUsed = kwhUsed * estimationFactor. Set billType="ESTIMATED", else "ACTUAL".
    //  2. Net metering: netKwh = kwhUsed - kwhGenerated. If netKwh < 0,
    //     netMeteringCredit = (-netKwh) * netMeteringRate, set netKwh = 0.
    //  3. Compute tiered charge on netKwh:
    //     tier1 = min(netKwh, tier1Limit) * tier1Rate
    //     tier2 = min(max(netKwh - tier1Limit, 0), tier2Limit - tier1Limit) * tier2Rate
    //     tier3 = max(netKwh - tier2Limit, 0) * tier3Rate
    //     tieredCharge = tier1 + tier2 + tier3.
    //  4. Apply peak hour multiplier if peakFraction > 0:
    //     tieredCharge = tieredCharge * (1 + (peakMultiplier - 1) * peakFraction).
    //  5. Demand charge = peakDemandKw * demandRate.
    //  6. Power factor penalty: if powerFactor < pfThreshold,
    //     pfPenalty = (pfThreshold - powerFactor) * tieredCharge.
    //  7. subtotal = tieredCharge + demandCharge + pfPenalty - netMeteringCredit.
    //  8. totalBill = max(subtotal, minBill).
    //  9. Log "BILL:<totalBill>:<billType>".
    //  10. Return the EnergyBill with all fields populated.


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    EnergyBill computeBill(double kwhUsed, double kwhGenerated, double peakDemandKw, double powerFactor, bool isEstimated, double peakFraction) {
        EnergyBill bill; bill.isEstimated = isEstimated; bill.billType = isEstimated ? "ESTIMATED" : "ACTUAL";
        if (isEstimated) kwhUsed *= estimationFactor;
        bill.kwhUsed = kwhUsed; bill.kwhGenerated = kwhGenerated; bill.peakDemandKw = peakDemandKw; bill.powerFactor = powerFactor;
        double netKwh = kwhUsed - kwhGenerated; bill.netMeteringCredit = 0;
        if (netKwh < 0) { bill.netMeteringCredit = (-netKwh) * netMeteringRate; netKwh = 0; }
        double t1 = std::min(netKwh, tier1Limit) * tier1Rate;
        double t2 = std::min(std::max(netKwh - tier1Limit, 0.0), tier2Limit - tier1Limit) * tier2Rate;
        double t3 = std::max(netKwh - tier2Limit, 0.0) * tier3Rate;
        bill.tieredCharge = (t1 + t2 + t3) * (1.0 + (peakMultiplier - 1.0) * peakFraction);
        bill.demandCharge = peakDemandKw * demandRate;
        bill.pfPenalty = powerFactor < pfThreshold ? (pfThreshold - powerFactor) * bill.tieredCharge : 0;
        double subtotal = bill.tieredCharge + bill.demandCharge + bill.pfPenalty - bill.netMeteringCredit;
        bill.minimumBill = minBill; bill.totalBill = std::max(subtotal, minBill);
        billingLog.push_back("BILL:" + std::to_string(bill.totalBill) + ":" + bill.billType);
        return bill;
    }


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------

};

int main() {
    EnergyBiller biller;

    // Basic bill: 800 kWh, no solar, 10kW peak, PF=0.95, actual, no peak hours
    auto b1 = biller.computeBill(800, 0, 10, 0.95, false, 0.0);
    assert(b1.billType == "ACTUAL");
    // tier1: 500*0.08=40, tier2: 300*0.12=36, tier3: 0 -> tiered=76
    assert(std::abs(b1.tieredCharge - 76.0) < 0.01);
    // demand: 10*5=50
    assert(std::abs(b1.demandCharge - 50.0) < 0.01);
    // PF OK (0.95 >= 0.90), no penalty
    assert(std::abs(b1.pfPenalty) < 0.01);
    // total = 76+50+0-0 = 126
    assert(std::abs(b1.totalBill - 126.0) < 0.01);

    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // Hidden: estimated reading reduces usage
    EnergyBiller b2;
    auto r2 = b2.computeBill(1000, 0, 5, 0.95, true, 0.0);
    assert(r2.billType == "ESTIMATED");
    // adjusted: 1000*0.95 = 950
    // tier1: 500*0.08=40, tier2: 450*0.12=54 -> tiered=94
    assert(std::abs(r2.tieredCharge - 94.0) < 0.01);

    // Hidden: net metering credit when generation > usage
    EnergyBiller b3;
    auto r3 = b3.computeBill(300, 500, 0, 0.95, false, 0.0);
    // netKwh = 300-500 = -200, credit = 200*0.05 = 10, netKwh=0
    assert(std::abs(r3.netMeteringCredit - 10.0) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r3.tieredCharge) < 0.01); // no usage charge
    // total = 0 + 0 + 0 - 10 = -10, but min bill = 15
    assert(std::abs(r3.totalBill - 15.0) < 0.01);

    // Hidden: power factor penalty
    EnergyBiller b4;
    auto r4 = b4.computeBill(600, 0, 8, 0.80, false, 0.0);
    // tier1: 500*0.08=40, tier2: 100*0.12=12 -> tiered=52
    // PF penalty: (0.90-0.80)*52 = 5.2
    assert(std::abs(r4.pfPenalty - 5.2) < 0.01);
    // demand: 8*5=40
    // total = 52+40+5.2-0 = 97.2
    assert(std::abs(r4.totalBill - 97.2) < 0.01);

    // Hidden: tier 3 pricing
    EnergyBiller b5;
    auto r5 = b5.computeBill(1500, 0, 0, 0.95, false, 0.0);
    // tier1: 500*0.08=40, tier2: 500*0.12=60, tier3: 500*0.18=90 -> tiered=190
    assert(std::abs(r5.tieredCharge - 190.0) < 0.01);

    // Hidden: peak multiplier
    EnergyBiller b6;
    auto r6 = b6.computeBill(500, 0, 5, 0.95, false, 0.40);
    // tier1: 500*0.08=40
    // with peak: 40 * (1 + 0.5*0.4) = 40 * 1.2 = 48
    assert(std::abs(r6.tieredCharge - 48.0) < 0.01);

    // Hidden: minimum bill kicks in
    EnergyBiller b7;
    auto r7 = b7.computeBill(10, 0, 0, 0.95, false, 0.0);
    // tier1: 10*0.08=0.80, demand: 0, no penalty
    // subtotal=0.80 < 15 -> total=15
    assert(std::abs(r7.totalBill - 15.0) < 0.01);

    // Hidden: combined — estimated + net metering + peak + PF penalty
    EnergyBiller b8;
    auto r8 = b8.computeBill(2000, 100, 15, 0.85, true, 0.30);
    // adjusted: 2000*0.95=1900, net=1900-100=1800
    // tier1: 500*0.08=40, tier2: 500*0.12=60, tier3: 800*0.18=144 -> 244
    // peak: 244*(1+0.5*0.3) = 244*1.15 = 280.6
    // demand: 15*5=75
    // PF: (0.90-0.85)*280.6 = 14.03
    // total = 280.6+75+14.03-0 = 369.63
    assert(std::abs(r8.tieredCharge - 280.6) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r8.pfPenalty - 14.03) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r8.totalBill - 369.63) < 0.5);

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <cassert>
#include <algorithm>

struct BidEntry {
    std::string bidder;
    double amount;
    int timestamp;
};

class Auction {
public:
    double starting_price;
    double reserve_price;
    double min_increment;
    int deadline;           // auction end timestamp
    int snipe_window;       // seconds before deadline that trigger extension
    int extension_seconds;  // how much to extend on snipe

    double current_bid;
    std::string current_winner;
    bool reserve_met;
    std::vector<BidEntry> history;

    Auction(double start, double reserve, double increment,
            int deadline, int snipe_win, int ext_sec)
        : starting_price(start), reserve_price(reserve),
          min_increment(increment), deadline(deadline),
          snipe_window(snipe_win), extension_seconds(ext_sec),
          current_bid(start), current_winner(""), reserve_met(false) {}

    bool is_active(int ts) const { return ts <= deadline; }



GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    void place_bid(const std::string& bidder, double amount, int timestamp) {
        if (!is_active(timestamp))
            throw std::runtime_error("auction ended");
        if (amount < current_bid + min_increment)
            throw std::runtime_error("bid too low");
        current_bid = amount;
        current_winner = bidder;
        if (amount >= reserve_price)
            reserve_met = true;
        history.push_back({bidder, amount, timestamp});
        if (deadline - timestamp <= snipe_window)
            deadline += extension_seconds;
    }


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
};

int main() {
    // Auction: start 100, reserve 500, increment 10, deadline 1000, snipe within 60s, extend by 120s
    Auction auc(100, 500, 10, 1000, 60, 120);
    auc.place_bid("alice", 110, 100);
    assert(auc.current_bid == 110);
    assert(auc.current_winner == "alice");
    assert(!auc.reserve_met);
    assert(auc.history.size() == 1);

    auc.place_bid("bob", 200, 200);
    assert(auc.current_bid == 200);
    assert(!auc.reserve_met);

    // Bid below minimum increment should fail
    try {
        auc.place_bid("carol", 205, 300);  // need at least 210
        assert(false);
    } catch (const std::runtime_error&) {}
    assert(auc.current_bid == 200);  // unchanged
    assert(auc.history.size() == 2); // no new entry

    // Bid that meets reserve
    auc.place_bid("carol", 550, 400);
    assert(auc.reserve_met);

    // Snipe bid: within 60s of deadline (1000), should extend
    assert(auc.deadline == 1000);
    auc.place_bid("dave", 600, 960);  // 1000-960=40 <= 60
    assert(auc.deadline == 1120);  // extended by 120

    // Bid after original deadline but before extended deadline is valid
    auc.place_bid("alice", 700, 1050);
    assert(auc.current_winner == "alice");
    assert(auc.history.size() == 5);

    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // Hidden assertions
    Auction a2(50, 200, 5, 500, 30, 60);
    // First bid must be at least starting_price + min_increment = 55
    try {
        a2.place_bid("x", 54, 10);
        assert(false);
    } catch (const std::runtime_error&) {}

    a2.place_bid("x", 55, 10);
    assert(a2.current_bid == 55);
    assert(a2.history.size() == 1);
    assert(!a2.reserve_met);

    a2.place_bid("y", 200, 20);
    assert(a2.reserve_met);
    assert(a2.current_winner == "y");

    // Bid after deadline fails
    try {
        a2.place_bid("z", 300, 501);
        assert(false);
    } catch (const std::runtime_error&) {}

    // Snipe extends deadline
    Auction a3(10, 100, 1, 100, 20, 50);
    a3.place_bid("a", 11, 85);  // 100-85=15 <= 20 -> snipe
    assert(a3.deadline == 150);
    a3.place_bid("b", 20, 140); // 150-140=10 <= 20 -> snipe again
    assert(a3.deadline == 200);

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>

class GymMembership {
public:
    std::string member_id;
    double monthly_rate;
    double enrollment_fee;
    double freeze_fee_per_month;
    int family_members;        // 0 = individual
    double family_discount_pct; // applied to monthly_rate only, not add-ons
    bool has_personal_training;
    double pt_monthly_cost;
    int notice_period_months;

    std::string status;  // "active", "frozen", "cancelled"
    double total_charged;
    int months_active;
    int months_frozen;
    std::vector<std::string> charge_log;

    GymMembership(const std::string& id, double rate, double enroll,
                  double freeze_fee, int family, double fam_disc,
                  double pt_cost, int notice)
        : member_id(id), monthly_rate(rate), enrollment_fee(enroll),
          freeze_fee_per_month(freeze_fee), family_members(family),
          family_discount_pct(fam_disc), has_personal_training(false),
          pt_monthly_cost(pt_cost), notice_period_months(notice),
          status("pending"), total_charged(0), months_active(0),
          months_frozen(0) {}

    double _discounted_rate() const {
        if (family_members > 0)
            return monthly_rate * (1.0 - family_discount_pct);
        return monthly_rate;
    }



GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    void enroll(int days_remaining_in_month, int days_in_month) {
        if (status != "pending")
            throw std::runtime_error("already enrolled");
        status = "active";
        double prorated = _discounted_rate() * days_remaining_in_month / days_in_month;
        double first_charge = enrollment_fee + prorated;
        total_charged += first_charge;
        charge_log.push_back("enroll:" + std::to_string(first_charge));
    }

    void bill_month() {
        if (status == "frozen") {
            total_charged += freeze_fee_per_month;
            months_frozen++;
            charge_log.push_back("freeze:" + std::to_string(freeze_fee_per_month));
            return;
        }
        if (status != "active")
            throw std::runtime_error("cannot bill");
        double charge = _discounted_rate();
        if (has_personal_training)
            charge += pt_monthly_cost;
        total_charged += charge;
        months_active++;
        charge_log.push_back("bill:" + std::to_string(charge));
    }


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------

    void freeze() {
        if (status != "active") throw std::runtime_error("can only freeze active");
        status = "frozen";
    }

    void unfreeze() {
        if (status != "frozen") throw std::runtime_error("not frozen");
        status = "active";
    }

    void add_personal_training() { has_personal_training = true; }
    void remove_personal_training() { has_personal_training = false; }
};

int main() {
    // rate=100, enroll_fee=50, freeze_fee=25, family=2, disc=10%, pt=40, notice=1
    GymMembership gym("m1", 100, 50, 25, 2, 0.10, 40, 1);

    // Enroll on day 15 of 30-day month -> prorated = 90 * 15/30 = 45
    gym.enroll(15, 30);
    assert(std::abs(gym.total_charged - 95.0) < 0.01);  // 50 enroll + 45 prorated
    assert(gym.status == "active");
    assert(gym.charge_log.size() == 1);

    // Bill month without PT: 90 (discounted)
    gym.bill_month();
    assert(std::abs(gym.total_charged - 185.0) < 0.01);
    assert(gym.months_active == 1);

    // Add PT, bill month: 90 + 40 = 130
    gym.add_personal_training();
    gym.bill_month();
    assert(std::abs(gym.total_charged - 315.0) < 0.01);

    // Freeze: charges freeze fee instead
    gym.freeze();
    gym.bill_month();
    assert(std::abs(gym.total_charged - 340.0) < 0.01);
    assert(gym.months_frozen == 1);
    assert(gym.months_active == 2);

    // Unfreeze and bill: PT still on, 90 + 40 = 130
    gym.unfreeze();
    gym.bill_month();
    assert(std::abs(gym.total_charged - 470.0) < 0.01);

    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // Individual membership (no family discount)
    GymMembership g2("m2", 80, 30, 20, 0, 0.0, 50, 2);
    g2.enroll(10, 30);  // prorated = 80 * 10/30 = 26.67
    assert(std::abs(g2.total_charged - 56.67) < 0.01);  // 30 + 26.67

    g2.bill_month();
    assert(std::abs(g2.total_charged - 136.67) < 0.01);  // + 80
    assert(g2.months_active == 1);

    g2.add_personal_training();
    g2.bill_month();
    assert(std::abs(g2.total_charged - 266.67) < 0.01);  // + 80 + 50

    g2.freeze();
    g2.bill_month();
    assert(std::abs(g2.total_charged - 286.67) < 0.01);  // + 20 freeze fee
    assert(g2.months_frozen == 1);

    // Cannot double-enroll
    GymMembership g3("m3", 100, 50, 25, 0, 0.0, 40, 1);
    g3.enroll(30, 30);
    try {
        g3.enroll(15, 30);
        assert(false);
    } catch (const std::runtime_error&) {}

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
#include <map>

struct ShipmentDetails {
    double actual_weight_lbs;
    double length, width, height;  // inches for dimensional weight
    int zone;
    bool residential;
    bool signature_required;
    bool saturday_delivery;
};

class ShippingCalculator {
public:
    double dim_factor;          // dimensional weight divisor
    double fuel_surcharge_pct;  // e.g. 0.15 for 15%
    double residential_fee;
    double signature_fee;
    double saturday_multiplier; // e.g. 1.5

    // zone -> vector of (max_weight, rate_per_lb) breakpoints, sorted ascending
    std::map<int, std::vector<std::pair<double, double>>> zone_rates;

    std::vector<std::string> calc_log;

    ShippingCalculator(double dim, double fuel, double res, double sig, double sat)
        : dim_factor(dim), fuel_surcharge_pct(fuel), residential_fee(res),
          signature_fee(sig), saturday_multiplier(sat) {}

    void add_rate(int zone, double max_weight, double rate_per_lb) {
        zone_rates[zone].push_back({max_weight, rate_per_lb});
    }

    double _billable_weight(const ShipmentDetails& s) const {
        double dim_weight = (s.length * s.width * s.height) / dim_factor;
        return std::max(s.actual_weight_lbs, dim_weight);
    }

    double _base_rate(int zone, double weight) const {
        auto it = zone_rates.find(zone);
        if (it == zone_rates.end()) throw std::runtime_error("unknown zone");
        for (auto& [max_w, rate] : it->second) {
            if (weight <= max_w) return weight * rate;
        }
        return weight * it->second.back().second;  // use highest tier rate
    }



GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    double calculate(const ShipmentDetails& s) {
        double weight = _billable_weight(s);
        double base = _base_rate(s.zone, weight);
        double fuel = base * fuel_surcharge_pct;
        double subtotal = base + fuel;
        if (s.residential)
            subtotal += residential_fee;
        if (s.signature_required)
            subtotal += signature_fee;
        if (s.saturday_delivery)
            subtotal *= saturday_multiplier;
        calc_log.push_back("zone:" + std::to_string(s.zone) +
            ",wt:" + std::to_string(weight) +
            ",total:" + std::to_string(subtotal));
        return subtotal;
    }


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
};

int main() {
    // dim_factor=139, fuel=15%, residential=$5, signature=$3, saturday=1.5x
    ShippingCalculator calc(139.0, 0.15, 5.0, 3.0, 1.5);
    calc.add_rate(1, 10, 2.0);   // zone 1: up to 10 lbs -> $2/lb
    calc.add_rate(1, 50, 1.5);   // zone 1: 10-50 lbs -> $1.5/lb
    calc.add_rate(2, 10, 3.0);
    calc.add_rate(2, 50, 2.5);

    // 5 lbs actual, small box -> actual weight dominates
    ShipmentDetails s1 = {5.0, 10, 10, 10, 1, false, false, false};
    double r1 = calc.calculate(s1);
    // billable = max(5, 1000/139=7.19) = 7.19
    // base = 7.19 * 2.0 = 14.39, fuel = 14.39*0.15 = 2.16, total = 16.55
    assert(std::abs(r1 - 16.55) < 0.1);

    // Large box: dimensional weight dominates
    ShipmentDetails s2 = {5.0, 20, 20, 20, 1, true, true, false};
    double r2 = calc.calculate(s2);
    // dim_weight = 8000/139 = 57.55, actual=5 -> billable=57.55
    // base = 57.55 * 1.5 = 86.33 (over 10lb tier), fuel = 86.33*0.15=12.95
    // subtotal = 86.33 + 12.95 + 5 + 3 = 107.28
    assert(std::abs(r2 - 107.28) < 0.5);

    // Saturday delivery multiplier applies to everything
    ShipmentDetails s3 = {5.0, 10, 10, 10, 2, false, false, true};
    double r3 = calc.calculate(s3);
    // billable=7.19, base=7.19*3=21.58, fuel=21.58*0.15=3.24, sub=24.82, *1.5=37.23
    assert(std::abs(r3 - 37.23) < 0.5);

    assert(calc.calc_log.size() == 3);
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // Residential + signature + saturday
    ShipmentDetails s4 = {8.0, 10, 10, 10, 1, true, true, true};
    double r4 = calc.calculate(s4);
    // billable = max(8, 7.19) = 8, base = 8*2 = 16, fuel = 16*0.15 = 2.4
    // sub = 16+2.4+5+3 = 26.4, *1.5 = 39.6
    assert(std::abs(r4 - 39.6) < 0.1);

    // Zone 2, no extras
    ShipmentDetails s5 = {3.0, 5, 5, 5, 2, false, false, false};
    double r5 = calc.calculate(s5);
    // dim = 125/139 = 0.9, billable=3, base=3*3=9, fuel=9*0.15=1.35, total=10.35
    assert(std::abs(r5 - 10.35) < 0.1);

    assert(calc.calc_log.size() == 5);

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>

struct TicketBooking {
    std::string booking_id;
    double ticket_price;
    double service_fee;
    int num_tickets;
    double promo_discount;   // total discount applied
    int loyalty_points_earned;
    int purchase_timestamp;
    int event_timestamp;
    std::string payment_method;  // "credit" or "cash"
};

struct RefundResult {
    double refund_amount;
    double service_fee_retained;
    int loyalty_points_clawed;
    std::string refund_method;   // "original" or "credit"
    std::string refund_tier;     // "full", "partial_75", "partial_50", "none"
};

class RefundProcessor {
public:
    int full_refund_window;     // seconds before event for 100% refund
    int partial75_window;       // seconds before event for 75% refund
    int partial50_window;       // seconds before event for 50% refund

    std::vector<RefundResult> refund_log;

    RefundProcessor(int full_w, int p75_w, int p50_w)
        : full_refund_window(full_w), partial75_window(p75_w),
          partial50_window(p50_w) {}

    double _refund_pct(int request_time, int event_time) const {
        int before = event_time - request_time;
        if (before >= full_refund_window) return 1.0;
        if (before >= partial75_window) return 0.75;
        if (before >= partial50_window) return 0.50;
        return 0.0;
    }



GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    RefundResult process_refund(const TicketBooking& b, int request_time,
                                int tickets_to_refund) {
        if (tickets_to_refund <= 0 || tickets_to_refund > b.num_tickets)
            throw std::runtime_error("invalid refund quantity");
        double pct = _refund_pct(request_time, b.event_timestamp);
        std::string tier = pct == 1.0 ? "full" :
                           pct == 0.75 ? "partial_75" :
                           pct == 0.50 ? "partial_50" : "none";
        double per_ticket = b.ticket_price;
        double proportion = (double)tickets_to_refund / b.num_tickets;
        double promo_reversal = b.promo_discount * proportion;
        double gross = per_ticket * tickets_to_refund - promo_reversal;
        double refund = gross * pct;
        double svc_retained = b.service_fee * proportion;
        int points_clawed = (int)(b.loyalty_points_earned * proportion);
        std::string method = (pct < 1.0) ? "credit" : b.payment_method;
        RefundResult result = {refund, svc_retained, points_clawed, method, tier};
        refund_log.push_back(result);
        return result;
    }


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
};

int main() {
    // full=7200 (2h), 75%=3600 (1h), 50%=1800 (30min)
    RefundProcessor proc(7200, 3600, 1800);

    // Booking: $50/ticket, $10 service fee, 4 tickets, $20 promo, 100 points
    TicketBooking b1 = {"B1", 50.0, 10.0, 4, 20.0, 100, 1000, 10000, "credit"};

    // Full refund (3h before event): refund 2 of 4 tickets
    RefundResult r1 = proc.process_refund(b1, 10000 - 7500, 2);
    // proportion = 0.5, promo_reversal = 10, gross = 100-10 = 90, refund = 90*1.0=90
    assert(r1.refund_tier == "full");
    assert(std::abs(r1.refund_amount - 90.0) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r1.service_fee_retained - 5.0) < 0.01);  // 10 * 0.5
    assert(r1.loyalty_points_clawed == 50);  // 100 * 0.5
    assert(r1.refund_method == "credit");     // original payment

    // 75% refund (1.5h before): all 4 tickets
    RefundResult r2 = proc.process_refund(b1, 10000 - 5000, 4);
    // proportion=1.0, promo_reversal=20, gross=200-20=180, refund=180*0.75=135
    assert(r2.refund_tier == "partial_75");
    assert(std::abs(r2.refund_amount - 135.0) < 0.01);
    assert(r2.refund_method == "credit");  // partial -> always credit

    // No refund (5 min before)
    RefundResult r3 = proc.process_refund(b1, 10000 - 300, 1);
    assert(r3.refund_tier == "none");
    assert(r3.refund_amount == 0.0);
    assert(r3.loyalty_points_clawed == 25);

    assert(proc.refund_log.size() == 3);
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // 50% refund tier
    TicketBooking b2 = {"B2", 80.0, 15.0, 2, 0.0, 200, 5000, 8000, "cash"};
    RefundResult r4 = proc.process_refund(b2, 8000 - 2000, 1);
    // proportion=0.5, promo=0, gross=80, refund=80*0.5=40
    assert(r4.refund_tier == "partial_50");
    assert(std::abs(r4.refund_amount - 40.0) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r4.service_fee_retained - 7.5) < 0.01);
    assert(r4.loyalty_points_clawed == 100);
    assert(r4.refund_method == "credit");  // partial -> credit

    // Full refund with cash payment -> original method
    RefundResult r5 = proc.process_refund(b2, 8000 - 8000, 2);
    assert(r5.refund_tier == "full");
    assert(r5.refund_method == "cash");

    // Invalid quantity
    try {
        proc.process_refund(b2, 5000, 0);
        assert(false);
    } catch (const std::runtime_error&) {}
    try {
        proc.process_refund(b2, 5000, 3);
        assert(false);
    } catch (const std::runtime_error&) {}

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>

struct Ingredient {
    std::string name;
    double quantity;
    std::string unit;  // "cup", "tsp", "tbsp", "oz", "g"
};

struct NutritionInfo {
    double calories;
    double protein_g;
    double fat_g;
};

class Recipe {
public:
    std::string recipe_name;
    int base_servings;
    std::vector<Ingredient> ingredients;
    NutritionInfo base_nutrition;  // per serving

    Recipe(const std::string& name, int servings)
        : recipe_name(name), base_servings(servings) {}

    void add_ingredient(const std::string& name, double qty, const std::string& unit) {
        ingredients.push_back({name, qty, unit});
    }

    void set_nutrition(double cal, double prot, double fat) {
        base_nutrition = {cal, prot, fat};
    }

    double _round_quarter(double val) const {
        return std::round(val * 4.0) / 4.0;
    }

    // cups->tsp = *48, cups->tbsp = *16, tbsp->tsp = *3
    double _to_tsp(double qty, const std::string& unit) const {
        if (unit == "tsp") return qty;
        if (unit == "tbsp") return qty * 3.0;
        if (unit == "cup") return qty * 48.0;
        return qty;  // oz, g stay as-is
    }



GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    std::vector<Ingredient> scale(int target_servings) const {
        double factor = (double)target_servings / base_servings;
        std::vector<Ingredient> scaled;
        for (const auto& ing : ingredients) {
            double new_qty = ing.quantity * factor;
            std::string new_unit = ing.unit;
            if ((ing.unit == "cup" || ing.unit == "tbsp") && new_qty < 1.0) {
                new_qty = _to_tsp(new_qty, ing.unit);
                new_unit = "tsp";
            }
            new_qty = _round_quarter(new_qty);
            scaled.push_back({ing.name, new_qty, new_unit});
        }
        return scaled;
    }

    NutritionInfo scaled_nutrition(int target_servings) const {
        return {base_nutrition.calories * target_servings,
                base_nutrition.protein_g * target_servings,
                base_nutrition.fat_g * target_servings};
    }


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
};

int main() {
    Recipe r("Pancakes", 4);
    r.add_ingredient("flour", 2.0, "cup");
    r.add_ingredient("sugar", 3.0, "tbsp");
    r.add_ingredient("salt", 0.5, "tsp");
    r.add_ingredient("butter", 4.0, "oz");
    r.set_nutrition(250, 8, 12);

    // Scale to 2 servings (factor = 0.5)
    auto s2 = r.scale(2);
    assert(s2[0].name == "flour");
    assert(std::abs(s2[0].quantity - 1.0) < 0.01);  // 2*0.5 = 1 cup
    assert(s2[0].unit == "cup");

    assert(s2[1].name == "sugar");
    // 3 tbsp * 0.5 = 1.5 tbsp (>= 1, stays tbsp)
    assert(std::abs(s2[1].quantity - 1.5) < 0.01);
    assert(s2[1].unit == "tbsp");

    assert(s2[2].name == "salt");
    assert(std::abs(s2[2].quantity - 0.25) < 0.01);  // 0.5*0.5 = 0.25 tsp
    assert(s2[2].unit == "tsp");

    assert(std::abs(s2[3].quantity - 2.0) < 0.01);  // 4*0.5 = 2 oz
    assert(s2[3].unit == "oz");

    // Scale to 1 serving (factor = 0.25)
    auto s1 = r.scale(1);
    // flour: 2*0.25 = 0.5 cup -> < 1 cup -> convert to tsp: 0.5*48=24 tsp
    assert(s1[0].unit == "tsp");
    assert(std::abs(s1[0].quantity - 24.0) < 0.01);

    // sugar: 3*0.25 = 0.75 tbsp -> < 1 -> convert to tsp: 0.75*3 = 2.25 tsp
    assert(s1[1].unit == "tsp");
    assert(std::abs(s1[1].quantity - 2.25) < 0.01);

    auto n = r.scaled_nutrition(2);
    assert(std::abs(n.calories - 500) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(n.protein_g - 16) < 0.01);

    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // Scale to 8 servings (factor = 2)
    auto s8 = r.scale(8);
    assert(std::abs(s8[0].quantity - 4.0) < 0.01);
    assert(s8[0].unit == "cup");
    assert(std::abs(s8[1].quantity - 6.0) < 0.01);
    assert(s8[1].unit == "tbsp");
    assert(std::abs(s8[3].quantity - 8.0) < 0.01);

    // Scale to 3 servings (factor = 0.75)
    auto s3 = r.scale(3);
    // flour: 2*0.75 = 1.5 cup
    assert(std::abs(s3[0].quantity - 1.5) < 0.01);
    assert(s3[0].unit == "cup");
    // sugar: 3*0.75 = 2.25 tbsp
    assert(std::abs(s3[1].quantity - 2.25) < 0.01);
    assert(s3[1].unit == "tbsp");

    auto n8 = r.scaled_nutrition(8);
    assert(std::abs(n8.calories - 2000) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(n8.fat_g - 96) < 0.01);
