#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
#include <numeric>

struct TaxBracket {
    double upper_limit;  // income up to this amount (0 = no limit)
    double rate;
};

class TaxReturn {
public:
    std::vector<double> income_sources;
    double standard_deduction;
    double itemized_deductions;
    std::vector<TaxBracket> brackets;  // sorted by upper_limit ascending
    double child_credit;
    double education_credit;
    double estimated_payments;

    std::string filing_status;  // "refund" or "owed" or "even"
    double final_amount;
    double effective_rate;
    std::vector<std::string> audit_trail;

    TaxReturn(double std_ded)
        : standard_deduction(std_ded), itemized_deductions(0),
          child_credit(0), education_credit(0), estimated_payments(0),
          final_amount(0), effective_rate(0) {}

    void add_income(double amount) { income_sources.push_back(amount); }
    void set_itemized(double amount) { itemized_deductions = amount; }
    void add_bracket(double limit, double rate) { brackets.push_back({limit, rate}); }
    void set_credits(double child, double education) {
        child_credit = child;
        education_credit = education;
    }
    void set_estimated_payments(double amount) { estimated_payments = amount; }

    double _gross_income() const {
        return std::accumulate(income_sources.begin(), income_sources.end(), 0.0);
    }



GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    void file() {
        double gross = _gross_income();
        double deduction = std::max(standard_deduction, itemized_deductions);
        std::string ded_type = (itemized_deductions > standard_deduction) ? "itemized" : "standard";
        double taxable = std::max(0.0, gross - deduction);
        audit_trail.push_back("gross:" + std::to_string(gross));
        audit_trail.push_back("deduction:" + ded_type + ":" + std::to_string(deduction));
        audit_trail.push_back("taxable:" + std::to_string(taxable));

        double tax = 0.0;
        double prev_limit = 0.0;
        for (const auto& b : brackets) {
            double bracket_income;
            if (b.upper_limit == 0) {
                bracket_income = std::max(0.0, taxable - prev_limit);
            } else {
                bracket_income = std::max(0.0, std::min(taxable, b.upper_limit) - prev_limit);
            }
            tax += bracket_income * b.rate;
            prev_limit = b.upper_limit;
        }
        double credits = child_credit + education_credit;
        double after_credits = std::max(0.0, tax - credits);
        double balance = after_credits - estimated_payments;
        filing_status = (balance > 0.01) ? "owed" : (balance < -0.01) ? "refund" : "even";
        final_amount = std::abs(balance);
        effective_rate = (gross > 0) ? (after_credits / gross) * 100.0 : 0.0;
        audit_trail.push_back("tax:" + std::to_string(tax));
        audit_trail.push_back("status:" + filing_status);
    }


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
};

int main() {
    TaxReturn tr(13850);
    tr.add_income(50000);  // salary
    tr.add_income(5000);   // freelance
    tr.add_income(2000);   // interest
    // gross = 57000
    tr.set_itemized(10000);  // less than standard, so standard wins

    tr.add_bracket(11000, 0.10);
    tr.add_bracket(44725, 0.12);
    tr.add_bracket(95375, 0.22);
    tr.add_bracket(0, 0.24);  // 0 = unlimited

    tr.set_credits(2000, 500);
    tr.set_estimated_payments(5000);

    tr.file();
    // taxable = 57000 - 13850 = 43150
    // tax: 11000*0.10=1100, (43150-11000)*0.12=3858, total=4958
    // after credits: 4958-2500=2458
    // balance: 2458-5000 = -2542 -> refund
    assert(tr.filing_status == "refund");
    assert(std::abs(tr.final_amount - 2542.0) < 1.0);
    assert(tr.audit_trail.size() == 5);

    // Itemized wins
    TaxReturn tr2(13850);
    tr2.add_income(100000);
    tr2.set_itemized(25000);
    tr2.add_bracket(11000, 0.10);
    tr2.add_bracket(44725, 0.12);
    tr2.add_bracket(95375, 0.22);
    tr2.add_bracket(0, 0.24);
    tr2.set_credits(0, 0);
    tr2.set_estimated_payments(0);
    tr2.file();
    // taxable = 100000-25000=75000
    // tax: 11000*0.10=1100, 33725*0.12=4047, 30275*0.22=6660.5, total=11807.5
    assert(tr2.filing_status == "owed");
    assert(std::abs(tr2.final_amount - 11807.5) < 1.0);

    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // Exact even case
    TaxReturn tr3(10000);
    tr3.add_income(21000);
    tr3.add_bracket(11000, 0.10);
    tr3.add_bracket(0, 0.12);
    tr3.set_credits(0, 0);
    // taxable = 11000, tax = 11000*0.10 = 1100
    tr3.set_estimated_payments(1100);
    tr3.file();
    assert(tr3.filing_status == "even");
    assert(std::abs(tr3.final_amount) < 0.1);

    // Credits cannot make tax negative
    TaxReturn tr4(0);
    tr4.add_income(5000);
    tr4.add_bracket(0, 0.10);
    tr4.set_credits(1000, 0);
    tr4.set_estimated_payments(0);
    tr4.file();
    // tax=500, after credits=max(0,500-1000)=0
    assert(tr4.filing_status == "even");
    assert(std::abs(tr4.final_amount) < 0.1);

    // Effective rate calculation
    assert(tr4.effective_rate < 0.01);  // 0/5000 = 0%

Test Case #27

================================================================================
Test Case ID: 27
Source: devbench-code-purpose-understanding
Language: cpp

PREFIX CODE:

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>

struct Attendee {
    std::string name;
    std::string dietary;  // "none", "vegetarian", "vegan", "halal"
    std::vector<std::string> workshops;
};

struct RegResult {
    double total_cost;
    bool on_waitlist;
    int attendees_registered;
    int attendees_waitlisted;
};

class ConferenceReg {
public:
    double base_price;
    double early_bird_price;
    int early_bird_cutoff;  // timestamp
    double workshop_price;  // per workshop per person
    int capacity;
    int registered_count;

    // group discount tiers: (min_size, discount_pct)
    std::vector<std::pair<int, double>> group_tiers;  // sorted ascending by min_size

    std::vector<Attendee> attendees;
    std::vector<Attendee> waitlist;
    std::vector<std::string> reg_log;

    ConferenceReg(double base, double early, int cutoff, double ws_price, int cap)
        : base_price(base), early_bird_price(early), early_bird_cutoff(cutoff),
          workshop_price(ws_price), capacity(cap), registered_count(0) {}

    void add_group_tier(int min_size, double discount) {
        group_tiers.push_back({min_size, discount});
    }

    double _group_discount(int group_size) const {
        double disc = 0.0;
        for (const auto& [min_sz, d] : group_tiers) {
            if (group_size >= min_sz) disc = d;
        }
        return disc;
    }



GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    RegResult register_group(const std::vector<Attendee>& group, int timestamp) {
        double unit_price = (timestamp <= early_bird_cutoff) ? early_bird_price : base_price;
        double discount = _group_discount(group.size());
        double discounted_price = unit_price * (1.0 - discount);

        int spots = capacity - registered_count;
        int to_register = std::min((int)group.size(), spots);
        int to_waitlist = (int)group.size() - to_register;

        double total = 0.0;
        for (int i = 0; i < (int)group.size(); i++) {
            double person_cost = discounted_price + group[i].workshops.size() * workshop_price;
            total += person_cost;
            if (i < to_register) {
                attendees.push_back(group[i]);
                registered_count++;
            } else {
                waitlist.push_back(group[i]);
            }
        }
        reg_log.push_back("group:" + std::to_string(group.size()) +
                          ",cost:" + std::to_string(total));
        return {total, to_waitlist > 0, to_register, to_waitlist};
    }


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
};

int main() {
    // base=$500, early=$350, cutoff=1000, workshop=$75, capacity=5
    ConferenceReg conf(500, 350, 1000, 75, 5);
    conf.add_group_tier(3, 0.10);  // 3+ people: 10% off base
    conf.add_group_tier(5, 0.20);  // 5+ people: 20% off base

    // Early bird, group of 3 -> 10% off $350 = $315/person
    std::vector<Attendee> g1 = {
        {"Alice", "none", {"ML"}},
        {"Bob", "vegetarian", {"ML", "NLP"}},
        {"Carol", "vegan", {}}
    };
    RegResult r1 = conf.register_group(g1, 500);
    // Alice: 315+75=390, Bob: 315+150=465, Carol: 315+0=315, total=1170
    assert(std::abs(r1.total_cost - 1170.0) < 0.01);
    assert(!r1.on_waitlist);
    assert(r1.attendees_registered == 3);
    assert(conf.registered_count == 3);
    assert(conf.attendees.size() == 3);

    // After cutoff, group of 3 -> 10% off $500 = $450/person, only 2 spots left
    std::vector<Attendee> g2 = {
        {"Dave", "halal", {"CV"}},
        {"Eve", "none", {}},
        {"Frank", "none", {"ML"}}
    };
    RegResult r2 = conf.register_group(g2, 1500);
    // Dave: 450+75=525, Eve: 450=450, Frank: 450+75=525, total=1500
    assert(std::abs(r2.total_cost - 1500.0) < 0.01);
    assert(r2.on_waitlist);
    assert(r2.attendees_registered == 2);
    assert(r2.attendees_waitlisted == 1);
    assert(conf.waitlist.size() == 1);
    assert(conf.waitlist[0].name == "Frank");

    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // All waitlisted (capacity full)
    std::vector<Attendee> g3 = {{"Grace", "none", {}}};
    RegResult r3 = conf.register_group(g3, 2000);
    assert(r3.on_waitlist);
    assert(r3.attendees_registered == 0);
    assert(r3.attendees_waitlisted == 1);
    assert(conf.waitlist.size() == 2);

    // Fresh conference: group of 5 -> 20% off
    ConferenceReg conf2(400, 300, 500, 50, 10);
    conf2.add_group_tier(3, 0.10);
    conf2.add_group_tier(5, 0.15);
    std::vector<Attendee> g4 = {
        {"A", "none", {}}, {"B", "none", {"W1"}},
        {"C", "none", {}}, {"D", "none", {"W1","W2"}},
        {"E", "none", {}}
    };
    RegResult r4 = conf2.register_group(g4, 100);  // early bird
    // 15% off $300 = $255/person, workshops: 0+50+0+100+0 = 150
    // total = 255*5 + 150 = 1275+150 = 1425
    assert(std::abs(r4.total_cost - 1425.0) < 0.01);
    assert(!r4.on_waitlist);

Test Case #28

================================================================================
Test Case ID: 28
Source: devbench-code-purpose-understanding
Language: cpp

PREFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <set>
#include <map>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>

struct FillResult {
    bool approved;
    std::string denial_reason;  // empty if approved
    int qty_dispensed;
    double copay;
    int next_refill_day;  // day number when refill is allowed
};

class Pharmacy {
public:
    // drug -> set of drugs it interacts with
    std::map<std::string, std::set<std::string>> interactions;
    // drug -> formulary tier (1=generic $10, 2=preferred $25, 3=specialty $50)
    std::map<std::string, int> formulary;
    // drug -> max quantity per fill
    std::map<std::string, int> qty_limits;
    // drugs requiring prior authorization
    std::set<std::string> prior_auth_required;
    // approved prior auths: drug -> true
    std::set<std::string> prior_auth_approved;
    // patient's current medications
    std::set<std::string> current_meds;

    std::vector<FillResult> fill_log;
    double copay_tiers[4] = {0, 10.0, 25.0, 50.0};
    int supply_days = 30;  // standard days supply

    Pharmacy() = default;

    void add_interaction(const std::string& a, const std::string& b) {
        interactions[a].insert(b);
        interactions[b].insert(a);
    }

    void set_formulary(const std::string& drug, int tier) { formulary[drug] = tier; }
    void set_qty_limit(const std::string& drug, int max_qty) { qty_limits[drug] = max_qty; }
    void require_prior_auth(const std::string& drug) { prior_auth_required.insert(drug); }
    void approve_prior_auth(const std::string& drug) { prior_auth_approved.insert(drug); }
    void add_current_med(const std::string& drug) { current_meds.insert(drug); }



GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    FillResult fill_prescription(const std::string& drug, int requested_qty,
                                  int current_day) {
        // 1. Check drug interactions
        if (interactions.count(drug)) {
            for (const auto& med : current_meds) {
                if (interactions[drug].count(med)) {
                    FillResult r = {false, "interaction:" + med, 0, 0, 0};
                    fill_log.push_back(r);
                    return r;
                }
            }
        }
        // 2. Check prior authorization
        if (prior_auth_required.count(drug) && !prior_auth_approved.count(drug)) {
            FillResult r = {false, "prior_auth_needed", 0, 0, 0};
            fill_log.push_back(r);
            return r;
        }
        // 3. Determine quantity (apply limit)
        int qty = requested_qty;
        if (qty_limits.count(drug))
            qty = std::min(qty, qty_limits[drug]);
        // 4. Determine copay from formulary
        int tier = formulary.count(drug) ? formulary[drug] : 3;
        double copay = copay_tiers[tier];
        // 5. Calculate next refill
        int next_refill = current_day + supply_days;
        current_meds.insert(drug);
        FillResult r = {true, "", qty, copay, next_refill};
        fill_log.push_back(r);
        return r;
    }


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
};

int main() {
    Pharmacy pharm;
    pharm.set_formulary("metformin", 1);   // generic $10
    pharm.set_formulary("lisinopril", 2);  // preferred $25
    pharm.set_formulary("humira", 3);      // specialty $50
    pharm.set_qty_limit("metformin", 90);
    pharm.set_qty_limit("humira", 2);
    pharm.require_prior_auth("humira");
    pharm.add_interaction("warfarin", "aspirin");
    pharm.add_current_med("warfarin");

    // Simple generic fill
    FillResult r1 = pharm.fill_prescription("metformin", 90, 1);
    assert(r1.approved);
    assert(r1.qty_dispensed == 90);
    assert(std::abs(r1.copay - 10.0) < 0.01);
    assert(r1.next_refill_day == 31);

    // Drug interaction: aspirin interacts with warfarin
    FillResult r2 = pharm.fill_prescription("aspirin", 30, 1);
    assert(!r2.approved);
    assert(r2.denial_reason == "interaction:warfarin");

    // Prior auth not yet approved
    FillResult r3 = pharm.fill_prescription("humira", 4, 1);
    assert(!r3.approved);
    assert(r3.denial_reason == "prior_auth_needed");

    // Approve and retry -> quantity limited to 2
    pharm.approve_prior_auth("humira");
    FillResult r4 = pharm.fill_prescription("humira", 4, 10);
    assert(r4.approved);
    assert(r4.qty_dispensed == 2);
    assert(std::abs(r4.copay - 50.0) < 0.01);
    assert(r4.next_refill_day == 40);

    assert(pharm.fill_log.size() == 4);
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // Unknown drug defaults to tier 3
    FillResult r5 = pharm.fill_prescription("newdrug", 10, 50);
    assert(r5.approved);
    assert(std::abs(r5.copay - 50.0) < 0.01);
    assert(r5.qty_dispensed == 10);  // no qty limit for unknown drug
    assert(r5.next_refill_day == 80);

    // Preferred brand
    FillResult r6 = pharm.fill_prescription("lisinopril", 30, 100);
    assert(r6.approved);
    assert(std::abs(r6.copay - 25.0) < 0.01);

    // Filling adds to current_meds, so interaction check works in future
    pharm.add_interaction("lisinopril", "potassium");
    FillResult r7 = pharm.fill_prescription("potassium", 30, 100);
    assert(!r7.approved);
    assert(r7.denial_reason == "interaction:lisinopril");

    assert(pharm.fill_log.size() == 7);

Test Case #29
================================================================================

================================================================================
Test Case ID: 29
Source: devbench-code-purpose-understanding
Language: cpp
================================================================================

PREFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>

struct MaterialLine {
    std::string name;
    double unit_cost;
    double quantity;
    double waste_factor;  // e.g. 0.10 = 10% waste
};

struct LaborLine {
    std::string trade;      // e.g. "electrician", "plumber"
    double hourly_rate;
    double hours;
    double efficiency;      // 0.0-1.0, actual productive fraction
};

struct Estimate {
    double material_cost;
    double labor_cost;
    double permit_fee;
    double subtotal;
    double markup_amount;
    double contingency_amount;
    double grand_total;
    std::vector<double> payment_schedule;  // 3 payments: 50%, 40%, 10%
};

class ConstructionEstimator {
public:
    std::vector<MaterialLine> materials;
    std::vector<LaborLine> labor;
    double permit_pct;       // % of material cost
    double markup_pct;       // contractor markup on subtotal
    double contingency_pct;  // on grand total before contingency

    ConstructionEstimator(double permit, double markup, double contingency)
        : permit_pct(permit), markup_pct(markup), contingency_pct(contingency) {}

    void add_material(const std::string& name, double cost, double qty, double waste) {
        materials.push_back({name, cost, qty, waste});
    }

    void add_labor(const std::string& trade, double rate, double hours, double eff) {
        labor.push_back({trade, rate, hours, eff});
    }



GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    Estimate generate() const {
        double mat_cost = 0;
        for (const auto& m : materials)
            mat_cost += m.unit_cost * m.quantity * (1.0 + m.waste_factor);

        double lab_cost = 0;
        for (const auto& l : labor)
            lab_cost += l.hourly_rate * (l.hours / l.efficiency);

        double permit = mat_cost * permit_pct;
        double subtotal = mat_cost + lab_cost + permit;
        double markup = subtotal * markup_pct;
        double before_contingency = subtotal + markup;
        double contingency = before_contingency * contingency_pct;
        double grand = before_contingency + contingency;

        std::vector<double> schedule = {grand * 0.50, grand * 0.40, grand * 0.10};
        return {mat_cost, lab_cost, permit, subtotal, markup, contingency, grand, schedule};
    }


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
};

int main() {
    ConstructionEstimator est(0.05, 0.15, 0.10);

    est.add_material("lumber", 5.0, 100, 0.10);    // 5*100*1.1 = 550
    est.add_material("concrete", 120.0, 3, 0.05);   // 120*3*1.05 = 378

    est.add_labor("carpenter", 45.0, 40, 0.80);     // 45*(40/0.8) = 2250
    est.add_labor("electrician", 65.0, 16, 1.0);    // 65*16 = 1040

    Estimate e = est.generate();

    assert(std::abs(e.material_cost - 928.0) < 0.01);    // 550+378
    assert(std::abs(e.labor_cost - 3290.0) < 0.01);      // 2250+1040
    assert(std::abs(e.permit_fee - 46.4) < 0.01);        // 928*0.05
    // subtotal = 928+3290+46.4 = 4264.4
    assert(std::abs(e.subtotal - 4264.4) < 0.1);
    // markup = 4264.4*0.15 = 639.66
    assert(std::abs(e.markup_amount - 639.66) < 0.1);
    // before_contingency = 4904.06
    // contingency = 490.41
    double expected_grand = 4264.4 + 639.66 + 490.406;
    assert(std::abs(e.grand_total - expected_grand) < 1.0);

    // Payment schedule sums to grand total
    double sum = e.payment_schedule[0] + e.payment_schedule[1] + e.payment_schedule[2];
    assert(std::abs(sum - e.grand_total) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(e.payment_schedule[0] - e.grand_total * 0.5) < 0.01);

    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // Minimal estimate
    ConstructionEstimator est2(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    est2.add_material("paint", 30.0, 5, 0.0);  // 150
    est2.add_labor("painter", 25.0, 8, 1.0);   // 200
    Estimate e2 = est2.generate();
    assert(std::abs(e2.material_cost - 150.0) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(e2.labor_cost - 200.0) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(e2.permit_fee) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(e2.grand_total - 350.0) < 0.01);

    // High waste + low efficiency
    ConstructionEstimator est3(0.10, 0.20, 0.05);
    est3.add_material("steel", 200.0, 10, 0.25);  // 200*10*1.25 = 2500
    est3.add_labor("welder", 80.0, 20, 0.5);      // 80*(20/0.5) = 3200
    Estimate e3 = est3.generate();
    assert(std::abs(e3.material_cost - 2500.0) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(e3.labor_cost - 3200.0) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(e3.permit_fee - 250.0) < 0.01);  // 2500*0.10
    // subtotal=5950, markup=1190, before_cont=7140, cont=357, grand=7497
    assert(std::abs(e3.grand_total - 7497.0) < 1.0);

Test Case #30
================================================================================

================================================================================
Test Case ID: 30
Source: devbench-code-purpose-understanding
Language: cpp
================================================================================

PREFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>

struct RentalAgreement {
    std::string rental_id;
    double daily_rate;
    int rental_days;
    int miles_included;      // free mileage allowance
    double per_mile_overage;
    double fuel_tank_size;   // gallons
    double fuel_price_per_gal;
    double insurance_deductible;
    double hourly_late_fee;  // for first 4 hours
    double daily_late_fee;   // per day after 4 hours
    int loyalty_points_per_dollar;  // earned on base rental only
};

struct ReturnDetails {
    int miles_driven;
    double fuel_level;    // 0.0 to 1.0 (fraction of tank)
    int hours_late;       // 0 = on time
    double damage_amount; // 0 = no damage
};

struct Invoice {
    double base_rental;
    double mileage_charge;
    double fuel_penalty;
    double late_surcharge;
    double damage_charge;
    double total;
    int loyalty_points;
    std::vector<std::string> line_items;
};

class RentalReturn {
public:
    std::vector<Invoice> invoices;



GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    Invoice process_return(const RentalAgreement& a, const ReturnDetails& d) {
        Invoice inv;
        inv.base_rental = a.daily_rate * a.rental_days;
        inv.line_items.push_back("base:" + std::to_string(inv.base_rental));

        int overage_miles = std::max(0, d.miles_driven - a.miles_included);
        inv.mileage_charge = overage_miles * a.per_mile_overage;
        if (inv.mileage_charge > 0)
            inv.line_items.push_back("mileage:" + std::to_string(inv.mileage_charge));

        double missing_fuel = (1.0 - d.fuel_level) * a.fuel_tank_size;
        inv.fuel_penalty = missing_fuel * a.fuel_price_per_gal;
        if (inv.fuel_penalty > 0.01)
            inv.line_items.push_back("fuel:" + std::to_string(inv.fuel_penalty));

        inv.late_surcharge = 0;
        if (d.hours_late > 0) {
            int hourly_hours = std::min(d.hours_late, 4);
            int late_days = (d.hours_late > 4) ? (d.hours_late - 4 + 23) / 24 : 0;
            inv.late_surcharge = hourly_hours * a.hourly_late_fee + late_days * a.daily_late_fee;
            inv.line_items.push_back("late:" + std::to_string(inv.late_surcharge));
        }

        inv.damage_charge = std::max(0.0, d.damage_amount - a.insurance_deductible);
        if (inv.damage_charge > 0)
            inv.line_items.push_back("damage:" + std::to_string(inv.damage_charge));

        inv.total = inv.base_rental + inv.mileage_charge + inv.fuel_penalty +
                    inv.late_surcharge + inv.damage_charge;
        inv.loyalty_points = (int)(inv.base_rental * a.loyalty_points_per_dollar);
        inv.line_items.push_back("total:" + std::to_string(inv.total));
        invoices.push_back(inv);
        return inv;
    }


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
};

int main() {
    RentalReturn rr;
    RentalAgreement agr = {"R1", 50.0, 3, 300, 0.25, 15.0, 4.50, 500.0, 15.0, 75.0, 10};

    // On time, full tank, no damage, within mileage
    ReturnDetails d1 = {200, 1.0, 0, 0.0};
    Invoice i1 = rr.process_return(agr, d1);
    assert(std::abs(i1.base_rental - 150.0) < 0.01);
    assert(i1.mileage_charge == 0);
    assert(i1.fuel_penalty < 0.01);
    assert(i1.late_surcharge == 0);
    assert(i1.damage_charge == 0);
    assert(std::abs(i1.total - 150.0) < 0.01);
    assert(i1.loyalty_points == 1500);

    // Over mileage, half tank, 3 hours late, damage under deductible
    ReturnDetails d2 = {450, 0.5, 3, 300.0};
    Invoice i2 = rr.process_return(agr, d2);
    assert(std::abs(i2.mileage_charge - 37.5) < 0.01);   // 150 * 0.25
    assert(std::abs(i2.fuel_penalty - 33.75) < 0.01);     // 0.5*15*4.50
    assert(std::abs(i2.late_surcharge - 45.0) < 0.01);    // 3*15
    assert(i2.damage_charge == 0);  // 300 < 500 deductible
    // total = 150+37.5+33.75+45+0 = 266.25
    assert(std::abs(i2.total - 266.25) < 0.01);
    assert(i2.loyalty_points == 1500);  // only on base rental

    // Heavy damage, very late (28 hours = 4hr hourly + 1 day)
    ReturnDetails d3 = {100, 0.75, 28, 1200.0};
    Invoice i3 = rr.process_return(agr, d3);
    assert(std::abs(i3.damage_charge - 700.0) < 0.01);  // 1200-500
    // late: 4*15 + ceil(24/24)*75 = 60+75 = 135
    assert(std::abs(i3.late_surcharge - 135.0) < 0.01);

    assert(rr.invoices.size() == 3);
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // Exactly at mileage limit
    ReturnDetails d4 = {300, 1.0, 0, 0.0};
    Invoice i4 = rr.process_return(agr, d4);
    assert(i4.mileage_charge == 0);
    assert(std::abs(i4.total - 150.0) < 0.01);

    // Exactly at deductible
    ReturnDetails d5 = {200, 1.0, 0, 500.0};
    Invoice i5 = rr.process_return(agr, d5);
    assert(i5.damage_charge == 0);

    // 5 hours late: 4*hourly + ceil(1/24)*daily = 60+75 = 135
    ReturnDetails d6 = {200, 1.0, 5, 0.0};
    Invoice i6 = rr.process_return(agr, d6);
    assert(std::abs(i6.late_surcharge - 135.0) < 0.01);

    // 29 hours late: 4*15 + ceil(25/24)*75 = 60+150 = 210
    ReturnDetails d7 = {200, 1.0, 29, 0.0};
    Invoice i7 = rr.process_return(agr, d7);
    assert(std::abs(i7.late_surcharge - 210.0) < 0.01);

    // Fuel: completely empty
    ReturnDetails d8 = {200, 0.0, 0, 0.0};
    Invoice i8 = rr.process_return(agr, d8);
    assert(std::abs(i8.fuel_penalty - 67.5) < 0.01);  // 15*4.50

    assert(rr.invoices.size() == 8);

PREFIX CODE:

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>

struct TransferResult {
    std::string status;   // OK, DENIED, LIMIT, FROZEN, UNKNOWN_RECIPIENT
    double fee;
    double converted;     // amount in recipient currency after exchange
    double senderBalance; // sender balance after deduction
    bool complianceFlag;
};

struct Account {
    double balance;
    bool frozen;
    double dailySent;
    double dailyLimit;
    std::string currency;
};

static std::unordered_map<std::string, Account> accounts;
static std::unordered_set<std::string> verifiedRecipients;
static std::vector<std::string> auditLog;
static std::unordered_set<std::string> processedIds;

// exchange rates TO USD (multiply to get USD)
static std::unordered_map<std::string, double> toUsd = {
    {"USD", 1.0}, {"EUR", 1.08}, {"GBP", 1.27}, {"JPY", 0.0067}
};

static void reset() {
    accounts.clear(); verifiedRecipients.clear();
    auditLog.clear(); processedIds.clear();
}

static void addAccount(const std::string& id, double bal, bool frozen,
                       double dailySent, double dailyLimit, const std::string& cur) {
    accounts[id] = {bal, frozen, dailySent, dailyLimit, cur};
}

static void verifyRecipient(const std::string& id) { verifiedRecipients.insert(id); }

// Execute a wire transfer.
// Rules:
//  1. txId is idempotent — if already processed, return {"DUPLICATE",0,0,0,false}
//     before any mutation.
//  2. Sender must exist and not be frozen; otherwise FROZEN.
//  3. Recipient must exist AND be in verifiedRecipients; otherwise UNKNOWN_RECIPIENT.
//  4. Fee is 1.5% of amount for same-currency, 2.5% for cross-currency, minimum 5.0.
//  5. Total deduction = amount + fee.  If sender.dailySent + amount > dailyLimit → LIMIT
//     (do NOT include fee in daily-limit check).
//  6. If sender.balance < totalDeduction → DENIED.
//  7. converted = amount * (senderToUsd / recipientToUsd).
//  8. complianceFlag = true when amount * senderToUsd >= 10000.
//  9. Deduct totalDeduction from sender, add converted to recipient, update dailySent,
//     push audit log "TX:<txId>:<amount>".
static TransferResult transfer(const std::string& txId,
                               const std::string& senderId,
                               const std::string& recipientId,
                               double amount) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    if (processedIds.count(txId)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, 0, false};
    if (!accounts.count(senderId) || accounts[senderId].frozen)
        return {"FROZEN", 0, 0, 0, false};
    if (!accounts.count(recipientId) || !verifiedRecipients.count(recipientId))
        return {"UNKNOWN_RECIPIENT", 0, 0, 0, false};
    auto& s = accounts[senderId];
    auto& r = accounts[recipientId];
    bool crossCur = s.currency != r.currency;
    double feeRate = crossCur ? 0.025 : 0.015;
    double fee = std::max(amount * feeRate, 5.0);
    if (s.dailySent + amount > s.dailyLimit) return {"LIMIT", 0, 0, 0, false};
    double total = amount + fee;
    if (s.balance < total) return {"DENIED", 0, 0, 0, false};
    double sToUsd = toUsd[s.currency], rToUsd = toUsd[r.currency];
    double converted = amount * (sToUsd / rToUsd);
    bool flag = (amount * sToUsd) >= 10000.0;
    s.balance -= total;
    s.dailySent += amount;
    r.balance += converted;
    processedIds.insert(txId);
    auditLog.push_back("TX:" + txId + ":" + std::to_string(amount));
    return {"OK", fee, converted, s.balance, flag};


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
}

int main() {
    reset();
    addAccount("S1", 50000, false, 0, 20000, "USD");
    addAccount("R1", 1000, false, 0, 50000, "EUR");
    verifyRecipient("R1");
    auto res = transfer("T1", "S1", "R1", 10000);
    assert(res.status == "OK");
    assert(std::abs(res.fee - 250.0) < 0.01);           // 2.5% cross-currency
    assert(std::abs(res.converted - 10000.0*(1.0/1.08)) < 0.01);
    assert(res.complianceFlag == true);                  // >= 10000 USD
    assert(std::abs(accounts["S1"].balance - (50000 - 10250)) < 0.01);
    assert(auditLog.size() == 1);
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // idempotent duplicate
    reset();
    addAccount("S1", 50000, false, 0, 20000, "USD");
    addAccount("R1", 1000, false, 0, 50000, "EUR");
    verifyRecipient("R1");
    transfer("T1", "S1", "R1", 5000);
    double balAfter1 = accounts["S1"].balance;
    auto dup = transfer("T1", "S1", "R1", 5000);
    assert(dup.status == "DUPLICATE");
    assert(accounts["S1"].balance == balAfter1);  // no double deduction

    // frozen sender
    reset();
    addAccount("S2", 10000, true, 0, 50000, "USD");
    addAccount("R2", 0, false, 0, 50000, "USD");
    verifyRecipient("R2");
    auto fr = transfer("T2", "S2", "R2", 100);
    assert(fr.status == "FROZEN");

    // unverified recipient
    reset();
    addAccount("S3", 10000, false, 0, 50000, "USD");
    addAccount("R3", 0, false, 0, 50000, "USD");
    auto uv = transfer("T3", "S3", "R3", 100);
    assert(uv.status == "UNKNOWN_RECIPIENT");

    // daily limit exceeded
    reset();
    addAccount("S4", 100000, false, 9500, 10000, "USD");
    addAccount("R4", 0, false, 0, 50000, "USD");
    verifyRecipient("R4");
    auto lim = transfer("T4", "S4", "R4", 600);
    assert(lim.status == "LIMIT");

    // same-currency fee = 1.5%, minimum 5
    reset();
    addAccount("S5", 10000, false, 0, 50000, "USD");
    addAccount("R5", 0, false, 0, 50000, "USD");
    verifyRecipient("R5");
    auto sc = transfer("T5", "S5", "R5", 100);
    assert(sc.status == "OK");
    assert(std::abs(sc.fee - 5.0) < 0.01);  // 1.5% of 100 = 1.5, minimum 5

    // compliance flag false when < 10000 USD
    assert(sc.complianceFlag == false);

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>

struct ClaimResult {
    std::string status;     // APPROVED, DENIED, DUPLICATE, NO_COVERAGE
    double patientOwes;
    double planPays;
    double deductibleUsed;
    bool subrogation;
};

static double annualDeductible = 500.0;
static double ytdDeductible = 0.0;
static double coinsuranceRate = 0.20;   // patient pays 20% after deductible
static double oopMax = 2000.0;
static double ytdOop = 0.0;

static std::unordered_set<std::string> coveredCodes;
static std::unordered_set<std::string> authRequired;
static std::unordered_set<std::string> authorizedClaims;
static std::unordered_set<std::string> processedClaims;
static std::vector<std::string> ledger;
static bool subrogationTriggered = false;

static void reset() {
    ytdDeductible = 0; ytdOop = 0;
    coveredCodes.clear(); authRequired.clear();
    authorizedClaims.clear(); processedClaims.clear();
    ledger.clear(); subrogationTriggered = false;
}

static void addCoverage(const std::string& code) { coveredCodes.insert(code); }
static void requireAuth(const std::string& code) { authRequired.insert(code); }
static void authorize(const std::string& claimId) { authorizedClaims.insert(claimId); }

// Adjudicate an insurance claim.
// Rules:
//  1. claimId idempotent: duplicate returns {"DUPLICATE",0,0,0,false} with no mutation.
//  2. Code must be in coveredCodes; otherwise NO_COVERAGE.
//  3. If code is in authRequired, claimId must be in authorizedClaims; otherwise DENIED.
//  4. If thirdPartyFault is true, set subrogationTriggered = true.
//  5. Patient responsibility: first apply remaining deductible, then coinsuranceRate on rest.
//  6. OOP max caps patient total: if ytdOop + patientOwes > oopMax, clamp patientOwes
//     and plan absorbs the difference.
//  7. Update ytdDeductible, ytdOop, push ledger "CLAIM:<id>:<patientOwes>", mark processed.
static ClaimResult adjudicate(const std::string& claimId,
                              const std::string& code,
                              double billed,
                              bool thirdPartyFault) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    if (processedClaims.count(claimId)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, 0, false};
    if (!coveredCodes.count(code)) return {"NO_COVERAGE", 0, 0, 0, false};
    if (authRequired.count(code) && !authorizedClaims.count(claimId))
        return {"DENIED", 0, 0, 0, false};
    if (thirdPartyFault) subrogationTriggered = true;
    double remainDed = annualDeductible - ytdDeductible;
    double dedUsed = std::min(billed, std::max(remainDed, 0.0));
    double afterDed = billed - dedUsed;
    double coins = afterDed * coinsuranceRate;
    double patient = dedUsed + coins;
    double cap = oopMax - ytdOop;
    if (patient > cap) patient = std::max(cap, 0.0);
    double plan = billed - patient;
    ytdDeductible += dedUsed;
    ytdOop += patient;
    processedClaims.insert(claimId);
    ledger.push_back("CLAIM:" + claimId + ":" + std::to_string(patient));
    return {"APPROVED", patient, plan, dedUsed, subrogationTriggered};


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
}

int main() {
    reset();
    addCoverage("XRAY");
    addCoverage("SURGERY");
    requireAuth("SURGERY");
    authorize("C2");

    auto r1 = adjudicate("C1", "XRAY", 600.0, false);
    assert(r1.status == "APPROVED");
    assert(std::abs(r1.deductibleUsed - 500.0) < 0.01);
    // patient = 500 deductible + 20% of 100 = 520
    assert(std::abs(r1.patientOwes - 520.0) < 0.01);
    assert(r1.subrogation == false);

    auto r2 = adjudicate("C2", "SURGERY", 8000.0, true);
    assert(r2.status == "APPROVED");
    assert(r2.subrogation == true);    // thirdPartyFault
    assert(std::abs(r2.deductibleUsed - 0.0) < 0.01); // deductible exhausted
    // patient = 20% of 8000 = 1600, but oop cap: 2000-520 = 1480
    assert(std::abs(r2.patientOwes - 1480.0) < 0.01);

    assert(ledger.size() == 2);
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // duplicate idempotency
    reset();
    addCoverage("XRAY");
    adjudicate("C1", "XRAY", 100, false);
    double savedOop = ytdOop;
    auto d = adjudicate("C1", "XRAY", 100, false);
    assert(d.status == "DUPLICATE");
    assert(ytdOop == savedOop);

    // uncovered code
    reset();
    auto nc = adjudicate("C3", "MRI", 500, false);
    assert(nc.status == "NO_COVERAGE");
    assert(processedClaims.empty());

    // unauthorized surgery
    reset();
    addCoverage("SURGERY");
    requireAuth("SURGERY");
    auto ua = adjudicate("C4", "SURGERY", 500, false);
    assert(ua.status == "DENIED");
    assert(ytdDeductible == 0);

    // OOP max reached — patient pays nothing extra
    reset();
    addCoverage("XRAY");
    adjudicate("C5", "XRAY", 10000, false); // exhausts oop
    auto over = adjudicate("C6", "XRAY", 5000, false);
    assert(over.status == "APPROVED");
    assert(std::abs(over.patientOwes - 0.0) < 0.01); // oop maxed

    // subrogation persists across claims
    reset();
    addCoverage("XRAY");
    adjudicate("C7", "XRAY", 100, true);
    auto r = adjudicate("C8", "XRAY", 100, false);
    assert(r.subrogation == true); // still true from C7

PREFIX CODE:

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>

struct ConvertResult {
    std::string status;  // OK, TRIAL_ACTIVE, EXPIRED, NO_PAYMENT, ALREADY_PAID
    double charged;
    double prorated;
    int quotaReset;
};

struct User {
    std::string plan;       // "trial", "basic", "pro"
    int trialEndDay;
    int usageCount;
    int usageQuota;
    bool paymentMethodValid;
    bool welcomeSent;
};

static std::unordered_map<std::string, User> users;
static std::unordered_set<std::string> convertedIds;
static std::vector<std::string> eventLog;
static std::unordered_map<std::string, double> planPrices = {
    {"basic", 29.0}, {"pro", 99.0}
};
static const int billingCycleDays = 30;

static void reset() {
    users.clear(); convertedIds.clear(); eventLog.clear();
}

static void addUser(const std::string& uid, int trialEnd, int usage, bool validPay) {
    users[uid] = {"trial", trialEnd, usage, 100, validPay, false};
}

// Convert a trial user to paid.
// Rules:
//  1. convertId is idempotent: duplicate returns {"ALREADY_PAID",0,0,0}.
//  2. User must exist and be on "trial" plan; otherwise ALREADY_PAID if paid, or error.
//  3. If currentDay < user.trialEndDay, return TRIAL_ACTIVE (trial not over yet).
//  4. If currentDay > user.trialEndDay + 7, return EXPIRED (grace period = 7 days).
//  5. Payment method must be valid; otherwise NO_PAYMENT.
//  6. Proration: daysUsed = currentDay - trialEndDay; prorated = planPrice * daysUsed / billingCycleDays.
//  7. charged = planPrice - prorated (they already "used" some of the billing cycle).
//  8. Update plan, reset usageCount to 0, set quota based on plan (basic=500, pro=2000),
//     set welcomeSent=true, log "CONVERT:<uid>:<plan>".
static ConvertResult convert(const std::string& convertId,
                             const std::string& uid,
                             const std::string& newPlan,
                             int currentDay) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    if (convertedIds.count(convertId)) return {"ALREADY_PAID", 0, 0, 0};
    if (!users.count(uid)) return {"ALREADY_PAID", 0, 0, 0};
    auto& u = users[uid];
    if (u.plan != "trial") return {"ALREADY_PAID", 0, 0, 0};
    if (currentDay < u.trialEndDay) return {"TRIAL_ACTIVE", 0, 0, 0};
    if (currentDay > u.trialEndDay + 7) return {"EXPIRED", 0, 0, 0};
    if (!u.paymentMethodValid) return {"NO_PAYMENT", 0, 0, 0};
    double price = planPrices[newPlan];
    int daysUsed = currentDay - u.trialEndDay;
    double prorated = price * daysUsed / billingCycleDays;
    double charged = price - prorated;
    u.plan = newPlan;
    u.usageCount = 0;
    u.usageQuota = (newPlan == "pro") ? 2000 : 500;
    u.welcomeSent = true;
    convertedIds.insert(convertId);
    eventLog.push_back("CONVERT:" + uid + ":" + newPlan);
    return {"OK", charged, prorated, u.usageQuota};


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
}

int main() {
    reset();
    addUser("U1", 30, 50, true);  // trial ends day 30
    auto r = convert("CV1", "U1", "pro", 33);  // 3 days into grace
    assert(r.status == "OK");
    // prorated = 99 * 3/30 = 9.9, charged = 99 - 9.9 = 89.1
    assert(std::abs(r.charged - 89.1) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r.prorated - 9.9) < 0.01);
    assert(r.quotaReset == 2000);
    assert(users["U1"].welcomeSent == true);
    assert(users["U1"].usageCount == 0);
    assert(eventLog.size() == 1);
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // idempotent
    reset();
    addUser("U2", 10, 5, true);
    convert("CV2", "U2", "basic", 12);
    auto dup = convert("CV2", "U2", "basic", 12);
    assert(dup.status == "ALREADY_PAID");

    // trial still active
    reset();
    addUser("U3", 30, 0, true);
    auto act = convert("CV3", "U3", "pro", 25);
    assert(act.status == "TRIAL_ACTIVE");
    assert(users["U3"].plan == "trial");

    // expired past grace
    reset();
    addUser("U4", 10, 0, true);
    auto exp = convert("CV4", "U4", "basic", 20);
    assert(exp.status == "EXPIRED");

    // no payment method
    reset();
    addUser("U5", 10, 0, false);
    auto np = convert("CV5", "U5", "basic", 12);
    assert(np.status == "NO_PAYMENT");

    // basic plan gets quota 500
    reset();
    addUser("U6", 10, 80, true);
    auto b = convert("CV6", "U6", "basic", 10);
    assert(b.status == "OK");
    assert(b.quotaReset == 500);
    assert(users["U6"].usageCount == 0);

PREFIX CODE:

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>

struct AwardResult {
    std::string status;  // AWARDED, INELIGIBLE, DUPLICATE, AID_CAP
    double needBased;
    double meritBased;
    double totalAwarded;
    int deadlineDay;
};

struct Student {
    double gpa;
    bool enrolled;
    double familyIncome;
    double existingAid;
};

static std::unordered_map<std::string, Student> students;
static std::unordered_set<std::string> processedApps;
static std::vector<std::string> awardLog;
static const double totalAidCap = 40000.0;
static const double meritGpaThreshold = 3.7;
static const double needIncomeThreshold = 50000.0;
static const int acceptancePeriod = 30;  // days to accept

static void reset() {
    students.clear(); processedApps.clear(); awardLog.clear();
}

static void addStudent(const std::string& sid, double gpa, bool enrolled,
                       double income, double existingAid) {
    students[sid] = {gpa, enrolled, income, existingAid};
}

// Calculate and award a scholarship.
// Rules:
//  1. appId idempotent: duplicate returns {"DUPLICATE",0,0,0,0}.
//  2. Student must exist, be enrolled, and have GPA >= 2.0; otherwise INELIGIBLE.
//  3. Need-based: if familyIncome < needIncomeThreshold →
//     needBased = (needIncomeThreshold - familyIncome) / needIncomeThreshold * 15000.
//  4. Merit-based: if gpa >= meritGpaThreshold → meritBased = (gpa - 3.0) * 5000.
//  5. Stacking: totalAwarded = needBased + meritBased.
//  6. Aid cap: if existingAid + totalAwarded > totalAidCap,
//     reduce totalAwarded to totalAidCap - existingAid (but not below 0).
//     Return AID_CAP status if capped, AWARDED otherwise.
//  7. Update existingAid, log "AWARD:<sid>:<totalAwarded>", set deadline = applicationDay + acceptancePeriod.
static AwardResult award(const std::string& appId,
                         const std::string& sid,
                         int applicationDay) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    if (processedApps.count(appId)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, 0, 0};
    if (!students.count(sid) || !students[sid].enrolled || students[sid].gpa < 2.0)
        return {"INELIGIBLE", 0, 0, 0, 0};
    auto& st = students[sid];
    double need = 0.0;
    if (st.familyIncome < needIncomeThreshold)
        need = (needIncomeThreshold - st.familyIncome) / needIncomeThreshold * 15000.0;
    double merit = 0.0;
    if (st.gpa >= meritGpaThreshold)
        merit = (st.gpa - 3.0) * 5000.0;
    double total = need + merit;
    std::string status = "AWARDED";
    double room = totalAidCap - st.existingAid;
    if (total > room) { total = std::max(room, 0.0); status = "AID_CAP"; }
    st.existingAid += total;
    processedApps.insert(appId);
    awardLog.push_back("AWARD:" + sid + ":" + std::to_string(total));
    return {status, need, merit, total, applicationDay + acceptancePeriod};


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
}

int main() {
    reset();
    addStudent("S1", 3.9, true, 30000, 10000);
    auto r = award("A1", "S1", 100);
    assert(r.status == "AWARDED");
    // need = (50000-30000)/50000 * 15000 = 6000
    assert(std::abs(r.needBased - 6000.0) < 0.01);
    // merit = (3.9 - 3.0) * 5000 = 4500
    assert(std::abs(r.meritBased - 4500.0) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r.totalAwarded - 10500.0) < 0.01);
    assert(r.deadlineDay == 130);
    assert(std::abs(students["S1"].existingAid - 20500.0) < 0.01);
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // duplicate
    reset();
    addStudent("S2", 3.5, true, 40000, 0);
    award("A2", "S2", 50);
    auto d = award("A2", "S2", 50);
    assert(d.status == "DUPLICATE");

    // ineligible — not enrolled
    reset();
    addStudent("S3", 3.9, false, 20000, 0);
    auto ie = award("A3", "S3", 10);
    assert(ie.status == "INELIGIBLE");

    // ineligible — low GPA
    reset();
    addStudent("S4", 1.8, true, 20000, 0);
    auto lg = award("A4", "S4", 10);
    assert(lg.status == "INELIGIBLE");

    // aid cap triggers
    reset();
    addStudent("S5", 4.0, true, 10000, 35000);
    auto cap = award("A5", "S5", 10);
    assert(cap.status == "AID_CAP");
    // need = (50000-10000)/50000*15000 = 12000, merit = (4.0-3.0)*5000 = 5000
    // total would be 17000, but room = 40000-35000 = 5000
    assert(std::abs(cap.totalAwarded - 5000.0) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(students["S5"].existingAid - 40000.0) < 0.01);

    // no need, no merit (GPA 3.5 < 3.7, income above threshold)
    reset();
    addStudent("S6", 3.5, true, 60000, 0);
    auto zero = award("A6", "S6", 10);
    assert(zero.status == "AWARDED");
    assert(std::abs(zero.totalAwarded - 0.0) < 0.01);

PREFIX CODE:

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>

struct TicketResult {
    std::string status;  // FIRED, QUEUED, DUPLICATE, OUT_OF_STOCK, ALLERGEN_BLOCK
    int prepTime;
    std::string station;
    int ticketNumber;
};

struct MenuItem {
    std::string station;   // "grill", "fry", "salad", "dessert"
    int prepMinutes;
    std::unordered_map<std::string, int> ingredients;  // ingredient -> qty needed
    std::unordered_set<std::string> allergens;
};

static std::unordered_map<std::string, MenuItem> menu;
static std::unordered_map<std::string, int> inventory;  // ingredient -> qty available
static std::unordered_set<std::string> processedOrders;
static std::vector<std::string> ticketQueue;
static std::vector<std::string> completionLog;
static int nextTicket = 1;
static int maxQueuePerStation = 5;
static std::unordered_map<std::string, int> stationLoad; // station -> current tickets

static void reset() {
    menu.clear(); inventory.clear(); processedOrders.clear();
    ticketQueue.clear(); completionLog.clear();
    nextTicket = 1; stationLoad.clear();
}

static void addMenuItem(const std::string& name, const std::string& station, int prep,
                        std::unordered_map<std::string, int> ingr,
                        std::unordered_set<std::string> allergens) {
    menu[name] = {station, prep, ingr, allergens};
}

static void setInventory(const std::string& item, int qty) { inventory[item] = qty; }

// Fire a kitchen order.
// Rules:
//  1. orderId idempotent: duplicate returns {"DUPLICATE",0,"",0}.
//  2. Item must exist in menu.
//  3. If customer has ANY listed allergen that the item contains, return ALLERGEN_BLOCK
//     BEFORE checking inventory.
//  4. All ingredients must have sufficient inventory; otherwise OUT_OF_STOCK (no deduction).
//  5. If stationLoad[station] >= maxQueuePerStation, return QUEUED, still deduct inventory
//     and assign ticket, but status is QUEUED not FIRED.
//  6. Deduct ingredients, increment stationLoad, assign ticket number, push to ticketQueue
//     "TICKET:<num>:<item>:<station>", log nothing to completionLog yet.
//  7. prepTime = item.prepMinutes + stationLoad[station] * 2 (congestion penalty AFTER increment).
static TicketResult fireOrder(const std::string& orderId,
                              const std::string& item,
                              const std::unordered_set<std::string>& customerAllergens) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    if (processedOrders.count(orderId)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, "", 0};
    if (!menu.count(item)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, "", 0};
    auto& mi = menu[item];
    for (auto& a : mi.allergens)
        if (customerAllergens.count(a)) return {"ALLERGEN_BLOCK", 0, "", 0};
    for (auto& [ing, qty] : mi.ingredients)
        if (inventory[ing] < qty) return {"OUT_OF_STOCK", 0, "", 0};
    for (auto& [ing, qty] : mi.ingredients)
        inventory[ing] -= qty;
    stationLoad[mi.station]++;
    int ticket = nextTicket++;
    processedOrders.insert(orderId);
    std::string status = "FIRED";
    if (stationLoad[mi.station] > maxQueuePerStation) status = "QUEUED";
    int prep = mi.prepMinutes + stationLoad[mi.station] * 2;
    ticketQueue.push_back("TICKET:" + std::to_string(ticket) + ":" + item + ":" + mi.station);
    return {status, prep, mi.station, ticket};


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
}

int main() {
    reset();
    maxQueuePerStation = 3;
    addMenuItem("burger", "grill", 10, {{"patty", 1}, {"bun", 1}}, {"gluten"});
    addMenuItem("salad", "salad", 5, {{"lettuce", 2}}, {"none"});
    setInventory("patty", 5);
    setInventory("bun", 5);
    setInventory("lettuce", 10);

    auto r1 = fireOrder("O1", "burger", {});
    assert(r1.status == "FIRED");
    assert(r1.station == "grill");
    assert(r1.ticketNumber == 1);
    // stationLoad is 1 after increment, prep = 10 + 1*2 = 12
    assert(r1.prepTime == 12);
    assert(inventory["patty"] == 4);

    // allergen block
    auto r2 = fireOrder("O2", "burger", {"gluten"});
    assert(r2.status == "ALLERGEN_BLOCK");
    assert(inventory["patty"] == 4);  // no deduction on block

    assert(ticketQueue.size() == 1);
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // duplicate
    reset();
    addMenuItem("fries", "fry", 4, {{"potato", 2}}, {});
    setInventory("potato", 20);
    fireOrder("O3", "fries", {});
    int invAfter = inventory["potato"];
    auto d = fireOrder("O3", "fries", {});
    assert(d.status == "DUPLICATE");
    assert(inventory["potato"] == invAfter);

    // out of stock — no partial deduction
    reset();
    addMenuItem("steak", "grill", 15, {{"beef", 1}, {"butter", 1}}, {});
    setInventory("beef", 1);
    setInventory("butter", 0);
    auto os = fireOrder("O4", "steak", {});
    assert(os.status == "OUT_OF_STOCK");
    assert(inventory["beef"] == 1);  // no deduction

    // station overload → QUEUED
    reset();
    maxQueuePerStation = 2;
    addMenuItem("wrap", "salad", 3, {{"tortilla", 1}}, {});
    setInventory("tortilla", 10);
    fireOrder("O5", "wrap", {});
    fireOrder("O6", "wrap", {});
    auto q = fireOrder("O7", "wrap", {});
    assert(q.status == "QUEUED");  // 3rd ticket, max is 2
    assert(q.ticketNumber == 3);
    assert(inventory["tortilla"] == 7);  // still deducted

    // ticket numbering sequential
    reset();
    addMenuItem("cake", "dessert", 8, {{"flour", 1}}, {});
    setInventory("flour", 10);
    auto t1 = fireOrder("O8", "cake", {});
    auto t2 = fireOrder("O9", "cake", {});
    assert(t2.ticketNumber == t1.ticketNumber + 1);

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>

struct TaxResult {
    std::string status;  // ASSESSED, DUPLICATE, EXEMPT
    double assessedValue;
    double totalTax;
    double exemptionAmount;
    int installments;
    int appealDeadlineDay;
};

static double assessmentRatio = 0.85;
static double millageRate = 0.025;       // per dollar of assessed value
static double homesteadExemption = 25000.0;
static double seniorExemption = 10000.0;
static int installmentThreshold = 2000;  // taxes above this get 4 installments
static int appealWindow = 45;            // days from assessment

static std::unordered_set<std::string> processedParcels;
static std::vector<std::string> taxRoll;

static void reset() { processedParcels.clear(); taxRoll.clear(); }

// Assess property tax for a parcel.
// Rules:
//  1. parcelId idempotent: duplicate returns {"DUPLICATE",0,0,0,0,0}.
//  2. assessedValue = marketValue * assessmentRatio.
//  3. Exemptions: homesteadExemption if isHomestead, seniorExemption if isSenior.
//     Exemptions stack but cannot exceed assessedValue.
//  4. taxableValue = assessedValue - totalExemptions (min 0).
//  5. totalTax = taxableValue * millageRate + specialLevy.
//  6. If totalTax == 0, return EXEMPT.
//  7. installments = 4 if totalTax > installmentThreshold, else 2.
//  8. appealDeadlineDay = assessmentDay + appealWindow.
//  9. Log "TAX:<parcelId>:<totalTax>", mark processed.
static TaxResult assess(const std::string& parcelId,
                        double marketValue,
                        bool isHomestead, bool isSenior,
                        double specialLevy,
                        int assessmentDay) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    if (processedParcels.count(parcelId)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
    double assessed = marketValue * assessmentRatio;
    double exempt = 0;
    if (isHomestead) exempt += homesteadExemption;
    if (isSenior) exempt += seniorExemption;
    if (exempt > assessed) exempt = assessed;
    double taxable = assessed - exempt;
    if (taxable < 0) taxable = 0;
    double tax = taxable * millageRate + specialLevy;
    if (tax == 0) { processedParcels.insert(parcelId); return {"EXEMPT", assessed, 0, exempt, 0, assessmentDay + appealWindow}; }
    int inst = (tax > installmentThreshold) ? 4 : 2;
    processedParcels.insert(parcelId);
    taxRoll.push_back("TAX:" + parcelId + ":" + std::to_string(tax));
    return {"ASSESSED", assessed, tax, exempt, inst, assessmentDay + appealWindow};


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
}

int main() {
    reset();
    auto r = assess("P1", 300000, true, false, 100, 60);
    // assessed = 300000 * 0.85 = 255000
    // exempt = 25000 (homestead only)
    // taxable = 230000, tax = 230000 * 0.025 + 100 = 5850
    assert(r.status == "ASSESSED");
    assert(std::abs(r.assessedValue - 255000) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r.totalTax - 5850) < 0.01);
    assert(r.installments == 4);  // > 2000
    assert(r.appealDeadlineDay == 105);
    assert(taxRoll.size() == 1);
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // duplicate
    reset();
    assess("P2", 100000, false, false, 0, 10);
    auto d = assess("P2", 100000, false, false, 0, 10);
    assert(d.status == "DUPLICATE");

    // stacked exemptions
    reset();
    auto s = assess("P3", 200000, true, true, 0, 10);
    // assessed = 170000, exempt = 35000, taxable = 135000, tax = 3375
    assert(std::abs(s.exemptionAmount - 35000) < 0.01);
    assert(s.installments == 4);

    // exemptions capped at assessed value
    reset();
    auto c = assess("P4", 30000, true, true, 0, 10);
    // assessed = 25500, exempt cannot exceed 25500
    assert(std::abs(c.exemptionAmount - 25500.0) < 0.01);

    // small tax → 2 installments
    reset();
    auto sm = assess("P5", 50000, true, false, 0, 10);
    // assessed=42500, exempt=25000, taxable=17500, tax=437.5
    assert(sm.installments == 2);

    // zero tax → EXEMPT
    reset();
    auto ex = assess("P6", 20000, true, true, 0, 10);
    // assessed=17000, exempt=17000 (capped), taxable=0, tax=0
    assert(ex.status == "EXEMPT");

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>

struct ShipResult {
    std::string status;  // BOOKED, DENIED, DUPLICATE, HAZMAT_DENIED
    double baseCharge;
    double fuelSurcharge;
    double totalCharge;
    int transitDays;
    std::string mode;     // "LTL" or "FTL"
    std::string bolNumber;
};

static std::unordered_set<std::string> processedShipments;
static std::unordered_set<std::string> hazmatCodes;
static std::unordered_set<std::string> hazmatCertifiedCarriers;
static std::vector<std::string> bolLog;
static int nextBol = 1000;
static double fuelSurchargeRate = 0.12;
static int ftlWeightThreshold = 15000;  // lbs
static double ltlRatePerLb = 0.35;
static double ftlFlatRate = 3500.0;
static int baseDaysLtl = 5;
static int baseDaysFtl = 3;

static void reset() {
    processedShipments.clear(); hazmatCodes.clear();
    hazmatCertifiedCarriers.clear(); bolLog.clear();
    nextBol = 1000;
}

static void addHazmat(const std::string& code) { hazmatCodes.insert(code); }
static void certifyCarrier(const std::string& c) { hazmatCertifiedCarriers.insert(c); }

// Book a freight shipment.
// Rules:
//  1. shipmentId idempotent: duplicate returns {"DUPLICATE",0,0,0,0,"",""}.
//  2. If commodityCode is hazmat AND carrier not certified → HAZMAT_DENIED.
//  3. If weight <= 0, DENIED.
//  4. mode: LTL if weight < ftlWeightThreshold, else FTL.
//  5. baseCharge: LTL = weight * ltlRatePerLb; FTL = ftlFlatRate.
//  6. For each accessorial in the list, add 150.0.
//  7. fuelSurcharge = (baseCharge + accessorials) * fuelSurchargeRate.
//  8. totalCharge = baseCharge + accessorials + fuelSurcharge.
//  9. transitDays: baseDaysLtl for LTL, baseDaysFtl for FTL; add 1 if hazmat.
// 10. bolNumber = "BOL-" + nextBol (then increment).
// 11. Log "SHIP:<bolNumber>:<totalCharge>", mark processed.
static ShipResult book(const std::string& shipmentId,
                       const std::string& commodityCode,
                       const std::string& carrier,
                       int weight,
                       const std::vector<std::string>& accessorials) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    if (processedShipments.count(shipmentId)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, 0, 0, "", ""};
    if (weight <= 0) return {"DENIED", 0, 0, 0, 0, "", ""};
    bool isHazmat = hazmatCodes.count(commodityCode) > 0;
    if (isHazmat && !hazmatCertifiedCarriers.count(carrier))
        return {"HAZMAT_DENIED", 0, 0, 0, 0, "", ""};
    std::string mode = (weight < ftlWeightThreshold) ? "LTL" : "FTL";
    double base = (mode == "LTL") ? weight * ltlRatePerLb : ftlFlatRate;
    double acc = accessorials.size() * 150.0;
    double fuel = (base + acc) * fuelSurchargeRate;
    double total = base + acc + fuel;
    int days = (mode == "LTL") ? baseDaysLtl : baseDaysFtl;
    if (isHazmat) days += 1;
    std::string bol = "BOL-" + std::to_string(nextBol++);
    processedShipments.insert(shipmentId);
    bolLog.push_back("SHIP:" + bol + ":" + std::to_string(total));
    return {"BOOKED", base, fuel, total, days, mode, bol};


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
}

int main() {
    reset();
    addHazmat("FLAM");
    certifyCarrier("SafeHaul");

    auto r = book("S1", "GENERAL", "AnyCarrier", 5000, {"liftgate", "inside"});
    assert(r.status == "BOOKED");
    assert(r.mode == "LTL");
    // base = 5000*0.35 = 1750, acc = 300, fuel = 2050*0.12 = 246
    assert(std::abs(r.baseCharge - 1750) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r.totalCharge - 2296) < 0.01);
    assert(r.transitDays == 5);
    assert(r.bolNumber == "BOL-1000");

    auto r2 = book("S2", "FLAM", "SafeHaul", 20000, {});
    assert(r2.status == "BOOKED");
    assert(r2.mode == "FTL");
    assert(r2.transitDays == 4);  // 3 + 1 hazmat
    assert(r2.bolNumber == "BOL-1001");
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // duplicate
    reset();
    book("S3", "GENERAL", "X", 1000, {});
    auto d = book("S3", "GENERAL", "X", 1000, {});
    assert(d.status == "DUPLICATE");

    // hazmat not certified
    reset();
    addHazmat("ACID");
    auto h = book("S4", "ACID", "NoCert", 500, {});
    assert(h.status == "HAZMAT_DENIED");

    // zero weight denied
    reset();
    auto z = book("S5", "GENERAL", "X", 0, {});
    assert(z.status == "DENIED");

    // exact threshold → FTL
    reset();
    auto th = book("S6", "GENERAL", "X", 15000, {});
    assert(th.mode == "FTL");
    assert(std::abs(th.baseCharge - 3500) < 0.01);

    // just under threshold → LTL
    reset();
    auto lt = book("S7", "GENERAL", "X", 14999, {});
    assert(lt.mode == "LTL");

    // fuel surcharge includes accessorials
    reset();
    auto fs = book("S8", "GENERAL", "X", 1000, {"tailgate"});
    // base=350, acc=150, fuel=(350+150)*0.12=60, total=560
    assert(std::abs(fs.totalCharge - 560) < 0.01);

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>

struct DisbursementResult {
    std::string status;  // DISBURSED, DENIED, DUPLICATE, HOLD
    double netAmount;
    double origFee;
    double refund;         // if over-award
    int disbursementDay;
};

struct LoanRecord {
    double awardAmount;
    double disbursed;
    bool enrollmentVerified;
    bool satisfactoryProgress;
    bool entranceCounseling;
    double tuitionBalance;
};

static std::unordered_map<std::string, LoanRecord> loans;
static std::unordered_set<std::string> processedDisb;
static std::vector<std::string> disbLog;
static double origFeeRate = 0.01057;  // ~1.057%
static int disbursementCount = 2;      // split into 2 disbursements

static void reset() { loans.clear(); processedDisb.clear(); disbLog.clear(); }

static void addLoan(const std::string& lid, double award, bool enrolled,
                    bool progress, bool counseling, double tuition) {
    loans[lid] = {award, 0, enrolled, progress, counseling, tuition};
}

// Disburse a student loan installment.
// Rules:
//  1. disbId idempotent: duplicate returns {"DUPLICATE",0,0,0,0}.
//  2. Loan must exist.
//  3. enrollmentVerified AND satisfactoryProgress AND entranceCounseling must all be true;
//     otherwise HOLD (loan exists but can't disburse).
//  4. Each disbursement is awardAmount / disbursementCount.
//  5. If already fully disbursed (disbursed >= awardAmount), DENIED.
//  6. origFee = installment * origFeeRate. netAmount = installment - origFee.
//  7. If netAmount > tuitionBalance, refund = netAmount - tuitionBalance, netAmount = tuitionBalance.
//     Otherwise refund = 0.
//  8. Update disbursed += installment (NOT netAmount), reduce tuitionBalance by netAmount.
//  9. Log "DISB:<lid>:<netAmount>", mark processed.
static DisbursementResult disburse(const std::string& disbId,
                                   const std::string& lid,
                                   int day) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    if (processedDisb.count(disbId)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, 0, 0};
    if (!loans.count(lid)) return {"DENIED", 0, 0, 0, 0};
    auto& ln = loans[lid];
    if (!ln.enrollmentVerified || !ln.satisfactoryProgress || !ln.entranceCounseling)
        return {"HOLD", 0, 0, 0, 0};
    if (ln.disbursed >= ln.awardAmount) return {"DENIED", 0, 0, 0, 0};
    double installment = ln.awardAmount / disbursementCount;
    double fee = installment * origFeeRate;
    double net = installment - fee;
    double refund = 0;
    if (net > ln.tuitionBalance) {
        refund = net - ln.tuitionBalance;
        net = ln.tuitionBalance;
    }
    ln.disbursed += installment;
    ln.tuitionBalance -= net;
    processedDisb.insert(disbId);
    disbLog.push_back("DISB:" + lid + ":" + std::to_string(net));
    return {"DISBURSED", net, fee, refund, day};


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
}

int main() {
    reset();
    addLoan("L1", 10000, true, true, true, 6000);
    auto r = disburse("D1", "L1", 90);
    assert(r.status == "DISBURSED");
    // installment = 5000, fee = 5000*0.01057 = 52.85, net = 4947.15
    assert(std::abs(r.origFee - 52.85) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r.netAmount - 4947.15) < 0.01);
    assert(r.refund == 0);  // 4947.15 < 6000 tuition
    assert(std::abs(loans["L1"].tuitionBalance - (6000 - 4947.15)) < 0.01);
    assert(loans["L1"].disbursed == 5000);  // tracks gross, not net
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // duplicate
    reset();
    addLoan("L2", 8000, true, true, true, 5000);
    disburse("D2", "L2", 50);
    auto d = disburse("D2", "L2", 50);
    assert(d.status == "DUPLICATE");

    // HOLD — missing counseling
    reset();
    addLoan("L3", 8000, true, true, false, 5000);
    auto h = disburse("D3", "L3", 50);
    assert(h.status == "HOLD");

    // fully disbursed
    reset();
    addLoan("L4", 4000, true, true, true, 3000);
    disburse("D4a", "L4", 50);
    disburse("D4b", "L4", 60);
    auto fd = disburse("D4c", "L4", 70);
    assert(fd.status == "DENIED");

    // refund when net > tuition
    reset();
    addLoan("L5", 20000, true, true, true, 3000);
    auto ref = disburse("D5", "L5", 50);
    assert(ref.status == "DISBURSED");
    // installment=10000, fee=105.7, net=9894.3, but tuition=3000
    assert(std::abs(ref.netAmount - 3000) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(ref.refund - 6894.3) < 0.01);
    assert(loans["L5"].tuitionBalance == 0);
    assert(loans["L5"].disbursed == 10000); // tracks gross

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>

struct VisitResult {
    std::string status;  // INVOICED, DUPLICATE
    double subtotal;
    double discount;
    double total;
    int followupDay;
};

static double examFee = 65.0;
static double labFee = 45.0;
static double wellnessDiscountRate = 0.10;
static double multiPetRate = 0.05;
static int followupDays = 14;

static std::unordered_map<std::string, double> procedurePrices;
static std::unordered_map<std::string, double> medPrices;
static std::unordered_set<std::string> processedVisits;
static std::vector<std::string> invoiceLog;

static void reset() {
    procedurePrices.clear(); medPrices.clear();
    processedVisits.clear(); invoiceLog.clear();
}

static void addProcedure(const std::string& code, double price) { procedurePrices[code] = price; }
static void addMed(const std::string& name, double price) { medPrices[name] = price; }

// Create a vet visit invoice.
// Rules:
//  1. visitId idempotent: duplicate returns {"DUPLICATE",0,0,0,0}.
//  2. subtotal starts with examFee.
//  3. Add price for each procedure code in procedures list.
//  4. Add price for each medication in meds list.
//  5. If includeLabWork, add labFee.
//  6. discount: wellnessDiscountRate applies if hasWellnessPlan.
//     multiPetRate applies if isMultiPet. Discounts are ADDITIVE rates on subtotal.
//  7. total = subtotal - discount. Minimum total is 0.
//  8. followupDay = visitDay + followupDays.
//  9. Log "INVOICE:<visitId>:<total>", mark processed.
static VisitResult invoice(const std::string& visitId,
                           const std::vector<std::string>& procedures,
                           const std::vector<std::string>& meds,
                           bool includeLabWork,
                           bool hasWellnessPlan,
                           bool isMultiPet,
                           int visitDay) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    if (processedVisits.count(visitId)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, 0, 0};
    double sub = examFee;
    for (auto& p : procedures) sub += procedurePrices[p];
    for (auto& m : meds) sub += medPrices[m];
    if (includeLabWork) sub += labFee;
    double rate = 0;
    if (hasWellnessPlan) rate += wellnessDiscountRate;
    if (isMultiPet) rate += multiPetRate;
    double disc = sub * rate;
    double tot = sub - disc;
    if (tot < 0) tot = 0;
    processedVisits.insert(visitId);
    invoiceLog.push_back("INVOICE:" + visitId + ":" + std::to_string(tot));
    return {"INVOICED", sub, disc, tot, visitDay + followupDays};


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
}

int main() {
    reset();
    addProcedure("VACC", 35.0);
    addProcedure("DENTAL", 120.0);
    addMed("antibiotics", 25.0);

    auto r = invoice("V1", {"VACC", "DENTAL"}, {"antibiotics"}, true, true, true, 50);
    assert(r.status == "INVOICED");
    // sub = 65 + 35 + 120 + 25 + 45 = 290
    // rate = 0.10 + 0.05 = 0.15, disc = 290*0.15 = 43.5
    // total = 246.5
    assert(std::abs(r.subtotal - 290) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r.discount - 43.5) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r.total - 246.5) < 0.01);
    assert(r.followupDay == 64);
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // duplicate
    reset();
    addProcedure("VACC", 35);
    invoice("V2", {"VACC"}, {}, false, false, false, 10);
    auto d = invoice("V2", {"VACC"}, {}, false, false, false, 10);
    assert(d.status == "DUPLICATE");

    // no discounts
    reset();
    addProcedure("VACC", 35);
    auto nd = invoice("V3", {"VACC"}, {}, false, false, false, 10);
    assert(std::abs(nd.discount - 0) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(nd.total - 100) < 0.01);  // 65 + 35

    // only wellness discount
    reset();
    addProcedure("VACC", 35);
    auto w = invoice("V4", {"VACC"}, {}, false, true, false, 10);
    // sub=100, disc=10, total=90
    assert(std::abs(w.total - 90) < 0.01);

    // lab work added
    reset();
    auto l = invoice("V5", {}, {}, true, false, false, 10);
    // sub = 65 + 45 = 110
    assert(std::abs(l.subtotal - 110) < 0.01);

Test Case ID: 40
Source: devbench-code-purpose-understanding

PREFIX CODE:

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>

struct BookingResult {
    std::string status;  // CONFIRMED, SOLD_OUT, DUPLICATE, INVALID_SEAT
    double ticketCost;
    double addOns;
    double processingFee;
    double total;
    int loyaltyPointsUsed;
};

struct Showtime {
    int capacity;
    int sold;
    std::unordered_set<std::string> takenSeats;
};

static std::unordered_map<std::string, Showtime> showtimes;
static std::unordered_set<std::string> processedBookings;
static std::vector<std::string> confirmLog;
static double adultPrice = 14.0;
static double childPrice = 9.0;
static double seniorPrice = 10.0;
static double processingFeeRate = 0.08;
static double comboMealPrice = 12.0;
static int loyaltyPointValue = 100;  // 100 points = $1 off

static void reset() { showtimes.clear(); processedBookings.clear(); confirmLog.clear(); }
static void addShowtime(const std::string& id, int cap) {
    showtimes[id] = {cap, 0, {}};
}

// Book movie tickets.
// Rules:
//  1. bookingId idempotent: duplicate returns {"DUPLICATE",0,0,0,0,0}.
//  2. Showtime must exist. Seat must not be taken; otherwise INVALID_SEAT.
//  3. If showtime.sold >= capacity, SOLD_OUT.
//  4. ticketCost based on tierType: "adult"→adultPrice, "child"→childPrice, "senior"→seniorPrice.
//  5. addOns = comboMealPrice * comboCount.
//  6. Loyalty: deduct min(loyaltyPoints, (ticketCost+addOns)*100) points.
//     loyaltyDiscount = pointsUsed / loyaltyPointValue.
//  7. processingFee = (ticketCost + addOns - loyaltyDiscount) * processingFeeRate.
//  8. total = ticketCost + addOns - loyaltyDiscount + processingFee.
//  9. Mark seat taken, increment sold, log "BOOK:<bookingId>:<total>".
static BookingResult bookTicket(const std::string& bookingId,
                                const std::string& showtimeId,
                                const std::string& seat,
                                const std::string& tierType,
                                int comboCount,
                                int loyaltyPoints) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    if (processedBookings.count(bookingId)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
    if (!showtimes.count(showtimeId)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
    auto& st = showtimes[showtimeId];
    if (st.takenSeats.count(seat)) return {"INVALID_SEAT", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
    if (st.sold >= st.capacity) return {"SOLD_OUT", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
    double ticket = adultPrice;
    if (tierType == "child") ticket = childPrice;
    else if (tierType == "senior") ticket = seniorPrice;
    double adds = comboMealPrice * comboCount;
    double base = ticket + adds;
    int maxPoints = (int)(base * 100);
    int used = std::min(loyaltyPoints, maxPoints);
    double loyDisc = (double)used / loyaltyPointValue;
    double afterDisc = base - loyDisc;
    double fee = afterDisc * processingFeeRate;
    double total = afterDisc + fee;
    st.takenSeats.insert(seat);
    st.sold++;
    processedBookings.insert(bookingId);
    confirmLog.push_back("BOOK:" + bookingId + ":" + std::to_string(total));
    return {"CONFIRMED", ticket, adds, fee, total, used};


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
}

int main() {
    reset();
    addShowtime("S1", 100);
    auto r = bookTicket("B1", "S1", "A1", "adult", 1, 500);
    assert(r.status == "CONFIRMED");
    // ticket=14, adds=12, base=26, maxPts=2600, used=500
    // disc=500/100=5, afterDisc=21, fee=21*0.08=1.68, total=22.68
    assert(std::abs(r.ticketCost - 14) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r.addOns - 12) < 0.01);
    assert(r.loyaltyPointsUsed == 500);
    assert(std::abs(r.total - 22.68) < 0.01);
    assert(showtimes["S1"].sold == 1);
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // duplicate
    reset();
    addShowtime("S2", 50);
    bookTicket("B2", "S2", "A1", "adult", 0, 0);
    auto d = bookTicket("B2", "S2", "A1", "adult", 0, 0);
    assert(d.status == "DUPLICATE");

    // taken seat
    reset();
    addShowtime("S3", 50);
    bookTicket("B3", "S3", "C5", "child", 0, 0);
    auto ts = bookTicket("B4", "S3", "C5", "adult", 0, 0);
    assert(ts.status == "INVALID_SEAT");

    // sold out
    reset();
    addShowtime("S4", 1);
    bookTicket("B5", "S4", "A1", "adult", 0, 0);
    auto so = bookTicket("B6", "S4", "A2", "adult", 0, 0);
    assert(so.status == "SOLD_OUT");

    // child pricing
    reset();
    addShowtime("S5", 50);
    auto ch = bookTicket("B7", "S5", "A1", "child", 0, 0);
    assert(std::abs(ch.ticketCost - 9) < 0.01);

    // loyalty points capped at base cost
    reset();
    addShowtime("S6", 50);
    auto lp = bookTicket("B8", "S6", "A1", "senior", 0, 99999);
    // ticket=10, adds=0, maxPts=1000, used=1000, disc=10 → total with fee
    assert(lp.loyaltyPointsUsed == 1000);
    // afterDisc=0, fee=0, total=0
    assert(std::abs(lp.total - 0) < 0.01);
