#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>

struct BookResult {
    std::string status;  // BOOKED, WAITLISTED, DUPLICATE, CANCEL_PENALTY, NO_PUNCHES
    int position;        // 0 if booked, waitlist position if waitlisted
    int punchesRemaining;
    bool equipmentReserved;
};

struct FitnessClass {
    int capacity;
    int enrolled;
    std::vector<std::string> waitlist;
    bool requiresEquipment;
    int equipmentAvailable;
};

struct Member {
    int punchCard;  // remaining punches
    std::unordered_set<std::string> bookedClasses;
    int lastCancelDay;
};

static std::unordered_map<std::string, FitnessClass> classes;
static std::unordered_map<std::string, Member> members;
static std::vector<std::string> bookingLog;
static int cancelPenaltyWindow = 2;  // cancel within 2 days of class = penalty
static int penaltyCost = 1;          // penalty deducts 1 punch

static void reset() { classes.clear(); members.clear(); bookingLog.clear(); }

static void addClass(const std::string& cid, int cap, bool equip, int equipQty) {
    classes[cid] = {cap, 0, {}, equip, equipQty};
}
static void addMember(const std::string& mid, int punches) {
    members[mid] = {punches, {}, -999};
}

// Book a fitness class.
// Rules:
//  1. Idempotent: if member already booked this class, return {"DUPLICATE",0,punches,false}.
//  2. Member must have punchCard > 0; otherwise NO_PUNCHES.
//  3. If enrolled < capacity: BOOKED, deduct 1 punch, increment enrolled.
//  4. If enrolled >= capacity: WAITLISTED, NO punch deduction, add to waitlist.
//  5. If requiresEquipment and equipmentAvailable > 0: reserve (decrement), set equipmentReserved.
//     If no equipment but booked, still BOOKED but equipmentReserved = false.
//  6. position = 0 for BOOKED, waitlist.size() for WAITLISTED (1-indexed after push).
//  7. Add classId to member's bookedClasses (even if waitlisted).
//  8. Log "BOOK:<mid>:<cid>".
static BookResult bookClass(const std::string& memberId,
                            const std::string& classId) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    if (!members.count(memberId)) return {"NO_PUNCHES", 0, 0, false};
    auto& mem = members[memberId];
    if (mem.bookedClasses.count(classId))
        return {"DUPLICATE", 0, mem.punchCard, false};
    if (mem.punchCard <= 0) return {"NO_PUNCHES", 0, 0, false};
    auto& cls = classes[classId];
    bool equip = false;
    if (cls.enrolled < cls.capacity) {
        mem.punchCard--;
        cls.enrolled++;
        if (cls.requiresEquipment && cls.equipmentAvailable > 0) {
            cls.equipmentAvailable--;
            equip = true;
        }
        mem.bookedClasses.insert(classId);
        bookingLog.push_back("BOOK:" + memberId + ":" + classId);
        return {"BOOKED", 0, mem.punchCard, equip};
    } else {
        cls.waitlist.push_back(memberId);
        mem.bookedClasses.insert(classId);
        bookingLog.push_back("BOOK:" + memberId + ":" + classId);
        return {"WAITLISTED", (int)cls.waitlist.size(), mem.punchCard, false};
    }


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
}

int main() {
    reset();
    addClass("YOGA", 2, true, 1);
    addMember("M1", 5);
    addMember("M2", 5);
    addMember("M3", 5);

    auto r1 = bookClass("M1", "YOGA");
    assert(r1.status == "BOOKED");
    assert(r1.equipmentReserved == true);  // first gets equipment
    assert(r1.punchesRemaining == 4);

    auto r2 = bookClass("M2", "YOGA");
    assert(r2.status == "BOOKED");
    assert(r2.equipmentReserved == false);  // equipment gone

    auto r3 = bookClass("M3", "YOGA");
    assert(r3.status == "WAITLISTED");
    assert(r3.position == 1);
    assert(r3.punchesRemaining == 5);  // no deduction on waitlist

    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // duplicate
    reset();
    addClass("SPIN", 10, false, 0);
    addMember("M4", 3);
    bookClass("M4", "SPIN");
    auto d = bookClass("M4", "SPIN");
    assert(d.status == "DUPLICATE");
    assert(d.punchesRemaining == 2); // only 1 deducted from first booking

    // no punches
    reset();
    addClass("SPIN", 10, false, 0);
    addMember("M5", 0);
    auto np = bookClass("M5", "SPIN");
    assert(np.status == "NO_PUNCHES");

    // waitlist doesn't deduct punch
    reset();
    addClass("BOX", 1, false, 0);
    addMember("M6", 2);
    addMember("M7", 2);
    bookClass("M6", "BOX");
    auto wl = bookClass("M7", "BOX");
    assert(wl.status == "WAITLISTED");
    assert(wl.punchesRemaining == 2);  // unchanged

    // multiple waitlist positions
    reset();
    addClass("PIK", 1, false, 0);
    addMember("M8", 3);
    addMember("M9", 3);
    addMember("M10", 3);
    bookClass("M8", "PIK");
    bookClass("M9", "PIK");
    auto w2 = bookClass("M10", "PIK");
    assert(w2.position == 2);

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>

struct DentalResult {
    std::string status;  // PAID, DENIED, DUPLICATE, WAITING_PERIOD, MAX_REACHED, FREQ_LIMIT
    double planPays;
    double patientPays;
    double deductibleApplied;
    double remainingMax;
};

// Coinsurance rates by category
static std::unordered_map<std::string, double> coinsurance = {
    {"preventive", 1.0},   // plan pays 100%
    {"basic", 0.80},       // plan pays 80%
    {"major", 0.50}        // plan pays 50%
};

// Deductible applies per category (preventive exempt)
static std::unordered_map<std::string, double> catDeductible = {
    {"preventive", 0.0}, {"basic", 50.0}, {"major", 100.0}
};

// Waiting periods (days from enrollment)
static std::unordered_map<std::string, int> waitingPeriod = {
    {"preventive", 0}, {"basic", 180}, {"major", 365}
};

// Frequency limits (max claims per benefit year)
static std::unordered_map<std::string, int> freqLimit = {
    {"preventive", 2}, {"basic", 4}, {"major", 1}
};

static double annualMax = 1500.0;
static double ytdPlanPaid = 0.0;

static std::unordered_map<std::string, double> catDeductibleMet;
static std::unordered_map<std::string, int> catClaimCount;
static std::unordered_set<std::string> processedClaims;
static std::vector<std::string> claimLog;
static int enrollmentDay = 0;

static void reset() {
    ytdPlanPaid = 0; catDeductibleMet.clear(); catClaimCount.clear();
    processedClaims.clear(); claimLog.clear(); enrollmentDay = 0;
}

// Process a dental insurance claim.
// Rules:
//  1. claimId idempotent: duplicate returns {"DUPLICATE",0,0,0,0}.
//  2. Category must exist in coinsurance; otherwise DENIED.
//  3. If claimDay < enrollmentDay + waitingPeriod[category] → WAITING_PERIOD.
//  4. If catClaimCount[category] >= freqLimit[category] → FREQ_LIMIT.
//  5. Deductible: remaining = catDeductible[cat] - catDeductibleMet[cat].
//     deductibleApplied = min(billed, max(remaining, 0)).
//  6. afterDed = billed - deductibleApplied.
//  7. planPortion = afterDed * coinsurance[category].
//  8. If ytdPlanPaid + planPortion > annualMax, clamp planPortion; return MAX_REACHED if clamped to 0.
//  9. patientPays = billed - planPortion (which includes deductible + coinsurance).
// 10. Update catDeductibleMet, ytdPlanPaid, catClaimCount, log, mark processed.
static DentalResult processClaim(const std::string& claimId,
                                 const std::string& category,
                                 double billed,
                                 int claimDay) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    if (processedClaims.count(claimId)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, 0, 0};
    if (!coinsurance.count(category)) return {"DENIED", 0, 0, 0, 0};
    if (claimDay < enrollmentDay + waitingPeriod[category])
        return {"WAITING_PERIOD", 0, 0, 0, annualMax - ytdPlanPaid};
    if (catClaimCount[category] >= freqLimit[category])
        return {"FREQ_LIMIT", 0, 0, 0, annualMax - ytdPlanPaid};
    double remDed = catDeductible[category] - catDeductibleMet[category];
    double dedApplied = std::min(billed, std::max(remDed, 0.0));
    double afterDed = billed - dedApplied;
    double planPortion = afterDed * coinsurance[category];
    double room = annualMax - ytdPlanPaid;
    std::string status = "PAID";
    if (planPortion > room) { planPortion = std::max(room, 0.0); if (planPortion == 0) status = "MAX_REACHED"; }
    double patient = billed - planPortion;
    catDeductibleMet[category] += dedApplied;
    ytdPlanPaid += planPortion;
    catClaimCount[category]++;
    processedClaims.insert(claimId);
    claimLog.push_back("DENTAL:" + claimId + ":" + std::to_string(planPortion));
    return {status, planPortion, patient, dedApplied, annualMax - ytdPlanPaid};


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
}

int main() {
    reset();
    enrollmentDay = 0;

    // preventive — no deductible, 100% coverage, no waiting
    auto r1 = processClaim("D1", "preventive", 200, 30);
    assert(r1.status == "PAID");
    assert(std::abs(r1.deductibleApplied - 0) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r1.planPays - 200) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r1.patientPays - 0) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r1.remainingMax - 1300) < 0.01);

    // basic — 50 deductible, 80% coverage
    auto r2 = processClaim("D2", "basic", 300, 200);
    assert(r2.status == "PAID");
    assert(std::abs(r2.deductibleApplied - 50) < 0.01);
    // afterDed=250, plan=200
    assert(std::abs(r2.planPays - 200) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r2.patientPays - 100) < 0.01);
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // duplicate
    reset();
    enrollmentDay = 0;
    processClaim("D3", "preventive", 100, 10);
    auto d = processClaim("D3", "preventive", 100, 10);
    assert(d.status == "DUPLICATE");

    // waiting period
    reset();
    enrollmentDay = 0;
    auto wp = processClaim("D4", "major", 500, 100);
    assert(wp.status == "WAITING_PERIOD");

    // frequency limit
    reset();
    enrollmentDay = 0;
    processClaim("D5a", "preventive", 100, 10);
    processClaim("D5b", "preventive", 100, 20);
    auto fl = processClaim("D5c", "preventive", 100, 30);
    assert(fl.status == "FREQ_LIMIT");

    // annual max reached
    reset();
    enrollmentDay = 0;
    processClaim("D6a", "preventive", 1400, 10);
    auto mx = processClaim("D6b", "preventive", 200, 20);
    assert(mx.status == "PAID");
    assert(std::abs(mx.planPays - 100) < 0.01); // only 100 room left
    assert(std::abs(mx.remainingMax - 0) < 0.01);

    // major after waiting period passes
    reset();
    enrollmentDay = 0;
    auto mj = processClaim("D7", "major", 1000, 400);
    assert(mj.status == "PAID");
    // ded=100, afterDed=900, plan=450
    assert(std::abs(mj.deductibleApplied - 100) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(mj.planPays - 450) < 0.01);

PREFIX CODE:

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>

struct WashResult {
    std::string status;  // WASHED, OVER_LIMIT, DUPLICATE, WRONG_LOCATION, UPGRADE_APPLIED
    int washesUsed;
    int washesRemaining;
    int rolloverCredits;
    double addOnCharge;
};

struct Subscription {
    std::string tier;      // "basic"(8/mo), "premium"(unlimited)
    int monthlyLimit;      // 8 for basic, 999 for premium
    int washesThisMonth;
    int rollover;          // unused from last month, max 3
    std::string homeLocation;
    double referralBalance;
};

static std::unordered_map<std::string, Subscription> subs;
static std::unordered_set<std::string> processedWashes;
static std::vector<std::string> washLog;
static double addOnPrice = 5.0;
static int maxRollover = 3;

static void reset() { subs.clear(); processedWashes.clear(); washLog.clear(); }

static void addSub(const std::string& sid, const std::string& tier, int limit,
                   int washes, int rollover, const std::string& loc, double refBal) {
    subs[sid] = {tier, limit, washes, rollover, loc, refBal};
}

// Use a car wash.
// Rules:
//  1. washId idempotent: duplicate returns {"DUPLICATE",0,0,0,0}.
//  2. Sub must exist. If location != homeLocation, return WRONG_LOCATION.
//  3. Total available = monthlyLimit - washesThisMonth + rollover.
//  4. If available <= 0, return OVER_LIMIT.
//  5. Consume from rollover first, then monthlyLimit quota.
//  6. addOnCharge = addOnPrice * addOnCount. If referralBalance covers it, deduct from balance.
//     Otherwise addOnCharge = addOnPrice * addOnCount - referralBalance, set referralBalance=0.
//  7. Increment washesThisMonth, log "WASH:<sid>:<location>".
static WashResult useWash(const std::string& washId,
                          const std::string& subId,
                          const std::string& location,
                          int addOnCount) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    if (processedWashes.count(washId)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, 0, 0};
    if (!subs.count(subId)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, 0, 0};
    auto& s = subs[subId];
    if (location != s.homeLocation) return {"WRONG_LOCATION", 0, 0, 0, 0};
    int available = s.monthlyLimit - s.washesThisMonth + s.rollover;
    if (available <= 0) return {"OVER_LIMIT", 0, 0, 0, 0};
    if (s.rollover > 0) { s.rollover--; }
    else { s.washesThisMonth++; }
    double addCharge = addOnPrice * addOnCount;
    if (s.referralBalance >= addCharge) {
        s.referralBalance -= addCharge;
        addCharge = 0;
    } else {
        addCharge -= s.referralBalance;
        s.referralBalance = 0;
    }
    processedWashes.insert(washId);
    washLog.push_back("WASH:" + subId + ":" + location);
    int remaining = s.monthlyLimit - s.washesThisMonth + s.rollover;
    return {"WASHED", s.washesThisMonth, remaining, s.rollover, addCharge};


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
}

int main() {
    reset();
    addSub("S1", "basic", 8, 6, 2, "LOC_A", 10.0);

    // first wash — uses rollover credit
    auto r1 = useWash("W1", "S1", "LOC_A", 1);
    assert(r1.status == "WASHED");
    assert(r1.rolloverCredits == 1);  // was 2, used 1
    assert(r1.washesRemaining == 3);  // 8-6+1 = 3
    assert(std::abs(r1.addOnCharge - 0) < 0.01);  // covered by $10 referral

    // second wash — uses another rollover
    auto r2 = useWash("W2", "S1", "LOC_A", 2);
    assert(r2.rolloverCredits == 0);  // rollover exhausted
    // addOn = $10, referral was 10-5=5 remaining, now 10-5=5 charge
    assert(std::abs(r2.addOnCharge - 5.0) < 0.01);

    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // duplicate
    reset();
    addSub("S2", "basic", 8, 0, 0, "X", 0);
    useWash("W3", "S2", "X", 0);
    auto d = useWash("W3", "S2", "X", 0);
    assert(d.status == "DUPLICATE");

    // wrong location
    reset();
    addSub("S3", "basic", 8, 0, 0, "LOC_A", 0);
    auto wl = useWash("W4", "S3", "LOC_B", 0);
    assert(wl.status == "WRONG_LOCATION");

    // over limit
    reset();
    addSub("S4", "basic", 2, 2, 0, "X", 0);
    auto ol = useWash("W5", "S4", "X", 0);
    assert(ol.status == "OVER_LIMIT");

    // rollover consumed before monthly
    reset();
    addSub("S5", "basic", 8, 0, 1, "X", 0);
    auto ro = useWash("W6", "S5", "X", 0);
    assert(ro.rolloverCredits == 0);
    assert(subs["S5"].washesThisMonth == 0);  // didn't increment monthly

    // no referral balance means full charge
    reset();
    addSub("S6", "basic", 8, 0, 0, "X", 0);
    auto fc = useWash("W7", "S6", "X", 3);
    assert(std::abs(fc.addOnCharge - 15) < 0.01);

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>

struct RoyaltyResult {
    std::string status;  // DISTRIBUTED, BELOW_THRESHOLD, DUPLICATE
    double artistShare;
    double labelShare;
    double mechanicalFee;
    double performanceFee;
    double totalPayout;
    double convertedPayout;  // in artist's currency
};

static double revenuePerPlay = 0.004;
static double artistSplitRate = 0.55;   // artist gets 55%
static double labelSplitRate = 0.30;    // label gets 30%
static double mechanicalRate = 0.091;   // mechanical license 9.1% of revenue
static double performanceRate = 0.06;   // performance rights 6%
static double minPayout = 50.0;         // USD minimum

static std::unordered_map<std::string, double> exchangeRates = {
    {"USD", 1.0}, {"EUR", 0.93}, {"GBP", 0.79}, {"JPY", 149.5}
};

static std::unordered_set<std::string> processedPeriods;
static std::vector<std::string> payoutLog;
static std::unordered_map<std::string, double> artistAccrued;

static void reset() {
    processedPeriods.clear(); payoutLog.clear(); artistAccrued.clear();
}

// Distribute royalties for a play period.
// Rules:
//  1. periodId idempotent: duplicate returns {"DUPLICATE",0,0,0,0,0,0}.
//  2. revenue = playCount * revenuePerPlay.
//  3. mechanicalFee = revenue * mechanicalRate.
//  4. performanceFee = revenue * performanceRate.
//  5. distributable = revenue - mechanicalFee - performanceFee.
//  6. artistShare = distributable * artistSplitRate.
//  7. labelShare = distributable * labelSplitRate.
//  8. totalPayout = artistShare (label and platform shares go elsewhere).
//  9. Accrue to artistAccrued[artistId]. If accrued < minPayout, return BELOW_THRESHOLD
//     (still accrue, but don't pay out).
// 10. convertedPayout = totalPayout * exchangeRates[artistCurrency].
// 11. If paying out (above threshold), reset accrued to 0. Log "ROYALTY:<artistId>:<totalPayout>".
static RoyaltyResult distribute(const std::string& periodId,
                                const std::string& artistId,
                                int playCount,
                                const std::string& artistCurrency) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    if (processedPeriods.count(periodId)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
    double revenue = playCount * revenuePerPlay;
    double mech = revenue * mechanicalRate;
    double perf = revenue * performanceRate;
    double dist = revenue - mech - perf;
    double artist = dist * artistSplitRate;
    double label = dist * labelSplitRate;
    artistAccrued[artistId] += artist;
    processedPeriods.insert(periodId);
    if (artistAccrued[artistId] < minPayout) {
        double conv = artist * exchangeRates[artistCurrency];
        return {"BELOW_THRESHOLD", artist, label, mech, perf, artist, conv};
    }
    double payout = artistAccrued[artistId];
    double conv = payout * exchangeRates[artistCurrency];
    artistAccrued[artistId] = 0;
    payoutLog.push_back("ROYALTY:" + artistId + ":" + std::to_string(payout));
    return {"DISTRIBUTED", artist, label, mech, perf, payout, conv};


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
}

int main() {
    reset();
    // 50000 plays: revenue = 200
    auto r = distribute("P1", "ART1", 50000, "EUR");
    assert(r.status == "DISTRIBUTED");
    // mech=200*0.091=18.2, perf=200*0.06=12, dist=169.8
    assert(std::abs(r.mechanicalFee - 18.2) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r.performanceFee - 12) < 0.01);
    // artist=169.8*0.55=93.39, label=169.8*0.30=50.94
    assert(std::abs(r.artistShare - 93.39) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r.labelShare - 50.94) < 0.01);
    // payout = 93.39 (first period, above $50)
    assert(std::abs(r.totalPayout - 93.39) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r.convertedPayout - 93.39*0.93) < 0.01);
    assert(payoutLog.size() == 1);
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // below threshold accrues
    reset();
    auto r1 = distribute("P2", "ART2", 10000, "USD");
    // revenue=40, mech=3.64, perf=2.4, dist=33.96, artist=18.678
    assert(r1.status == "BELOW_THRESHOLD");
    assert(std::abs(artistAccrued["ART2"] - 18.678) < 0.01);

    // second period pushes over threshold
    auto r2 = distribute("P3", "ART2", 20000, "USD");
    // new artist share = 37.356, accrued = 18.678+37.356 = 56.034
    assert(r2.status == "DISTRIBUTED");
    assert(std::abs(r2.totalPayout - 56.034) < 0.01); // pays full accrued
    assert(std::abs(artistAccrued["ART2"] - 0) < 0.01); // reset

    // duplicate
    reset();
    distribute("P4", "ART3", 10000, "USD");
    auto d = distribute("P4", "ART3", 10000, "USD");
    assert(d.status == "DUPLICATE");

    // currency conversion
    reset();
    auto r3 = distribute("P5", "ART4", 50000, "JPY");
    // revenue=200, mech=18.2, perf=12, dist=169.8, artist=93.39
    assert(r3.status == "DISTRIBUTED");
    assert(std::abs(r3.convertedPayout - 93.39 * 149.5) < 1.0);

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>

struct DonationResult {
    std::string status;  // PROCESSED, DUPLICATE, INVALID
    double receiptAmount;
    double matchAmount;
    double annualTotal;
    bool taxDeductible;
    int nextPledgeDay;
};

static std::unordered_set<std::string> processedDonations;
static std::unordered_map<std::string, double> donorAnnualTotal;
static std::vector<std::string> ackLog;
static double matchRate = 0.50;       // employer matches 50%
static double matchCap = 1000.0;      // max match per year
static std::unordered_map<std::string, double> matchUsed;  // per donor
static int pledgeInterval = 30;

static void reset() {
    processedDonations.clear(); donorAnnualTotal.clear();
    ackLog.clear(); matchUsed.clear();
}

// Process a donation.
// Rules:
//  1. donationId idempotent: duplicate returns {"DUPLICATE",0,0,0,false,0}.
//  2. amount must be > 0; otherwise INVALID.
//  3. If isRestricted, tax deductibility = false. Otherwise true.
//  4. matchAmount: if employerMatch is true, match = amount * matchRate,
//     capped at matchCap - matchUsed[donorId]. Match cannot go below 0.
//  5. receiptAmount = amount + matchAmount.
//  6. annualTotal: donorAnnualTotal[donorId] += receiptAmount.
//  7. nextPledgeDay = donationDay + pledgeInterval.
//  8. Log "DONATION:<donorId>:<receiptAmount>", mark processed, update matchUsed.
static DonationResult processDonation(const std::string& donationId,
                                      const std::string& donorId,
                                      double amount,
                                      bool isRestricted,
                                      bool employerMatch,
                                      int donationDay) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    if (processedDonations.count(donationId)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, 0, false, 0};
    if (amount <= 0) return {"INVALID", 0, 0, 0, false, 0};
    bool taxDed = !isRestricted;
    double match = 0;
    if (employerMatch) {
        double room = matchCap - matchUsed[donorId];
        match = std::min(amount * matchRate, std::max(room, 0.0));
    }
    double receipt = amount + match;
    donorAnnualTotal[donorId] += receipt;
    matchUsed[donorId] += match;
    processedDonations.insert(donationId);
    ackLog.push_back("DONATION:" + donorId + ":" + std::to_string(receipt));
    return {"PROCESSED", receipt, match, donorAnnualTotal[donorId], taxDed, donationDay + pledgeInterval};


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
}

int main() {
    reset();
    auto r = processDonation("D1", "DONOR1", 500, false, true, 100);
    assert(r.status == "PROCESSED");
    assert(std::abs(r.matchAmount - 250) < 0.01);   // 500*0.5
    assert(std::abs(r.receiptAmount - 750) < 0.01);
    assert(r.taxDeductible == true);
    assert(r.nextPledgeDay == 130);
    assert(std::abs(r.annualTotal - 750) < 0.01);

    // second donation — match cap partially used
    auto r2 = processDonation("D2", "DONOR1", 2000, false, true, 140);
    // match would be 1000, but cap=1000-250=750
    assert(std::abs(r2.matchAmount - 750) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r2.receiptAmount - 2750) < 0.01);
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // duplicate
    reset();
    processDonation("D3", "X", 100, false, false, 10);
    auto d = processDonation("D3", "X", 100, false, false, 10);
    assert(d.status == "DUPLICATE");

    // invalid amount
    reset();
    auto inv = processDonation("D4", "X", 0, false, false, 10);
    assert(inv.status == "INVALID");

    // restricted = not tax deductible
    reset();
    auto restr = processDonation("D5", "X", 200, true, false, 10);
    assert(restr.taxDeductible == false);

    // no employer match
    reset();
    auto nm = processDonation("D6", "X", 500, false, false, 10);
    assert(std::abs(nm.matchAmount - 0) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(nm.receiptAmount - 500) < 0.01);

    // match cap exhausted
    reset();
    processDonation("D7a", "Y", 2000, false, true, 10); // uses full 1000 match
    auto ex = processDonation("D7b", "Y", 500, false, true, 50);
    assert(std::abs(ex.matchAmount - 0) < 0.01);  // cap exhausted

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>

struct ServiceResult {
    std::string status;  // SCHEDULED, DUPLICATE, UNSAFE, WAITPERIOD
    double totalCost;
    double chemicalQty;  // liters
    int safetyWaitDays;
    int followupDay;
    int warrantyEndDay;
};

static std::unordered_set<std::string> processedJobs;
static std::vector<std::string> serviceLog;

struct PestProfile {
    double baseRate;        // per 1000 sqft
    double chemPerSqft;     // liters per sqft
    int safetyDays;
    int warrantyMonths;
    int followupWeeks;
};

static std::unordered_map<std::string, PestProfile> pestDb = {
    {"termite",   {150.0, 0.005, 3, 12, 4}},
    {"rodent",    {100.0, 0.002, 1, 6,  2}},
    {"cockroach", {80.0,  0.003, 2, 3,  3}},
    {"ant",       {60.0,  0.001, 0, 1,  2}}
};

static std::unordered_map<std::string, int> lastServiceDay;  // property -> last service day

static void reset() {
    processedJobs.clear(); serviceLog.clear(); lastServiceDay.clear();
}

// Schedule a pest control service.
// Rules:
//  1. jobId idempotent: duplicate returns {"DUPLICATE",0,0,0,0,0}.
//  2. pestType must exist in pestDb; otherwise return {"DUPLICATE",0,0,0,0,0} (unknown).
//  3. If lastServiceDay[propertyId] exists and serviceDay - lastServiceDay < safetyDays
//     of the PREVIOUS pest type... simplified: if serviceDay - lastServiceDay[propertyId] < 7,
//     return WAITPERIOD (minimum 7-day gap between any services on same property).
//  4. chemicalQty = propertySqft * chemPerSqft.
//  5. If hasPets, chemicalQty *= 0.5 (reduced dosage) and safetyDays += 2.
//  6. totalCost = (propertySqft / 1000.0) * baseRate.
//  7. followupDay = serviceDay + followupWeeks * 7.
//  8. warrantyEndDay = serviceDay + warrantyMonths * 30.
//  9. Update lastServiceDay, log "SERVICE:<jobId>:<pestType>", mark processed.
static ServiceResult schedule(const std::string& jobId,
                              const std::string& propertyId,
                              const std::string& pestType,
                              int propertySqft,
                              bool hasPets,
                              int serviceDay) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    if (processedJobs.count(jobId)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
    if (!pestDb.count(pestType)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
    if (lastServiceDay.count(propertyId) && serviceDay - lastServiceDay[propertyId] < 7)
        return {"WAITPERIOD", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
    auto prof = pestDb[pestType];
    double chem = propertySqft * prof.chemPerSqft;
    int safety = prof.safetyDays;
    if (hasPets) { chem *= 0.5; safety += 2; }
    double cost = (propertySqft / 1000.0) * prof.baseRate;
    int followup = serviceDay + prof.followupWeeks * 7;
    int warranty = serviceDay + prof.warrantyMonths * 30;
    lastServiceDay[propertyId] = serviceDay;
    processedJobs.insert(jobId);
    serviceLog.push_back("SERVICE:" + jobId + ":" + pestType);
    return {"SCHEDULED", cost, chem, safety, followup, warranty};


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
}

int main() {
    reset();
    auto r = schedule("J1", "PROP1", "termite", 2000, false, 100);
    assert(r.status == "SCHEDULED");
    // cost = 2000/1000 * 150 = 300
    assert(std::abs(r.totalCost - 300) < 0.01);
    // chem = 2000 * 0.005 = 10
    assert(std::abs(r.chemicalQty - 10) < 0.01);
    assert(r.safetyWaitDays == 3);
    assert(r.followupDay == 128);   // 100 + 4*7
    assert(r.warrantyEndDay == 460); // 100 + 12*30

    // with pets — reduced dosage
    auto r2 = schedule("J2", "PROP2", "cockroach", 1500, true, 100);
    assert(std::abs(r2.chemicalQty - 1500*0.003*0.5) < 0.01);  // 2.25
    assert(r2.safetyWaitDays == 4);  // 2 + 2
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // duplicate
    reset();
    schedule("J3", "P", "ant", 1000, false, 50);
    auto d = schedule("J3", "P", "ant", 1000, false, 50);
    assert(d.status == "DUPLICATE");

    // wait period between services on same property
    reset();
    schedule("J4", "PX", "ant", 1000, false, 10);
    auto wp = schedule("J5", "PX", "rodent", 1000, false, 15);
    assert(wp.status == "WAITPERIOD");  // only 5 days, need 7

    // exact 7 day gap is fine
    reset();
    schedule("J6", "PY", "ant", 1000, false, 10);
    auto ok = schedule("J7", "PY", "rodent", 1000, false, 17);
    assert(ok.status == "SCHEDULED");

    // unknown pest type
    reset();
    auto unk = schedule("J8", "P", "spider", 1000, false, 10);
    assert(unk.status == "DUPLICATE");

    // ant has 0 safety days, but with pets becomes 2
    reset();
    auto ant = schedule("J9", "P", "ant", 1000, true, 10);
    assert(ant.safetyWaitDays == 2);  // 0 + 2

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>

struct OrderResult {
    std::string status;  // PLACED, DUPLICATE, OUT_OF_SEASON, WRONG_ZONE, LOW_STOCK
    double subtotal;
    double discount;
    double handlingFee;
    double total;
    int deliveryDay;
};

struct Plant {
    double price;
    int stockQty;
    int minZone;
    int maxZone;
    bool coldSensitive;
    int seasonStart;  // month 1-12
    int seasonEnd;    // month 1-12
};

static std::unordered_map<std::string, Plant> catalog;
static std::unordered_set<std::string> processedOrders;
static std::vector<std::string> orderLog;
static int qtyDiscountThreshold = 5;
static double qtyDiscountRate = 0.10;
static double coldHandlingFee = 15.0;
static int deliveryDays = 5;
static int coldDeliveryDays = 3;  // cold-sensitive ships faster

static void reset() { catalog.clear(); processedOrders.clear(); orderLog.clear(); }

static void addPlant(const std::string& name, double price, int stock,
                     int minZ, int maxZ, bool cold, int sStart, int sEnd) {
    catalog[name] = {price, stock, minZ, maxZ, cold, sStart, sEnd};
}

// Place a nursery order.
// Rules:
//  1. orderId idempotent: duplicate returns {"DUPLICATE",0,0,0,0,0}.
//  2. Plant must exist in catalog.
//  3. If orderMonth < seasonStart OR orderMonth > seasonEnd → OUT_OF_SEASON.
//  4. If customerZone < minZone OR customerZone > maxZone → WRONG_ZONE.
//  5. If qty > stockQty → LOW_STOCK (no partial fills).
//  6. subtotal = price * qty.
//  7. discount: if qty >= qtyDiscountThreshold, discount = subtotal * qtyDiscountRate.
//  8. handlingFee: if coldSensitive, handlingFee = coldHandlingFee * qty.
//  9. total = subtotal - discount + handlingFee.
// 10. deliveryDay = orderDay + (coldSensitive ? coldDeliveryDays : deliveryDays).
// 11. Deduct stockQty, log "ORDER:<orderId>:<total>", mark processed.
static OrderResult placeOrder(const std::string& orderId,
                              const std::string& plantName,
                              int qty,
                              int customerZone,
                              int orderMonth,
                              int orderDay) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    if (processedOrders.count(orderId)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
    if (!catalog.count(plantName)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
    auto& p = catalog[plantName];
    if (orderMonth < p.seasonStart || orderMonth > p.seasonEnd)
        return {"OUT_OF_SEASON", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
    if (customerZone < p.minZone || customerZone > p.maxZone)
        return {"WRONG_ZONE", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
    if (qty > p.stockQty) return {"LOW_STOCK", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
    double sub = p.price * qty;
    double disc = 0;
    if (qty >= qtyDiscountThreshold) disc = sub * qtyDiscountRate;
    double handling = p.coldSensitive ? coldHandlingFee * qty : 0;
    double total = sub - disc + handling;
    int delDay = orderDay + (p.coldSensitive ? coldDeliveryDays : deliveryDays);
    p.stockQty -= qty;
    processedOrders.insert(orderId);
    orderLog.push_back("ORDER:" + orderId + ":" + std::to_string(total));
    return {"PLACED", sub, disc, handling, total, delDay};


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
}

int main() {
    reset();
    addPlant("rose", 12.0, 20, 5, 9, false, 3, 10);
    addPlant("orchid", 25.0, 10, 8, 11, true, 4, 9);

    auto r = placeOrder("O1", "rose", 6, 7, 5, 100);
    assert(r.status == "PLACED");
    // sub=72, disc=72*0.10=7.2, handling=0, total=64.8
    assert(std::abs(r.subtotal - 72) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r.discount - 7.2) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r.total - 64.8) < 0.01);
    assert(r.deliveryDay == 105);
    assert(catalog["rose"].stockQty == 14);

    // cold-sensitive plant
    auto r2 = placeOrder("O2", "orchid", 2, 9, 6, 200);
    assert(r2.status == "PLACED");
    assert(std::abs(r2.handlingFee - 30) < 0.01);  // 15 * 2
    assert(r2.deliveryDay == 203);  // faster delivery
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // duplicate
    reset();
    addPlant("daisy", 5, 50, 3, 10, false, 1, 12);
    placeOrder("O3", "daisy", 1, 5, 6, 10);
    auto d = placeOrder("O3", "daisy", 1, 5, 6, 10);
    assert(d.status == "DUPLICATE");

    // out of season
    reset();
    addPlant("tulip", 8, 30, 4, 8, false, 3, 5);
    auto os = placeOrder("O4", "tulip", 1, 5, 7, 10);
    assert(os.status == "OUT_OF_SEASON");

    // wrong zone
    reset();
    addPlant("cactus", 15, 20, 8, 11, false, 1, 12);
    auto wz = placeOrder("O5", "cactus", 1, 5, 6, 10);
    assert(wz.status == "WRONG_ZONE");

    // low stock
    reset();
    addPlant("fern", 10, 3, 3, 10, false, 1, 12);
    auto ls = placeOrder("O6", "fern", 5, 5, 6, 10);
    assert(ls.status == "LOW_STOCK");
    assert(catalog["fern"].stockQty == 3);  // no deduction

    // no discount below threshold
    reset();
    addPlant("mint", 3, 50, 3, 10, false, 1, 12);
    auto nd = placeOrder("O7", "mint", 4, 5, 6, 10);
    assert(std::abs(nd.discount - 0) < 0.01);

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>

struct BoardResult {
    std::string status;  // RESERVED, DUPLICATE, VACC_EXPIRED, NO_KENNEL, SURGE
    double dailyRate;
    double totalCost;
    double surchargeAmount;
    int kennelAssigned;
};

struct Kennel {
    int size;       // 1=small, 2=medium, 3=large
    bool occupied;
};

static std::unordered_map<int, Kennel> kennels;
static std::unordered_set<std::string> processedReservations;
static std::vector<std::string> boardingLog;

static std::unordered_map<int, double> sizeRates = {
    {1, 35.0}, {2, 50.0}, {3, 70.0}
};
static double medSurcharge = 10.0;    // per day if pet has medications
static double surgeRate = 1.5;        // holiday surge multiplier
static std::unordered_set<int> surgeDays;  // days that are holidays

static void reset() {
    kennels.clear(); processedReservations.clear();
    boardingLog.clear(); surgeDays.clear();
}

static void addKennel(int id, int size) { kennels[id] = {size, false}; }
static void addSurgDay(int day) { surgeDays.insert(day); }

// Reserve a pet boarding stay.
// Rules:
//  1. reservationId idempotent: duplicate returns {"DUPLICATE",0,0,0,0}.
//  2. If vaccExpiryDay < checkInDay, return VACC_EXPIRED.
//  3. Find smallest available kennel that fits petSize (kennel.size >= petSize).
//     Prefer lower kennel ID among same-size ties. If none, NO_KENNEL.
//  4. dailyRate = sizeRates[kennel.size] (NOT petSize — charged for kennel size).
//  5. If hasMedications, dailyRate += medSurcharge.
//  6. Count surgeDays in [checkInDay, checkOutDay): surgeCount.
//  7. surchargeAmount = dailyRate * (surgeRate - 1.0) * surgeCount.
//  8. nights = checkOutDay - checkInDay.
//  9. totalCost = dailyRate * nights + surchargeAmount.
// 10. Mark kennel occupied, log "BOARD:<resId>:<kennelId>", mark processed.
static BoardResult reserve(const std::string& resId,
                           int petSize,
                           int vaccExpiryDay,
                           bool hasMedications,
                           int checkInDay,
                           int checkOutDay) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    if (processedReservations.count(resId)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, 0, 0};
    if (vaccExpiryDay < checkInDay) return {"VACC_EXPIRED", 0, 0, 0, 0};
    int bestKennel = -1, bestSize = 999;
    for (auto& [kid, k] : kennels) {
        if (!k.occupied && k.size >= petSize) {
            if (k.size < bestSize || (k.size == bestSize && kid < bestKennel)) {
                bestKennel = kid; bestSize = k.size;
            }
        }
    }
    if (bestKennel < 0) return {"NO_KENNEL", 0, 0, 0, 0};
    double rate = sizeRates[bestSize];
    if (hasMedications) rate += medSurcharge;
    int surgeCount = 0;
    for (int d = checkInDay; d < checkOutDay; d++)
        if (surgeDays.count(d)) surgeCount++;
    double surge = rate * (surgeRate - 1.0) * surgeCount;
    int nights = checkOutDay - checkInDay;
    double total = rate * nights + surge;
    kennels[bestKennel].occupied = true;
    processedReservations.insert(resId);
    boardingLog.push_back("BOARD:" + resId + ":" + std::to_string(bestKennel));
    std::string status = surgeCount > 0 ? "SURGE" : "RESERVED";
    return {status, rate, total, surge, bestKennel};


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
}

int main() {
    reset();
    addKennel(1, 1); addKennel(2, 2); addKennel(3, 3);
    addSurgDay(102); addSurgDay(103);

    // medium pet, check in day 100 for 5 nights
    auto r = reserve("R1", 2, 200, true, 100, 105);
    assert(r.status == "SURGE");
    assert(r.kennelAssigned == 2);   // smallest kennel >= 2
    // rate = 50 + 10(med) = 60, 2 surge days, surge = 60*0.5*2 = 60
    assert(std::abs(r.dailyRate - 60) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r.surchargeAmount - 60) < 0.01);
    // total = 60*5 + 60 = 360
    assert(std::abs(r.totalCost - 360) < 0.01);
    assert(kennels[2].occupied == true);
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // duplicate
    reset();
    addKennel(1, 2);
    reserve("R2", 1, 200, false, 100, 103);
    auto d = reserve("R2", 1, 200, false, 100, 103);
    assert(d.status == "DUPLICATE");

    // expired vaccine
    reset();
    addKennel(1, 2);
    auto ve = reserve("R3", 1, 90, false, 100, 105);
    assert(ve.status == "VACC_EXPIRED");

    // no kennel available
    reset();
    addKennel(1, 1);  // only small
    auto nk = reserve("R4", 2, 200, false, 100, 103);
    assert(nk.status == "NO_KENNEL");

    // charged for kennel size, not pet size
    reset();
    addKennel(10, 3);  // only large available for small pet
    auto big = reserve("R5", 1, 200, false, 100, 102);
    assert(big.status == "RESERVED");
    assert(std::abs(big.dailyRate - 70) < 0.01);  // large kennel rate
    assert(big.kennelAssigned == 10);

    // no surge days → RESERVED status
    reset();
    addKennel(1, 1);
    auto ns = reserve("R6", 1, 200, false, 50, 55);
    assert(ns.status == "RESERVED");
    assert(std::abs(ns.surchargeAmount - 0) < 0.01);

PREFIX CODE:

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>

struct QuoteResult {
    std::string status;  // QUOTED, DUPLICATE, TOO_SMALL, PERMIT_DENIED
    int panelCount;
    double systemCost;
    double taxCredit;
    double netCost;
    double annualSavings;
    double roiYears;
    double permitFee;
};

static double panelWatts = 400;          // watts per panel
static double costPerWatt = 2.80;
static double inverterCostPerKw = 200.0;
static double batteryCostPerKwh = 500.0;
static double taxCreditRate = 0.30;
static double utilityRate = 0.12;        // per kWh
static double buybackRate = 0.04;        // utility pays for excess
static double sunHoursPerDay = 5.0;
static double permitFeeBase = 500.0;
static double permitFeePerPanel = 25.0;
static int minPanels = 4;

static std::unordered_set<std::string> processedQuotes;
static std::vector<std::string> quoteLog;

static void reset() { processedQuotes.clear(); quoteLog.clear(); }

// Generate a solar panel installation quote.
// Rules:
//  1. quoteId idempotent: duplicate returns {"DUPLICATE",0,0,0,0,0,0,0}.
//  2. panelCount = ceil(roofSqft / 18.0) — each panel needs ~18 sqft.
//  3. If panelCount < minPanels, return TOO_SMALL.
//  4. systemWatts = panelCount * panelWatts.
//  5. systemCost = systemWatts * costPerWatt + (systemWatts/1000.0) * inverterCostPerKw.
//  6. If wantBattery: add batteryKwh * batteryCostPerKwh to systemCost.
//  7. permitFee = permitFeeBase + panelCount * permitFeePerPanel.
//  8. taxCredit = (systemCost + permitFee) * taxCreditRate.
//  9. netCost = systemCost + permitFee - taxCredit.
// 10. annualGenKwh = systemWatts/1000 * sunHoursPerDay * 365.
// 11. annualSavings = min(annualGenKwh, annualUsageKwh) * utilityRate
//     + max(annualGenKwh - annualUsageKwh, 0) * buybackRate.
// 12. roiYears = netCost / annualSavings. If annualSavings == 0, roiYears = 999.
// 13. Log "QUOTE:<quoteId>:<netCost>", mark processed.
static QuoteResult generateQuote(const std::string& quoteId,
                                 double roofSqft,
                                 bool wantBattery,
                                 double batteryKwh,
                                 double annualUsageKwh) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    if (processedQuotes.count(quoteId)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
    int panels = (int)std::ceil(roofSqft / 18.0);
    if (panels < minPanels) return {"TOO_SMALL", panels, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
    double sysW = panels * panelWatts;
    double cost = sysW * costPerWatt + (sysW / 1000.0) * inverterCostPerKw;
    if (wantBattery) cost += batteryKwh * batteryCostPerKwh;
    double permit = permitFeeBase + panels * permitFeePerPanel;
    double credit = (cost + permit) * taxCreditRate;
    double net = cost + permit - credit;
    double genKwh = (sysW / 1000.0) * sunHoursPerDay * 365;
    double savings = std::min(genKwh, annualUsageKwh) * utilityRate
                   + std::max(genKwh - annualUsageKwh, 0.0) * buybackRate;
    double roi = (savings == 0) ? 999.0 : net / savings;
    processedQuotes.insert(quoteId);
    quoteLog.push_back("QUOTE:" + quoteId + ":" + std::to_string(net));
    return {"QUOTED", panels, cost, credit, net, savings, roi, permit};


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
}

int main() {
    reset();
    // 500 sqft roof → ceil(500/18) = 28 panels
    auto r = generateQuote("Q1", 500, true, 10, 8000);
    assert(r.status == "QUOTED");
    assert(r.panelCount == 28);
    // sysW = 11200, cost = 11200*2.8 + 11.2*200 = 31360+2240 = 33600
    // battery = 10*500 = 5000, total cost = 38600
    assert(std::abs(r.systemCost - 38600) < 0.01);
    // permit = 500 + 28*25 = 1200
    assert(std::abs(r.permitFee - 1200) < 0.01);
    // credit = (38600+1200)*0.30 = 11940
    assert(std::abs(r.taxCredit - 11940) < 0.01);
    // net = 38600+1200-11940 = 27860
    assert(std::abs(r.netCost - 27860) < 0.01);
    // gen = 11.2*5*365 = 20440 kWh
    // savings = min(20440,8000)*0.12 + max(12440,0)*0.04 = 960+497.6 = 1457.6
    assert(std::abs(r.annualSavings - 1457.6) < 0.01);
    assert(std::abs(r.roiYears - 27860/1457.6) < 0.1);
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // duplicate
    reset();
    generateQuote("Q2", 500, false, 0, 5000);
    auto d = generateQuote("Q2", 500, false, 0, 5000);
    assert(d.status == "DUPLICATE");

    // too small
    reset();
    auto ts = generateQuote("Q3", 50, false, 0, 5000);
    assert(ts.status == "TOO_SMALL");
    assert(ts.panelCount == 3);  // ceil(50/18) = 3 < 4

    // no battery
    reset();
    auto nb = generateQuote("Q4", 200, false, 0, 5000);
    // panels = ceil(200/18) = 12, sysW=4800
    // cost = 4800*2.8 + 4.8*200 = 13440+960 = 14400
    assert(nb.panelCount == 12);
    assert(std::abs(nb.systemCost - 14400) < 0.01);

    // usage exceeds generation — no buyback
    reset();
    auto hi = generateQuote("Q5", 100, false, 0, 50000);
    // panels=6, sysW=2400, gen=2400/1000*5*365=4380
    // savings = 4380*0.12 = 525.6 (all self-consumed)
    assert(std::abs(hi.annualSavings - 525.6) < 0.01);

    // zero usage — all buyback
    reset();
    auto zr = generateQuote("Q6", 200, false, 0, 0);
    // panels=12, gen=4800/1000*5*365=8760
    // savings = 0 + 8760*0.04 = 350.4
    assert(std::abs(zr.annualSavings - 350.4) < 0.01);

Test Case #50

Test Case ID: 50

PREFIX CODE:

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>

struct CaterResult {
    std::string status;  // CONFIRMED, DUPLICATE, BELOW_MINIMUM, INVALID
    double foodCost;
    double equipmentCost;
    double staffCost;
    double travelFee;
    double gratuity;
    double total;
};

static double costPerHead = 35.0;
static double veganSurchargeRate = 0.15;   // 15% more for vegan portions
static double gfSurchargeRate = 0.10;      // 10% more for gluten-free
static double equipmentPerHour = 50.0;
static double staffRate = 25.0;            // per staff per hour
static int staffPer25 = 1;                 // 1 staff per 25 guests
static double travelPerMile = 0.65;
static double gratuityRate = 0.18;
static double minimumOrder = 500.0;

static std::unordered_set<std::string> processedOrders;
static std::vector<std::string> caterLog;

static void reset() { processedOrders.clear(); caterLog.clear(); }

// Place a catering order.
// Rules:
//  1. orderId idempotent: duplicate returns {"DUPLICATE",0,0,0,0,0,0}.
//  2. headcount must be > 0; otherwise INVALID.
//  3. veganCount + gfCount must be <= headcount; otherwise INVALID.
//  4. foodCost: (headcount - veganCount - gfCount) * costPerHead
//     + veganCount * costPerHead * (1 + veganSurchargeRate)
//     + gfCount * costPerHead * (1 + gfSurchargeRate).
//  5. staffNeeded = ceil(headcount / 25.0). staffCost = staffNeeded * staffRate * eventHours.
//  6. equipmentCost = equipmentPerHour * eventHours.
//  7. travelFee = travelPerMile * distanceMiles. Free if distanceMiles <= 10.
//  8. subtotal = foodCost + staffCost + equipmentCost + travelFee.
//  9. If subtotal < minimumOrder, return BELOW_MINIMUM.
// 10. gratuity = subtotal * gratuityRate.
// 11. total = subtotal + gratuity.
// 12. Log "CATER:<orderId>:<total>", mark processed.
static CaterResult placeCatering(const std::string& orderId,
                                 int headcount,
                                 int veganCount,
                                 int gfCount,
                                 double eventHours,
                                 double distanceMiles) {


GOLDEN COMPLETION:
----------------------------------------
    if (processedOrders.count(orderId)) return {"DUPLICATE", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
    if (headcount <= 0) return {"INVALID", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
    if (veganCount + gfCount > headcount) return {"INVALID", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
    int regular = headcount - veganCount - gfCount;
    double food = regular * costPerHead
                + veganCount * costPerHead * (1 + veganSurchargeRate)
                + gfCount * costPerHead * (1 + gfSurchargeRate);
    int staff = (int)std::ceil(headcount / 25.0);
    double staffC = staff * staffRate * eventHours;
    double equipC = equipmentPerHour * eventHours;
    double travel = (distanceMiles > 10) ? travelPerMile * distanceMiles : 0;
    double sub = food + staffC + equipC + travel;
    if (sub < minimumOrder) return {"BELOW_MINIMUM", food, equipC, staffC, travel, 0, sub};
    double grat = sub * gratuityRate;
    double total = sub + grat;
    processedOrders.insert(orderId);
    caterLog.push_back("CATER:" + orderId + ":" + std::to_string(total));
    return {"CONFIRMED", food, equipC, staffC, travel, grat, total};


SUFFIX CODE:
----------------------------------------
}

int main() {
    reset();
    // 50 guests, 10 vegan, 5 GF, 4 hours, 20 miles
    auto r = placeCatering("C1", 50, 10, 5, 4, 20);
    assert(r.status == "CONFIRMED");
    // regular=35, food = 35*35 + 10*35*1.15 + 5*35*1.10 = 1225+402.5+192.5 = 1820
    assert(std::abs(r.foodCost - 1820) < 0.01);
    // staff = ceil(50/25) = 2, staffC = 2*25*4 = 200
    assert(std::abs(r.staffCost - 200) < 0.01);
    // equip = 50*4 = 200
    assert(std::abs(r.equipmentCost - 200) < 0.01);
    // travel = 0.65*20 = 13
    assert(std::abs(r.travelFee - 13) < 0.01);
    // sub = 2233, grat = 2233*0.18 = 401.94, total = 2634.94
    assert(std::abs(r.total - 2634.94) < 0.01);
    return 0;
}


HIDDEN ASSERTIONS:
----------------------------------------

    // duplicate
    reset();
    placeCatering("C2", 30, 0, 0, 3, 5);
    auto d = placeCatering("C2", 30, 0, 0, 3, 5);
    assert(d.status == "DUPLICATE");

    // invalid headcount
    reset();
    auto ih = placeCatering("C3", 0, 0, 0, 3, 5);
    assert(ih.status == "INVALID");

    // dietary count exceeds headcount
    reset();
    auto dc = placeCatering("C4", 10, 6, 6, 3, 5);
    assert(dc.status == "INVALID");

    // below minimum
    reset();
    auto bm = placeCatering("C5", 2, 0, 0, 1, 5);
    assert(bm.status == "BELOW_MINIMUM");
    // food=70, staff=ceil(2/25)=1, staffC=25, equip=50, travel=0 (<=10)
    // sub=145
    assert(std::abs(bm.total - 145) < 0.01); // total field holds subtotal on BELOW_MINIMUM

    // free travel within 10 miles
    reset();
    auto ft = placeCatering("C6", 30, 0, 0, 3, 8);
    assert(std::abs(ft.travelFee - 0) < 0.01);

    // exactly 10 miles — still free
    reset();
    auto t10 = placeCatering("C7", 30, 0, 0, 3, 10);
    assert(std::abs(t10.travelFee - 0) < 0.01);
